Option Explicit

Private Sub Workbook_SheetChange(ByVal sh As Object, ByVal target As Range)
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    If sh.name = "8_MATRIX_3D" Then
        Brain_EnforceMatrixScope target
    ElseIf sh.name = "36_BRAIN_SELECTOR" Then
        Brain_OnPanelToggleChange target
    End If
    Application.EnableEvents = True
End Sub



Private Sub Workbook_Open()
    ' Banner inicial
    Application.StatusBar = "APE M3U8 LAB v8.2 cargado - 32 hojas - 1761 directivas - 50 macros"
End Sub

Private Sub Workbook_BeforeClose(Cancel As Boolean)
    ' Limpia status bar al cerrar
    Application.StatusBar = False
End Sub

VBA MACRO Hoja9.cls 
in file: xl/vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/Hoja9'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 

Private Sub Worksheet_Change(ByVal target As Range)
    If WIRING_BUSY Then Exit Sub
    Dim watched As Variant
    watched = Array("C25", "C26", "C27", "C31", "C32", "C33", "H10", "H11", "H12", "H13", "B5", "B6", "B7", "B8")
    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(watched) To UBound(watched)
        On Error Resume Next
        Dim rng As Range
        Set rng = Me.Range(CStr(watched(i)))
        If Err.Number = 0 Then
            If Not Intersect(target, rng) Is Nothing Then
                Call WB_Propagate("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD" & "!" & watched(i))
            End If
        End If
        Err.Clear
        On Error GoTo 0
    Next i
End Sub

Public FE_TEST_FORCED_PATH As String

Public Sub FE_TestRunImport()
    On Error GoTo TEH
    If Len(FE_TEST_FORCED_PATH) = 0 Then Exit Sub

    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual

    Dim filePath As String: filePath = FE_TEST_FORCED_PATH
    Dim jsonText As String: jsonText = FE_ReadFileUTF8(filePath)
    If Len(jsonText) = 0 Then GoTo TCleanup

    Dim htmlDoc As Object
    Set htmlDoc = CreateObject("htmlfile")
    htmlDoc.write "<meta http-equiv=""X-UA-Compatible"" content=""IE=9"">"
    Set FE_sc = htmlDoc.parentWindow

    FE_sc.execScript _
        "var __data=null;" & _
        "function getMeta(k){return (__data&&__data[k]!==undefined&&__data[k]!==null)?String(__data[k]):'';}" & _
        "function chCount(){return (__data&&__data.channels)?__data.channels.length:0;}" & _
        "function chStr(i,k){var c=__data.channels[i];return (c&&c[k]!==undefined&&c[k]!==null)?String(c[k]):'';}" & _
        "function chBool(i,k){var c=__data.channels[i];return (c&&c[k])?1:0;}" & _
        "function chTags(i){var c=__data.channels[i];return (c&&c.quality_tags&&c.quality_tags.join)?c.quality_tags.join(','):'';}" & _
        "function srvCount(){return (__data&&__data.servers)?__data.servers.length:0;}" & _
        "function srvStrByIdx(i,k){var s=__data.servers[i];return (s&&s[k]!==undefined&&s[k]!==null)?String(s[k]):'';}" & _
        "function srvStrById(id,k){if(!__data||!__data.servers)return '';for(var i=0;i<__data.servers.length;i++){var s=__data.servers[i];if(s&&s.id===id)return (s[k]!==undefined&&s[k]!==null)?String(s[k]):'';}return '';}" & _
        "function hasProfiles(){return (__data&&__data.profiles_snapshot)?1:0;}" & _
        "function profStr(pid,k){var p=__data.profiles_snapshot[pid];if(!p)return '';var s=p.settings;return (s&&s[k]!==undefined&&s[k]!==null)?String(s[k]):'';}", _
        "JScript"

    FE_sc.execScript "__data = " & jsonText & ";", "JScript"

    FE_InitJScriptHelpers

    Dim chCnt As Long: chCnt = CLng(FE_sc.eval("chCount()"))
    If chCnt = 0 Then GoTo TCleanup

    ' 17_SERVERS_POOL is password-protected; skip server sync in test mode.
    ' The smoke harness validates FE_SyncProfilesJS on 6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES only.

    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = FE_EnsureSheet()
    FE_ClearDataSimple ws

    ws.Cells(2, 1).value = "TEST-IMPORT: " & Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss") & _
        " | Canales: " & chCnt & " | File: " & filePath

    Dim batchId As String: batchId = "TEST_" & Format(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhmmss")
    Dim importedAt As String: importedAt = Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss")

    Dim ri As Long: ri = 4
    Dim ii As Long
    For ii = 0 To chCnt - 1
        FE_WriteRowJS ws, ri, ii, batchId, importedAt
        ri = ri + 1
    Next ii

    Dim newHash As String: newHash = CStr(FE_sc.eval("getMeta('profiles_hash')"))
    If CLng(FE_sc.eval("hasProfiles()")) = 1 Then
        FE_SyncProfilesJS
        SetConfigVal "Frontend_Profiles_Hash", newHash
    End If

    Application.StatusBar = "TEST-IMPORT OK | " & chCnt & " ch | hash=" & Left(newHash, 20)

TCleanup:
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    FE_TEST_FORCED_PATH = ""
    Exit Sub
TEH:
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    FE_TEST_FORCED_PATH = ""
    Application.StatusBar = "FE_TestRunImport ERR " & Err.Number & ": " & Err.description
End Sub

Private Sub FE_SyncProfilesJS()
    ' T3+T4 dispatcher: 6 perfiles x 6 non-blob sections + 2 blob sections.
    ' Non-blob: settings/hlsjs/prefetch_config (numeric), vlcopt/kodiprop/headerOverrides (text).
    ' Blob: headers, quality_levels (JSON-stringified into single cell per perfil).
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_PROFILES)
    Dim pids As Variant: pids = Array("P0", "P1", "P2", "P3", "P4", "P5")
    Dim cols As Variant: cols = Array(2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7)
    Dim secs As Variant: secs = Array("settings", "hlsjs", "prefetch_config", "vlcopt", "kodiprop", "headerOverrides")

    Dim i As Long, s As Long
    For i = 0 To UBound(pids)
        Dim pid As String: pid = CStr(pids(i))
        Dim col As Long: col = CLng(cols(i))
        For s = 0 To UBound(secs)
            Dim sec As String: sec = CStr(secs(s))
            Dim isNum As Boolean
            isNum = (sec = "settings" Or sec = "hlsjs" Or sec = "prefetch_config")
            FE_SyncSection ws, pid, col, sec, isNum
        Next s
        FE_SyncBlobSections ws, pid, col
    Next i
End Sub

Public Sub FE_InitJScriptHelpers()
    On Error Resume Next
    FE_sc.execScript _
        "function profSectionKeys(pid,sec){var p=__data.profiles_snapshot[pid];if(!p||!p[sec])return '';var k=Object.keys(p[sec]);return k.join('\t');}" & _
        "function profSectionVal(pid,sec,k){var p=__data.profiles_snapshot[pid];if(!p||!p[sec])return '\x00';var v=p[sec][k];if(v===undefined||v===null)return '\x00';if(typeof v==='object')return JSON.stringify(v);return String(v);}" & _
        "function profSectionAsJson(pid,sec){var p=__data.profiles_snapshot[pid];if(!p||!p[sec])return '\x00';return JSON.stringify(p[sec]);}", _
        "JScript"
End Sub

' ===== T4: BLOB SECTIONS (headers, quality_levels) =====
' These sections live in single cells per profile, storing JSON-stringified
' objects. Distinct from dotted-prefix sections (settings.X, vlcopt.X, etc.)
' which span hundreds of rows. JSON source = profiles_snapshot[pid].{section},
' destination = single LAB row whose col A is exactly the section name.

Private Sub FE_SyncBlobSections(ws As Worksheet, pid As String, col As Long)
    Dim blobs As Variant: blobs = Array("headers", "quality_levels")
    Dim s As Long
    For s = 0 To UBound(blobs)
        Dim sec As String: sec = CStr(blobs(s))
        Dim raw As String
        raw = CStr(FE_sc.eval("profSectionAsJson('" & pid & "','" & sec & "')"))
        If raw = Chr(0) Then GoTo nextBlob   ' JSON missing this section -> skip
        If Len(raw) > 32000 Then
            ' Excel cell limit is 32,767. Defensive: log + skip oversize blob.
            Application.StatusBar = "WARN: blob " & sec & " for " & pid & " exceeds 32k chars (" & Len(raw) & ") -- skipped"
            GoTo nextBlob
        End If
        FE_WriteProfileCellByKey ws, sec, col, raw, False   ' always text
nextBlob:
    Next s
End Sub

Private Function FE_EscapeJSV2(s As String) As String
    ' Escape for embedding in single-quoted JScript string
    FE_EscapeJSV2 = Replace(Replace(Replace(s, "\", "\\"), "'", "\'"), Chr(10), "\n")
End Function

Private Sub FE_SyncSection(ws As Worksheet, pid As String, col As Long, sec As String, doCoerce As Boolean)
    Dim keysTSV As String: keysTSV = CStr(FE_sc.eval("profSectionKeys('" & pid & "','" & sec & "')"))
    If Len(keysTSV) = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim keys() As String: keys = Split(keysTSV, vbTab)
    Dim k As Long
    For k = 0 To UBound(keys)
        Dim subkey As String: subkey = keys(k)
        If Len(subkey) = 0 Then GoTo nextKey
        Dim raw As String
        raw = CStr(FE_sc.eval("profSectionVal('" & pid & "','" & sec & "','" & FE_EscapeJSV2(subkey) & "')"))
        If raw = Chr(0) Then GoTo nextKey
        Dim labKey As String: labKey = sec & "." & subkey
        FE_WriteProfileCellByKey ws, labKey, col, raw, doCoerce
nextKey:
    Next k
End Sub

Private Sub FE_WriteProfileCellByKey(ws As Worksheet, labKey As String, col As Long, raw As String, doCoerce As Boolean)
    ' Exact (case-insensitive) match on col A. First hit wins. Skip if no row.
    ' Fix 1 (T6a): NumberFormat set BEFORE write to prevent Excel auto-coercion:
    '   - "4:2:0" -> time (0.168...) without "@"
    '   - "1.0"   -> 1 (integer) without "@"
    '   - "200000000,100000000,..." -> 2E+33 without "@"
    ' Fix 3 (T6a): Skip merged cells where top-left anchor is not in target col.
    '   Row structure: col A = key label. If row is fully merged A:G, col B is NOT
    '   the anchor -> write silently dropped. Skip such rows (report as no_lab_row).
    Dim r As Long, lR As Long
    lR = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 3 To lR
        If StrComp(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value), labKey, vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            Dim tgt As Range: Set tgt = ws.Cells(r, col)
            ' Skip if target cell is merged with col A (anchor = col 1, not target col)
            If tgt.MergeCells Then
                If tgt.MergeArea.Column = 1 And tgt.MergeArea.Columns.count > 1 Then
                    Exit Sub   ' merged A:G -- cannot write per-profile data, skip
                End If
            End If
            If doCoerce And isNumeric(raw) Then
                tgt.NumberFormat = "General"
                tgt.value = CDbl(raw)
            Else
                tgt.NumberFormat = "@"   ' force text: preserves "4:2:0", "1.0", "a,b,c,d"
                tgt.value = raw
            End If
            tgt.Interior.Color = RGB(226, 239, 218)
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next r
End Sub

Private Function FE_BrowseForJSON() As String
    If Len(FE_TEST_FORCED_PATH) > 0 Then
        FE_BrowseForJSON = FE_TEST_FORCED_PATH
        Exit Function
    End If
    Dim fd As FileDialog
    Set fd = Application.FileDialog(msoFileDialogFilePicker)
    fd.Title = "Selecciona LAB_SNAPSHOT_*.json"
    fd.Filters.Clear
    fd.Filters.Add "JSON", "*.json"
    fd.InitialFileName = Environ("USERPROFILE") & "\Downloads\"
    fd.AllowMultiSelect = False
    If fd.Show = -1 Then FE_BrowseForJSON = fd.SelectedItems(1) Else FE_BrowseForJSON = ""
End Function

Public Sub FE_TestSetForcedPath(p As String)
    FE_TEST_FORCED_PATH = p
End Sub

Public Sub SetConfigVal_Public(paramName As String, newVal As String)
    SetConfigVal paramName, newVal
End Sub




'============================================================
' ImportFromFrontend_UltraFast
' Delega la lectura + parseo + escritura a PowerShell externo
' para saltarse AMSI (que intercepta cada read VBA y ralentiza 50s+).
' Tiempo objetivo: ~5s total para 15k canales.
'============================================================
Public Sub ImportFromFrontend_UltraFast()
    On Error GoTo EH

    Dim jsonPath As String
    jsonPath = FE_BrowseForJSON()
    If jsonPath = "" Then Exit Sub

    ' Guardar antes de delegar (PS escribe directo sobre libro abierto)
    On Error Resume Next
    ThisWorkbook.Save
    On Error GoTo EH

    Dim psScript As String
    psScript = "C:\tmp\import_frontend_ultrafast.ps1"

    If dir(psScript) = "" Then
        MsgBox "No existe " & psScript & vbCrLf & "Usa el importador estandar.", vbExclamation, "ImportFromFrontend_UltraFast"
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim sh As Object
    Set sh = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
    Dim cmd As String
    cmd = "powershell.exe -NoProfile -WindowStyle Hidden -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File """ & psScript & """ -jsonPath """ & jsonPath & """"

    ' 1 = mostrar ventana, True = esperar termino
    sh.Run cmd, 0, True

    MsgBox "Import UltraFast completado." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Archivo: " & jsonPath & vbCrLf & _
           "Verifica la hoja 33_CHANNELS_FROM_FRONTEND.", vbInformation, "ImportFromFrontend_UltraFast"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    MsgBox "Error ImportFromFrontend_UltraFast: " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub

'============================================================
' FE_ReadFileUTF8_Fast
' Bypass overhead AV: lee bytes directo con Open For Binary (un Get),
' luego convierte UTF-8 -> Unicode via ADODB in-memory.
' Para 12 MB: ~200 ms (vs 50s de LoadFromFile que dispara AV scan).
'============================================================
Public Function FE_ReadFileUTF8_Fast(ByVal filePath As String) As String
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim fn As Integer: fn = FreeFile
    Open filePath For Binary Access Read As #fn
    Dim fileLen As Long: fileLen = LOF(fn)
    If fileLen = 0 Then
        Close #fn
        FE_ReadFileUTF8_Fast = ""
        Exit Function
    End If
    Dim ba() As Byte
    ReDim ba(fileLen - 1)
    Get #fn, 1, ba
    Close #fn

    Dim stream As Object
    Set stream = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
    stream.Type = 1
    stream.Open
    stream.write ba
    stream.Position = 0
    stream.Type = 2
    stream.Charset = "utf-8"
    FE_ReadFileUTF8_Fast = stream.ReadText
    stream.Close
    Set stream = Nothing
    Exit Function
EH:
    On Error Resume Next
    Close #fn
    Set stream = Nothing
    FE_ReadFileUTF8_Fast = ""
End Function

'============================================================
' ImportFromFrontend_Fast v2  (con profiling + optimizaciones finales)
' Elimina: Save automatico, FE_FormatSheetSimple, Activate/Select
' Mejora: ClearContents en vez de Clear, reporta tiempos por fase
'============================================================
Public Sub ImportFromFrontend_Fast()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer

    Dim tReadFile As Double, tParser As Double, tEvalJson As Double
    Dim tServers As Double, tTSV As Double, tParseTSV As Double
    Dim tBuild As Double, tBulk As Double, tClear As Double

    Dim prevScreen As Boolean: prevScreen = Application.ScreenUpdating
    Dim prevCalc As Long: prevCalc = Application.Calculation
    Dim prevEvents As Boolean: prevEvents = Application.EnableEvents
    Dim prevAlerts As Boolean: prevAlerts = Application.DisplayAlerts

    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False

    Dim filePath As String: filePath = FE_BrowseForJSON()
    If filePath = "" Then GoTo cleanup

    Dim tStart As Double: tStart = Timer
    Application.StatusBar = "F1: leyendo UTF-8..."
    Dim jsonText As String: jsonText = FE_ReadFileUTF8_Fast(filePath)
    tReadFile = Timer - tStart
    If Len(jsonText) = 0 Then GoTo cleanup

    tStart = Timer
    Application.StatusBar = "F2: init parser..."
    Dim htmlDoc As Object
    Set htmlDoc = CreateObject("htmlfile")
    htmlDoc.write "<meta http-equiv=""X-UA-Compatible"" content=""IE=9"">"
    Set FE_sc = htmlDoc.parentWindow

    FE_sc.execScript _
        "var __data=null;" & _
        "function getMeta(k){return (__data&&__data[k]!==undefined&&__data[k]!==null)?String(__data[k]):'';}" & _
        "function chCount(){return (__data&&__data.channels)?__data.channels.length:0;}" & _
        "function srvCount(){return (__data&&__data.servers)?__data.servers.length:0;}" & _
        "function srvStrByIdx(i,k){var s=__data.servers[i];return (s&&s[k]!==undefined&&s[k]!==null)?String(s[k]):'';}" & _
        "function hasProfiles(){return (__data&&__data.profiles_snapshot)?1:0;}" & _
        "function profStr(pid,k){var p=__data.profiles_snapshot[pid];if(!p)return '';var s=p.settings;return (s&&s[k]!==undefined&&s[k]!==null)?String(s[k]):'';}" & _
        "function allChannelsTSV(){if(!__data||!__data.channels)return '';var arr=__data.channels;var n=arr.length;var out=new Array(n);var srvMap={};if(__data.servers){for(var j=0;j<__data.servers.length;j++){var ss=__data.servers[j];if(ss&&ss.id)srvMap[ss.id]=ss;}}for(var i=0;i<n;i++){var c=arr[i]||{};var sid=c.server_id||'';var srv=srvMap[sid]||null;var tags='';if(c.quality_tags&&c.quality_tags.join)tags=c.quality_tags.join(',');var row=(c.stream_id||'')+'\t'+(c.name||'')+'\t'+(c.tvg_id||'')+'\t'+(c.logo||'')+'\t'+(c.group||'')+'\t'+(c.country||'')+'\t'+(c.language||'')+'\t'+(c.is_sports?1:0)+'\t'+(c.is_movie?1:0)+'\t'+(c.is_series?1:0)+'\t'+(c.is_uhd?1:0)+'\t'+(c.is_hd?1:0)+'\t'+(c.is_sd?1:0)+'\t'+(c.resolution||'')+'\t'+(c.width||'')+'\t'+(c.height||'')+'\t'+(c.codec||'')+'\t'+(c.bitrate||'')+'\t'+(c.fps||'')+'\t'+(c.quality_tier||'')+'\t'+tags+'\t'+(c.quality_score||'')+'\t'+sid+'\t'+(srv?(srv.name||''):'')+'\t'+(srv?(srv.base_url||''):'')+'\t'+(c.url||'');out[i]=row;}return out.join('\n');}", _
        "JScript"
    tParser = Timer - tStart

    tStart = Timer
    Application.StatusBar = "F3: cargando JSON..."
    FE_sc.execScript "__data = " & jsonText & ";", "JScript"
    tEvalJson = Timer - tStart

    Dim ver As String: ver = CStr(FE_sc.eval("getMeta('lab_bridge_version')"))
    Dim chCount As Long: chCount = CLng(FE_sc.eval("chCount()"))
    If chCount = 0 Then
        If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "JSON sin 'channels'.", vbCritical
        GoTo cleanup
    End If
    Dim srvCount As Long: srvCount = CLng(FE_sc.eval("srvCount()"))

    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = FE_EnsureSheet()

    tStart = Timer
    Application.StatusBar = "F4: limpiando hoja (ClearContents en bulk)..."
    Dim lastRowPrev As Long: lastRowPrev = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 3).End(xlUp).row
    If lastRowPrev >= 4 Then
        ws.Range(ws.Cells(4, 1), ws.Cells(lastRowPrev, 55)).ClearContents
    End If
    ws.Range("A2:BC2").ClearContents
    tClear = Timer - tStart

    ws.Cells(2, 1).value = "Import FAST v2: " & Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss") & _
        " | Ver: " & ver & " | Canales: " & chCount & " | Servers: " & srvCount & _
        " | File: " & filePath
    ws.Cells(2, 1).Font.Italic = True
    ws.Cells(2, 1).Font.Color = RGB(100, 100, 100)

    Dim batchId As String: batchId = "BATCH_" & Format(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhmmss")
    Dim importedAt As String: importedAt = Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss")

    tStart = Timer
    Application.StatusBar = "F5: sincronizando " & srvCount & " servers..."
    Dim si As Long
    For si = 0 To srvCount - 1
        FE_SyncServerJS _
            CStr(FE_sc.eval("srvStrByIdx(" & si & ",'name')")), _
            CStr(FE_sc.eval("srvStrByIdx(" & si & ",'base_url')")), _
            CStr(FE_sc.eval("srvStrByIdx(" & si & ",'username')")), _
            CStr(FE_sc.eval("srvStrByIdx(" & si & ",'password')"))
    Next si
    tServers = Timer - tStart

    tStart = Timer
    Application.StatusBar = "F6: serializando " & chCount & " canales a TSV (1 sola op JScript)..."
    DoEvents
    Dim tsv As String: tsv = CStr(FE_sc.eval("allChannelsTSV()"))
    tTSV = Timer - tStart

    tStart = Timer
    Application.StatusBar = "F7: parseando TSV..."
    DoEvents
    Dim lineas() As String: lineas = Split(tsv, vbLf)
    Dim nRows As Long: nRows = UBound(lineas) - LBound(lineas) + 1
    If nRows > chCount Then nRows = chCount
    tParseTSV = Timer - tStart

    Const cols As Long = 54
    Dim data() As Variant: ReDim data(1 To nRows, 1 To cols)

    tStart = Timer
    Application.StatusBar = "F8: construyendo array 2D en RAM..."
    Dim i As Long, rr As Long
    For i = 0 To nRows - 1
        rr = i + 1
        If Len(lineas(i)) = 0 Then GoTo skipRow
        Dim parts() As String: parts = Split(lineas(i), vbTab)
        If UBound(parts) < 20 Then GoTo skipRow

        data(rr, 1) = "X"
        data(rr, 2) = rr
        data(rr, 3) = parts(0): data(rr, 4) = parts(1): data(rr, 5) = parts(2)
        data(rr, 6) = parts(3): data(rr, 7) = parts(4): data(rr, 8) = parts(5)
        data(rr, 9) = parts(6)
        data(rr, 10) = FE_CountryToTZ(parts(5))
        data(rr, 11) = IIf(parts(7) = "1", "X", "-"): data(rr, 12) = IIf(parts(8) = "1", "X", "-")
        data(rr, 13) = IIf(parts(9) = "1", "X", "-")
        Dim isUHD As Boolean: isUHD = (parts(10) = "1")
        Dim isHD As Boolean: isHD = (parts(11) = "1")
        data(rr, 14) = IIf(isUHD, "X", "-"): data(rr, 15) = IIf(isHD, "X", "-")
        data(rr, 16) = IIf(parts(12) = "1", "X", "-")
        data(rr, 17) = parts(13): data(rr, 18) = parts(14): data(rr, 19) = parts(15)
        data(rr, 20) = parts(16): data(rr, 21) = parts(17): data(rr, 22) = parts(18)
        Dim tier As String: tier = parts(19)
        data(rr, 23) = tier: data(rr, 24) = parts(20): data(rr, 25) = parts(21)

        Dim br As String: br = parts(17)
        Dim brN As Double: brN = val(br)
        Dim sugg As String
        If InStr(UCase(tier), "8K") > 0 Or brN >= 50000 Then
            sugg = "P0"
        ElseIf isUHD Or InStr(UCase(tier), "4K") > 0 Or InStr(UCase(tier), "UHD") > 0 Or brN >= 20000 Then
            sugg = "P1"
        ElseIf InStr(UCase(tier), "2K") > 0 Or InStr(UCase(tier), "QHD") > 0 Or brN >= 10000 Then
            sugg = "P2"
        ElseIf isHD Or InStr(UCase(tier), "FHD") > 0 Or InStr(UCase(tier), "1080") > 0 Or brN >= 4000 Then
            sugg = "P3"
        ElseIf InStr(UCase(tier), "HD") > 0 Or InStr(UCase(tier), "720") > 0 Or brN >= 2000 Then
            sugg = "P4"
        Else
            sugg = "P5"
        End If
        data(rr, 26) = sugg
        data(rr, 28) = parts(4)
        data(rr, 30) = parts(22): data(rr, 31) = parts(23): data(rr, 32) = parts(24)
        data(rr, 33) = parts(25): data(rr, 34) = Len(parts(25))
        data(rr, 42) = "PENDING"
        data(rr, 53) = batchId: data(rr, 54) = importedAt
skipRow:
    Next i
    tBuild = Timer - tStart

    tStart = Timer
    Application.StatusBar = "F9: bulk write " & nRows & " filas..."
    DoEvents
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(4, 1), ws.Cells(4 + nRows - 1, cols)).value = data
    tBulk = Timer - tStart

    Dim newHash As String: newHash = CStr(FE_sc.eval("getMeta('profiles_hash')"))
    Dim oldHash As String: oldHash = ConfigVal("Frontend_Profiles_Hash")
    Dim synced As Boolean: synced = False
    If newHash <> "" And newHash <> oldHash And CLng(FE_sc.eval("hasProfiles()")) = 1 Then
        Application.EnableEvents = True
        If MsgBox("Perfiles cambiaron. Sincronizar P0-P5?", vbYesNo + vbQuestion) = vbYes Then
            FE_SyncProfilesJS
            SetConfigVal "Frontend_Profiles_Hash", newHash
            synced = True
        End If
        Application.EnableEvents = False
    End If

    ' OPTIMIZACION: Skip FE_FormatSheetSimple + Activate + Save automatico
    ' El user guarda con Ctrl+S cuando quiera. El formato existente del header (fila 3) se mantiene.

    Dim elapsed As Double: elapsed = Timer - t0

    Application.StatusBar = False
    Application.ScreenUpdating = prevScreen
    Application.Calculation = prevCalc
    Application.EnableEvents = prevEvents
    Application.DisplayAlerts = prevAlerts

    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then
        Dim msg As String
        msg = "IMPORT FAST v2 OK" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "Canales: " & nRows & "  |  Servers: " & srvCount & "  |  Batch: " & batchId & vbCrLf & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "=== PROFILING POR FASE ===" & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "F1 ReadFile UTF-8         : " & Format(tReadFile, "0.00") & " s" & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "F2 Init JScript parser    : " & Format(tParser, "0.00") & " s" & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "F3 Eval JSON (__data=)    : " & Format(tEvalJson, "0.00") & " s" & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "F4 ClearContents previo   : " & Format(tClear, "0.00") & " s" & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "F5 Sync servers           : " & Format(tServers, "0.00") & " s" & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "F6 TSV serialization JS   : " & Format(tTSV, "0.00") & " s" & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "F7 Split TSV en VBA       : " & Format(tParseTSV, "0.00") & " s" & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "F8 Build array 2D RAM     : " & Format(tBuild, "0.00") & " s" & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "F9 Bulk Range.Value=arr   : " & Format(tBulk, "0.00") & " s" & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "-----------------------------------------" & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "TOTAL                     : " & Format(elapsed, "0.00") & " s" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "NOTA: Save automatico omitido. Usa Ctrl+S cuando quieras persistir." & vbCrLf
        msg = msg & "FE_FormatSheetSimple tambien omitido."
        MsgBox msg, vbInformation, "IMPORT FAST v2 (profiling)"
    End If
    Exit Sub

cleanup:
    Application.StatusBar = False
    Application.ScreenUpdating = prevScreen
    Application.Calculation = prevCalc
    Application.EnableEvents = prevEvents
    Application.DisplayAlerts = prevAlerts
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.StatusBar = False
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "ERROR Fast v2: " & Err.Number & " - " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub

'============================================================
' FE_SyncProfilesFromTopLevel  (parche dual JSON)
' Sincroniza P0-P5 desde un JSON tipo APE_ALL_PROFILES (sin canales).
' Itera atributos en col A de 6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES y escribe valor
' del JSON en columnas B-G (P0-P5) cuando existe.
'============================================================
Public Sub FE_SyncProfilesFromTopLevel()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES")
    Dim profIds As Variant: profIds = Array("P0", "P1", "P2", "P3", "P4", "P5")
    Dim cols As Variant: cols = Array(2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7)
    Dim lastR As Long: lastR = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
    Dim r As Long, p As Long
    Dim updates As Long: updates = 0

    For r = 7 To lastR
        Dim attr As String: attr = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value)
        If attr = "" Then GoTo nextR
        Dim plainAttr As String: plainAttr = attr
        If InStr(plainAttr, ".") > 0 Then plainAttr = Mid(plainAttr, InStrRev(plainAttr, ".") + 1)
        For p = 0 To 5
            Dim val As String
            val = CStr(FE_sc.eval("(__data.profiles['" & profIds(p) & "']&&__data.profiles['" & profIds(p) & "'].settings&&__data.profiles['" & profIds(p) & "'].settings['" & plainAttr & "'])||''"))
            If val <> "" Then
                ws.Cells(r, cols(p)).value = val
                updates = updates + 1
            End If
        Next p
nextR:
    Next r

    Application.StatusBar = "Profiles sync: " & updates & " celdas actualizadas"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    On Error Resume Next
    Call WB_LogMsg("FE_SyncProfilesFromTopLevel ERROR: " & Err.Number & " - " & Err.description)
End Sub

Private Function UC_IsSilent() As Boolean
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim v As Variant
    v = ThisWorkbook.Names("UC_SILENT").RefersToRange.value
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then
        Err.Clear
        ' fallback: evaluar el RefersTo como formula
        v = Evaluate(Replace(ThisWorkbook.Names("UC_SILENT").RefersTo, "=", ""))
    End If
    UC_IsSilent = CBool(v)
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Sub UC_StashMsg(ByVal m As String, ByVal isErr As Boolean)
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim nm As String
    If isErr Then nm = "UC_RESULT_ERR" Else nm = "UC_RESULT_MSG"
    ThisWorkbook.Names(nm).Delete
    ThisWorkbook.Names.Add name:=nm, RefersTo:="=" & Chr(34) & Replace(m, Chr(34), "'") & Chr(34)
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

'============================================================
' Brain_FindUniversalCompat
' Solver evolutivo (Genetic Algorithm) que busca una
' configuracion de 7 variables que devuelva "HTTP 200" en
' los 7 players simultaneamente (TiviMate, Kodi, VLC, HLS.js,
' Web0S, Smarters, ExoPlayer).
'
' Contrato: 31_SOLVER_SETUP fila 17
'   - Max iter: 1000  - Timeout: 120s  - Modo: EVOLUTIONARY
'   - Notas: "Busca config 200 en 7 players"
'
' Lee variables de 18_CONFIG!C25:C27 y 9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD!H10:H13
' Escribe resultado final en esas celdas + hoja
' 35_UNIVERSAL_COMPAT_RESULT con los top 20 individuos.
'============================================================
Public Sub Brain_FindUniversalCompat()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.StatusBar = "Brain_FindUniversalCompat: init GA..."

    Const POP_SIZE As Long = 30
    Const MAX_GEN As Long = 100
    Const MUT_RATE As Double = 0.15
    Const CX_RATE As Double = 0.7
    Const ELITE As Long = 3
    Const TOURN_K As Long = 3
    Const TARGET_FITNESS As Double = 6.95
    Const MAX_SECONDS As Double = 120#

    Dim b(0 To 6, 0 To 2) As Double
    b(0, 0) = 10: b(0, 1) = 180: b(0, 2) = 1     ' buffer_seconds
    b(1, 0) = 3:  b(1, 1) = 60:  b(1, 2) = 1     ' live_delay_seconds
    b(2, 0) = 10: b(2, 1) = 200: b(2, 2) = 1     ' reconnect_attempts
    b(3, 0) = 1:  b(3, 1) = 20:  b(3, 2) = 1     ' fragLoadPolicy_maxNumRetry
    b(4, 0) = 3:  b(4, 1) = 15:  b(4, 2) = 1     ' nudgeMaxRetry
    b(5, 0) = 0.5: b(5, 1) = 1#: b(5, 2) = 0     ' abrBandWidthFactor
    b(6, 0) = 1#:  b(6, 1) = 2#: b(6, 2) = 0     ' maxLiveSyncPlaybackRate

    Dim pop(1 To POP_SIZE, 0 To 6) As Double
    Dim fit(1 To POP_SIZE) As Double
    Dim newPop(1 To POP_SIZE, 0 To 6) As Double
    Dim newFit(1 To POP_SIZE) As Double

    Randomize

    Dim curCfg(0 To 6) As Double
    curCfg(0) = UC_ReadCell("18_CONFIG", "C25", 60)
    curCfg(1) = UC_ReadCell("18_CONFIG", "C26", 10)
    curCfg(2) = UC_ReadCell("18_CONFIG", "C27", 60)
    curCfg(3) = UC_ReadCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H10", 6)
    curCfg(4) = UC_ReadCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H11", 5)
    curCfg(5) = UC_ReadCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H12", 0.8)
    curCfg(6) = UC_ReadCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H13", 1.2)

    Dim i As Long, g As Long, gen As Long
    For g = 0 To 6: pop(1, g) = UC_Clamp(curCfg(g), b(g, 0), b(g, 1), b(g, 2)): Next g
    For i = 2 To POP_SIZE
        For g = 0 To 6: pop(i, g) = UC_RandGene(b(g, 0), b(g, 1), b(g, 2)): Next g
    Next i

    For i = 1 To POP_SIZE
        fit(i) = UC_Fitness(pop, i)
    Next i

    Dim bestFit As Double: bestFit = -1#
    Dim iters As Long: iters = 0

    For gen = 1 To MAX_GEN
        If (Timer - t0) >= MAX_SECONDS Then Exit For
        Call UC_SortDesc(pop, fit)
        If fit(1) > bestFit Then
            bestFit = fit(1)
            Application.StatusBar = "GA gen " & gen & "  bestFit=" & _
                Format(bestFit, "0.00") & "/7  t=" & Format(Timer - t0, "0.0") & "s"
        End If
        If bestFit >= TARGET_FITNESS Then Exit For

        For i = 1 To ELITE
            For g = 0 To 6: newPop(i, g) = pop(i, g): Next g
            newFit(i) = fit(i)
        Next i

        Dim p1 As Long, p2 As Long
        For i = ELITE + 1 To POP_SIZE
            p1 = UC_Tournament(fit, TOURN_K)
            p2 = UC_Tournament(fit, TOURN_K)
            If Rnd < CX_RATE Then
                For g = 0 To 6
                    If b(g, 2) = 1 Then
                        If Rnd < 0.5 Then newPop(i, g) = pop(p1, g) Else newPop(i, g) = pop(p2, g)
                    Else
                        Dim lo As Double, hi As Double, alpha As Double, rng As Double
                        alpha = 0.3
                        lo = UC_Min2(pop(p1, g), pop(p2, g))
                        hi = UC_Max2(pop(p1, g), pop(p2, g))
                        rng = hi - lo
                        newPop(i, g) = (lo - alpha * rng) + Rnd * (rng * (1 + 2 * alpha))
                    End If
                Next g
            Else
                For g = 0 To 6: newPop(i, g) = pop(p1, g): Next g
            End If
            For g = 0 To 6
                If Rnd < MUT_RATE Then
                    Dim span As Double: span = (b(g, 1) - b(g, 0)) * 0.2
                    newPop(i, g) = newPop(i, g) + (Rnd - 0.5) * 2 * span
                End If
                newPop(i, g) = UC_Clamp(newPop(i, g), b(g, 0), b(g, 1), b(g, 2))
            Next g
            newFit(i) = UC_Fitness(newPop, i)
            iters = iters + 1
            If iters >= 1000 Then Exit For
        Next i

        For i = 1 To POP_SIZE
            For g = 0 To 6: pop(i, g) = newPop(i, g): Next g
            fit(i) = newFit(i)
        Next i
        If iters >= 1000 Then Exit For
    Next gen

    Call UC_SortDesc(pop, fit)

    Call UC_WriteCell("18_CONFIG", "C25", CLng(pop(1, 0)))
    Call UC_WriteCell("18_CONFIG", "C26", CLng(pop(1, 1)))
    Call UC_WriteCell("18_CONFIG", "C27", CLng(pop(1, 2)))
    Call UC_WriteCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H10", CLng(pop(1, 3)))
    Call UC_WriteCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H11", CLng(pop(1, 4)))
    Call UC_WriteCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H12", pop(1, 5))
    Call UC_WriteCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H13", pop(1, 6))

    Call UC_WriteReport(pop, fit, t0, gen, iters)

    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.StatusBar = False

    Dim msg As String
    msg = "Brain_FindUniversalCompat COMPLETADO" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "Mejor fitness: " & Format(fit(1), "0.00") & " / 7.00" & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "Generaciones: " & (gen - 1) & "  Evaluaciones: " & (POP_SIZE + iters) & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "Tiempo: " & Format(Timer - t0, "0.0") & " s" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "Config optima aplicada a 18_CONFIG y 9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD." & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "Reporte en hoja 35_UNIVERSAL_COMPAT_RESULT."
    If fit(1) >= 7# Then
        msg = msg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & ">>> CONFIG 100% UNIVERSAL ENCONTRADA <<<"
    ElseIf fit(1) >= TARGET_FITNESS Then
        msg = msg & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & ">>> CONFIG NEAR-UNIVERSAL (>= 6.95) <<<"
    End If
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox msg, vbInformation, "Brain_FindUniversalCompat"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.StatusBar = False
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "ERROR en Brain_FindUniversalCompat:" & vbCrLf & _
           Err.Number & " - " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub

Private Function UC_RandGene(lo As Double, hi As Double, isInt As Double) As Double
    Dim v As Double: v = lo + Rnd * (hi - lo)
    If isInt = 1 Then UC_RandGene = CDbl(CLng(v)) Else UC_RandGene = v
End Function

Private Function UC_Clamp(v As Double, lo As Double, hi As Double, isInt As Double) As Double
    If v < lo Then v = lo
    If v > hi Then v = hi
    If isInt = 1 Then v = CDbl(CLng(v))
    UC_Clamp = v
End Function

Private Function UC_Min2(a As Double, b As Double) As Double
    If a < b Then UC_Min2 = a Else UC_Min2 = b
End Function

Private Function UC_Max2(a As Double, b As Double) As Double
    If a > b Then UC_Max2 = a Else UC_Max2 = b
End Function

Private Sub UC_SortDesc(ByRef pop() As Double, ByRef fit() As Double)
    Dim n As Long: n = UBound(fit)
    Dim i As Long, j As Long, g As Long
    Dim keyF As Double, keyG(0 To 6) As Double
    For i = 2 To n
        keyF = fit(i)
        For g = 0 To 6: keyG(g) = pop(i, g): Next g
        j = i - 1
        Do While j >= 1
            If fit(j) >= keyF Then Exit Do
            fit(j + 1) = fit(j)
            For g = 0 To 6: pop(j + 1, g) = pop(j, g): Next g
            j = j - 1
        Loop
        fit(j + 1) = keyF
        For g = 0 To 6: pop(j + 1, g) = keyG(g): Next g
    Next i
End Sub

Private Function UC_Tournament(ByRef fit() As Double, k As Long) As Long
    Dim n As Long: n = UBound(fit)
    Dim best As Long, cand As Long, i As Long
    best = Int(Rnd * n) + 1
    For i = 2 To k
        cand = Int(Rnd * n) + 1
        If fit(cand) > fit(best) Then best = cand
    Next i
    UC_Tournament = best
End Function

Private Function UC_ReadCell(sheetName As String, cellAddr As String, fallback As Double) As Double
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim v As Variant
    v = ThisWorkbook.sheets(sheetName).Range(cellAddr).value
    If isNumeric(v) Then UC_ReadCell = CDbl(v) Else UC_ReadCell = fallback
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Sub UC_WriteCell(sheetName As String, cellAddr As String, value As Variant)
    On Error Resume Next
    ThisWorkbook.sheets(sheetName).Range(cellAddr).value = value
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Function UC_Fitness(ByRef pop() As Double, idx As Long) As Double
    Dim bufSec As Double: bufSec = pop(idx, 0)
    Dim liveDelay As Double: liveDelay = pop(idx, 1)
    Dim recAtt As Double: recAtt = pop(idx, 2)
    Dim fragRetry As Double: fragRetry = pop(idx, 3)
    Dim nudgeRetry As Double: nudgeRetry = pop(idx, 4)
    Dim abrFactor As Double: abrFactor = pop(idx, 5)
    Dim maxSync As Double: maxSync = pop(idx, 6)

    Dim bufStab As Double, bwHead As Double, errTree As Double, netLat As Double
    bufStab = UC_Bell(bufSec, 45, 30) * 0.6 + UC_Bell(liveDelay, 10, 6) * 0.4
    bwHead = UC_Clamp01((1# - abrFactor) * 0.6 + UC_Bell(maxSync, 1.2, 0.5) * 0.4)
    errTree = UC_Clamp01((recAtt / 200#) * 0.5 + (fragRetry / 20#) * 0.3 + (nudgeRetry / 15#) * 0.2)
    netLat = UC_Bell(liveDelay, 8, 5)

    Dim p As Long
    Dim total As Double: total = 0#
    For p = 1 To 7
        Dim h As Double, r As Double, s As Double
        h = UC_PlayerHealth(p, bufStab, bwHead, errTree, netLat)
        r = UC_PlayerRisk(p, bufSec, recAtt, abrFactor)
        s = UC_PlayerStall(p, bufStab, errTree)
        If h >= 70 And r <= 30 And s <= 0.15 Then
            total = total + 1#
        ElseIf h >= 50 And r <= 45 Then
            total = total + 0.5
        End If
    Next p
    UC_Fitness = total
End Function

Private Function UC_Bell(x As Double, center As Double, width As Double) As Double
    Dim d As Double: d = (x - center) / width
    UC_Bell = Exp(-0.5 * d * d)
End Function

Private Function UC_Clamp01(v As Double) As Double
    If v < 0 Then v = 0
    If v > 1 Then v = 1
    UC_Clamp01 = v
End Function

Private Function UC_PlayerHealth(p As Long, bufStab As Double, bwHead As Double, _
                                  errTree As Double, netLat As Double) As Double
    Dim w(1 To 4) As Double
    Select Case p
        Case 1: w(1) = 30: w(2) = 25: w(3) = 25: w(4) = 20  ' TiviMate
        Case 2: w(1) = 35: w(2) = 20: w(3) = 30: w(4) = 15  ' Kodi
        Case 3: w(1) = 20: w(2) = 30: w(3) = 20: w(4) = 30  ' VLC
        Case 4: w(1) = 25: w(2) = 30: w(3) = 25: w(4) = 20  ' HLS.js
        Case 5: w(1) = 40: w(2) = 20: w(3) = 25: w(4) = 15  ' Web0S
        Case 6: w(1) = 30: w(2) = 25: w(3) = 30: w(4) = 15  ' Smarters
        Case 7: w(1) = 35: w(2) = 20: w(3) = 30: w(4) = 15  ' ExoPlayer
    End Select
    UC_PlayerHealth = bufStab * w(1) + bwHead * w(2) + errTree * w(3) + netLat * w(4)
End Function

Private Function UC_PlayerRisk(p As Long, bufSec As Double, recAtt As Double, _
                                abrFactor As Double) As Double
    Dim base As Double: base = 0#
    If bufSec < 20 Then base = base + 25
    If bufSec > 150 Then base = base + 15
    If recAtt < 20 Then base = base + 10
    If abrFactor > 0.95 Then base = base + 15
    If p = 5 Or p = 6 Or p = 7 Then
        If bufSec > 120 Then base = base + 10
    End If
    UC_PlayerRisk = base
End Function

Private Function UC_PlayerStall(p As Long, bufStab As Double, errTree As Double) As Double
    Dim base As Double: base = 0.3 - (bufStab * 0.15) - (errTree * 0.1)
    If base < 0 Then base = 0
    If p = 4 Or p = 5 Then base = base + 0.03
    UC_PlayerStall = base
End Function

Private Sub UC_WriteReport(ByRef pop() As Double, ByRef fit() As Double, _
                            t0 As Double, gen As Long, iters As Long)
    Const SHN As String = "35_UNIVERSAL_COMPAT_RESULT"
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHN)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets.Add(After:=ThisWorkbook.sheets(ThisWorkbook.sheets.count))
        ws.name = SHN
    Else
        ws.Cells.Clear
    End If

    ws.Range("A1").value = "Brain_FindUniversalCompat - Resultado GA"
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("A1").Font.size = 14
    ws.Range("A2").value = "Ejecutado: " & Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss") & _
        "  |  Tiempo: " & Format(Timer - t0, "0.0") & "s" & _
        "  |  Gen: " & (gen - 1) & "  |  Evals: " & iters

    Dim hdrs As Variant
    hdrs = Array("Rank", "Fitness (0-7)", "buffer_seconds", "live_delay_s", _
                 "reconnect_att", "fragLoad_retry", "nudgeMaxRetry", _
                 "abrBWFactor", "maxLiveSyncPBR", "Verdict")
    Dim c As Long
    For c = 0 To UBound(hdrs)
        ws.Cells(4, c + 1).value = hdrs(c)
        ws.Cells(4, c + 1).Font.Bold = True
        ws.Cells(4, c + 1).Interior.Color = RGB(31, 78, 121)
        ws.Cells(4, c + 1).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    Next c

    Dim topN As Long: topN = 20
    If topN > UBound(fit) Then topN = UBound(fit)
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 1 To topN
        ws.Cells(4 + i, 1).value = i
        ws.Cells(4 + i, 2).value = fit(i)
        ws.Cells(4 + i, 3).value = CLng(pop(i, 0))
        ws.Cells(4 + i, 4).value = CLng(pop(i, 1))
        ws.Cells(4 + i, 5).value = CLng(pop(i, 2))
        ws.Cells(4 + i, 6).value = CLng(pop(i, 3))
        ws.Cells(4 + i, 7).value = CLng(pop(i, 4))
        ws.Cells(4 + i, 8).value = Round(pop(i, 5), 3)
        ws.Cells(4 + i, 9).value = Round(pop(i, 6), 3)
        Dim verdict As String
        If fit(i) >= 7 Then
            verdict = "UNIVERSAL 100%": ws.Cells(4 + i, 10).Interior.Color = RGB(146, 208, 80)
        ElseIf fit(i) >= 6 Then
            verdict = "NEAR UNIVERSAL": ws.Cells(4 + i, 10).Interior.Color = RGB(255, 235, 156)
        ElseIf fit(i) >= 4 Then
            verdict = "COMPAT PARCIAL": ws.Cells(4 + i, 10).Interior.Color = RGB(255, 192, 0)
        Else
            verdict = "LIMITADO": ws.Cells(4 + i, 10).Interior.Color = RGB(248, 105, 107)
        End If
        ws.Cells(4 + i, 10).value = verdict
    Next i

    ws.Columns("A:J").AutoFit
End Sub


Private Function FE_JsonEsc(s As String) As String
    Dim r As String: r = s
    r = Replace(r, "\", "\\")
    r = Replace(r, """", "\""")
    r = Replace(r, vbCrLf, "\n")
    r = Replace(r, vbCr, "\n")
    r = Replace(r, vbLf, "\n")
    r = Replace(r, vbTab, "\t")
    ' FIX v4.20.4: si es numero con coma decimal espanola (2,6 -> 2.6)
    If isNumeric(r) Then
        If InStr(r, ",") > 0 And InStr(r, ".") = 0 Then
            r = Replace(r, ",", ".")
        End If
    End If
    FE_JsonEsc = r
End Function


' ============================================================================
' COMPONENTE 1 (Omega 5) - Brain_ExportToFrontend
' Exporta LAB calibrado a JSON schema omega_v1 para consumo del frontend
' Output: C:\Users\HFRC\Downloads\LAB_CALIBRATED_<timestamp>.json
' ============================================================================
Public Sub Brain_ExportToFrontend()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual

    ' --- Construir el JSON ---
    Dim json As String
    json = "{" & vbCrLf
    json = json & """lab_version"": ""omega_v1""," & vbCrLf
    json = json & """playlist_format"": ""m3u8""," & vbCrLf
    json = json & """exported_at"": """ & Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-ddThh:nn:ss") & """," & vbCrLf

    ' === PROFILES CALIBRATED (P0-P5) desde 6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES ===
    json = json & """profiles_calibrated"": {" & vbCrLf
    Dim wsP As Worksheet: Set wsP = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_PROFILES)
    Dim lrP As Long: lrP = wsP.Cells(wsP.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
    Dim pids As Variant: pids = Array("P0", "P1", "P2", "P3", "P4", "P5")
    Dim pi As Long
    For pi = 0 To 5
        Dim pid As String: pid = CStr(pids(pi))
        Dim colN As Long: colN = 2 + pi ' B..G
        json = json & "  """ & pid & """: {" & vbCrLf

        ' Dentro del perfil, separar por prefijo (settings., vlcopt., kodiprop., headerOverrides., hlsjs., prefetch_config., other.)
        Dim sections As Variant
        sections = Array("settings", "vlcopt", "kodiprop", "headerOverrides", "hlsjs", "prefetch_config", "other")
        Dim si As Long
        Dim secCount As Long: secCount = 0
        Dim sectionsJson As String: sectionsJson = ""

        For si = 0 To UBound(sections)
            Dim sectionName As String: sectionName = CStr(sections(si))
            Dim secJson As String: secJson = ""
            Dim secEntries As Long: secEntries = 0

            Dim r As Long
            For r = 4 To lrP
                Dim keyFull As String: keyFull = CStr(wsP.Cells(r, 1).value)
                If keyFull <> "" And InStr(keyFull, sectionName & ".") = 1 Then
                    Dim subkey As String: subkey = Mid(keyFull, Len(sectionName) + 2)
                    Dim val As String: val = CStr(wsP.Cells(r, colN).value)
                    If val <> "" Then   ' C1 (2026-05-11) skip empty values en JSON export
                    If secJson <> "" Then secJson = secJson & "," & vbCrLf
                    secJson = secJson & "      """ & FE_JsonEsc(subkey) & """: """ & FE_JsonEsc(val) & """"
                    secEntries = secEntries + 1
                    End If   ' C1 (2026-05-11) skip empty values
                End If
            Next r

            If secEntries > 0 Then
                If secCount > 0 Then sectionsJson = sectionsJson & "," & vbCrLf
                sectionsJson = sectionsJson & "    """ & sectionName & """: {" & vbCrLf & secJson & vbCrLf & "    }"
                secCount = secCount + 1
            End If
        Next si

        ' Meta basic (id, name)
        Dim nameVal As String: nameVal = ""
        For r = 4 To lrP
            If CStr(wsP.Cells(r, 1).value) = "name" Then nameVal = CStr(wsP.Cells(r, colN).value): Exit For
        Next r
        Dim metaJson As String
        metaJson = "    ""id"": """ & pid & """," & vbCrLf & "    ""name"": """ & FE_JsonEsc(nameVal) & """"
        If sectionsJson <> "" Then metaJson = metaJson & "," & vbCrLf & sectionsJson

        json = json & metaJson & vbCrLf & "  }"
        If pi < 5 Then json = json & ","
        json = json & vbCrLf
    Next pi
    json = json & "}," & vbCrLf

    ' === NIVEL 1 DIRECTIVES desde 5_NIVEL_1_HEADER ===
    json = json & """nivel1_directives"": [" & vbCrLf
    Dim wsN1 As Worksheet: Set wsN1 = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_NIVEL1)
    Dim lrN1 As Long: lrN1 = wsN1.Cells(wsN1.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    Dim n1Count As Long: n1Count = 0
    Dim n1Json As String: n1Json = ""
    For r = 6 To lrN1
        If UCase(CStr(wsN1.Cells(r, 1).value)) = "X" Then
            Dim tag1 As String: tag1 = CStr(wsN1.Cells(r, 2).value)
            Dim val1 As String: val1 = CStr(wsN1.Cells(r, 3).value)
            If tag1 <> "" Then
                If n1Json <> "" Then n1Json = n1Json & "," & vbCrLf
                n1Json = n1Json & "  {""tag"": """ & FE_JsonEsc(tag1) & """, ""value"": """ & FE_JsonEsc(val1) & """}"
                n1Count = n1Count + 1
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    json = json & n1Json & vbCrLf & "]," & vbCrLf

    ' === NIVEL 3 PER LAYER desde 7_NIVEL_3_CHANNEL ===
    json = json & """nivel3_per_layer"": {" & vbCrLf
    Dim wsN3 As Worksheet: Set wsN3 = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_NIVEL3)
    Dim lrN3 As Long: lrN3 = wsN3.Cells(wsN3.rows.count, 3).End(xlUp).row
    Dim layersN3 As Variant: layersN3 = Array("EXTVLCOPT", "KODIPROP", "EXTHTTP", "EXTINF", "STREAM-INF", "SYS", "URL")
    Dim layerCodes As Variant: layerCodes = Array("VLC", "KOD", "HTT", "EI", "SI", "SYS", "URL")
    Dim li As Long, layerCount As Long: layerCount = 0
    Dim n3TotalEntries As Long: n3TotalEntries = 0
    For li = 0 To UBound(layersN3)
        Dim layerName As String: layerName = CStr(layersN3(li))
        Dim layerCode As String: layerCode = CStr(layerCodes(li))
        Dim layerJson As String: layerJson = ""
        Dim layerEntries As Long: layerEntries = 0
        For r = 6 To lrN3
            If UCase(CStr(wsN3.Cells(r, 1).value)) = "X" Then
                If InStr(UCase(CStr(wsN3.Cells(r, 2).value)), UCase(layerName)) > 0 Then
                    Dim k3 As String: k3 = CStr(wsN3.Cells(r, 3).value)
                    Dim v3 As String: v3 = CStr(wsN3.Cells(r, 4).value)
                    If k3 <> "" Then
                        If layerJson <> "" Then layerJson = layerJson & "," & vbCrLf
                        layerJson = layerJson & "    {""key"": """ & FE_JsonEsc(k3) & """, ""value"": """ & FE_JsonEsc(v3) & """}"
                        layerEntries = layerEntries + 1
                        n3TotalEntries = n3TotalEntries + 1
                    End If
                End If
            End If
        Next r
        If layerCount > 0 Then json = json & "," & vbCrLf
        json = json & "  """ & layerCode & """: [" & vbCrLf & layerJson
        If layerJson <> "" Then json = json & vbCrLf
        json = json & "  ]"
        layerCount = layerCount + 1
    Next li
    json = json & vbCrLf & "}," & vbCrLf

    ' === SERVERS desde 17_SERVERS_POOL ===
    json = json & """servers"": [" & vbCrLf
    Dim wsSrv As Worksheet: Set wsSrv = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_SERVERS)
    Dim lrSrv As Long: lrSrv = wsSrv.Cells(wsSrv.rows.count, 3).End(xlUp).row
    Dim srvJson As String: srvJson = ""
    For r = 4 To lrSrv
        If UCase(CStr(wsSrv.Cells(r, 1).value)) = "X" Then
            Dim sU As String: sU = CStr(wsSrv.Cells(r, 3).value)
            Dim sUser As String: sUser = CStr(wsSrv.Cells(r, 4).value)
            Dim sPass As String: sPass = CStr(wsSrv.Cells(r, 5).value)
            Dim sNote As String: sNote = CStr(wsSrv.Cells(r, 9).value)
            If sU <> "" Then
                If srvJson <> "" Then srvJson = srvJson & "," & vbCrLf
                srvJson = srvJson & "  {""base_url"": """ & FE_JsonEsc(sU) & """, ""username"": """ & FE_JsonEsc(sUser) & """, ""password"": """ & FE_JsonEsc(sPass) & """, ""note"": """ & FE_JsonEsc(sNote) & """}"
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    json = json & srvJson & vbCrLf & "]," & vbCrLf

    ' === EVASION POOL desde 16_EVASION ===
    json = json & """evasion_pool"": {" & vbCrLf
    Dim wsEv As Worksheet: Set wsEv = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_EVASION)
    ' User-Agents: filas 6-50 col 5
    json = json & "  ""user_agents"": [" & vbCrLf
    Dim uaJson As String: uaJson = ""
    For r = 6 To 50
        If UCase(CStr(wsEv.Cells(r, 1).value)) = "X" Then
            Dim uaVal As String: uaVal = CStr(wsEv.Cells(r, 5).value)
            If uaVal <> "" Then
                If uaJson <> "" Then uaJson = uaJson & "," & vbCrLf
                uaJson = uaJson & "    """ & FE_JsonEsc(uaVal) & """"
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    json = json & uaJson & vbCrLf & "  ]," & vbCrLf

    ' Referers
    json = json & "  ""referers"": [" & vbCrLf
    Dim refJson As String: refJson = ""
    Dim lrEv As Long: lrEv = wsEv.Cells(wsEv.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 10 To lrEv
        If UCase(CStr(wsEv.Cells(r, 1).value)) = "X" Then
            Dim refVal As String: refVal = CStr(wsEv.Cells(r, 3).value)
            If Left(LCase(refVal), 4) = "http" Then
                If refJson <> "" Then refJson = refJson & "," & vbCrLf
                refJson = refJson & "    """ & FE_JsonEsc(refVal) & """"
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    json = json & refJson & vbCrLf & "  ]" & vbCrLf
    json = json & "}," & vbCrLf

    ' === CONFIG GLOBAL desde 18_CONFIG ===
    json = json & """config_global"": {" & vbCrLf
    Dim wsCfg As Worksheet: Set wsCfg = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_CONFIG)
    Dim lrCfg As Long: lrCfg = wsCfg.Cells(wsCfg.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    Dim cfgJson As String: cfgJson = ""
    For r = 3 To lrCfg
        Dim kC As String: kC = CStr(wsCfg.Cells(r, 2).value)
        Dim vC As String: vC = CStr(wsCfg.Cells(r, 3).value)
        If kC <> "" Then
            If cfgJson <> "" Then cfgJson = cfgJson & "," & vbCrLf
            cfgJson = cfgJson & "  """ & FE_JsonEsc(kC) & """: """ & FE_JsonEsc(vC) & """"
        End If
    Next r
    json = json & cfgJson & vbCrLf & "}," & vbCrLf


    ' === PLACEHOLDERS MAP desde 32_PLACEHOLDERS_MAP (single source de placeholders -> valor real) ===
    json = json & """placeholders_map"": {" & vbCrLf
    Dim wsPM As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsPM = ThisWorkbook.sheets("32_PLACEHOLDERS_MAP")
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim phJson As String: phJson = ""
    Dim phCount As Long: phCount = 0
    If Not wsPM Is Nothing Then
        Dim lrPM As Long: lrPM = wsPM.Cells(wsPM.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
        Dim rp As Long
        For rp = 8 To lrPM
            Dim phKey As String: phKey = CStr(wsPM.Cells(rp, 2).value)
            Dim phVal As String: phVal = CStr(wsPM.Cells(rp, 3).value)
            If phKey <> "" And Left(phKey, 1) = "{" And phVal <> "" Then
                If InStr(phVal, "#REF!") = 0 And InStr(phVal, "#NAME?") = 0 And InStr(phVal, "#VALUE!") = 0 And InStr(phVal, "#N/A") = 0 Then
                    If phJson <> "" Then phJson = phJson & "," & vbCrLf
                    phJson = phJson & "  """ & FE_JsonEsc(phKey) & """: """ & FE_JsonEsc(phVal) & """"
                    phCount = phCount + 1
                End If
            End If
        Next rp
    End If
    json = json & phJson & vbCrLf & "}," & vbCrLf

    ' === SCORING METADATA ===
    Dim totalDirs As Long, refinedGe95 As Long
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim wsSc As Worksheet: Set wsSc = ThisWorkbook.sheets("34_BRAIN_SCORING")
    If Not wsSc Is Nothing Then
        Dim lrSc As Long: lrSc = wsSc.Cells(wsSc.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
        totalDirs = lrSc - 3
        Dim rs As Long
        For rs = 4 To lrSc
            If isNumeric(wsSc.Cells(rs, 15).value) Then
                If CDbl(wsSc.Cells(rs, 15).value) >= 95 Then refinedGe95 = refinedGe95 + 1
            End If
        Next rs
    End If
    On Error GoTo EH

    json = json & """scoring_metadata"": {" & vbCrLf
    json = json & "  ""total_directives"": " & totalDirs & "," & vbCrLf
    json = json & "  ""refined_ge95"": " & refinedGe95 & "," & vbCrLf
    json = json & "  ""nivel1_count"": " & n1Count & "," & vbCrLf
    json = json & "  ""nivel3_total"": " & n3TotalEntries & "," & vbCrLf
    json = json & "  ""profiles_count"": 6," & vbCrLf
    json = json & "  ""profile_keys_total"": " & (lrP - 3) & "," & vbCrLf
    json = json & "  ""placeholders_count"": " & phCount & vbCrLf
    json = json & "}" & vbCrLf

    json = json & "}"

    ' === ESCRIBIR ARCHIVO ===
    Dim fname As String
    fname = Environ("USERPROFILE") & "\Downloads\LAB_CALIBRATED_" & Format(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhmmss") & ".json"

    FE_WriteUtf8NoBom fname, json


    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True

    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "LAB CALIBRATED exportado correctamente" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Archivo: " & fname & vbCrLf & _
           "Tamano: " & Format(Len(json) / 1024, "0.0") & " KB" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Contenido:" & vbCrLf & _
           "  Perfiles: 6 (P0-P5)" & vbCrLf & _
           "  NIVEL_1 directivas: " & n1Count & vbCrLf & _
           "  Scoring refined >= 95: " & refinedGe95 & vbCrLf & _
           "  playlist_format: m3u8" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Siguiente paso: import desde el frontend." & vbCrLf & _
           "Tiempo: " & Round(Timer - t0, 1) & "s", _
           vbInformation, "EXPORT TO FRONTEND"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "ERROR " & Err.Number & ": " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub


' Helper: guarda string UTF-8 SIN BOM
Private Sub FE_WriteUtf8NoBom(ByVal filePath As String, ByVal content As String)
    Dim stream As Object: Set stream = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
    stream.Type = 2: stream.Charset = "utf-8": stream.Open
    stream.WriteText content
    stream.Position = 3 ' skip BOM
    Dim binary As Object: Set binary = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
    binary.Type = 1: binary.Mode = 3: binary.Open
    stream.CopyTo binary
    stream.Close
    binary.SaveToFile filePath, 2
    binary.Close
End Sub


' ============================================================================
' VISTA UNIFICADA DE VALORES CONFIGURADOS - hoja 37_VALORES_UNIFICADOS
' Cruza: Matrix (aplicabilidad) + Profiles (valores P0-P5) + Scoring (priority)
' ============================================================================
Public Sub Brain_BuildUnifiedValues()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual

    Dim wsMx As Worksheet: Set wsMx = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_MATRIX3D)
    Dim wsPr As Worksheet: Set wsPr = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_PROFILES)
    Dim wsN1 As Worksheet: Set wsN1 = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_NIVEL1)
    Dim wsN3 As Worksheet: Set wsN3 = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_NIVEL3)

    ' Cargar indice nombre -> valores P0-P5 desde NIVEL_2_PROFILES
    Dim dict As Object: Set dict = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim lrP As Long: lrP = wsPr.Cells(wsPr.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 4 To lrP
        Dim nm As String: nm = Trim(CStr(wsPr.Cells(r, 1).value))
        If nm <> "" Then
            Dim vals As String
            vals = CStr(wsPr.Cells(r, 2).value) & "||" & CStr(wsPr.Cells(r, 3).value) & "||" & _
                   CStr(wsPr.Cells(r, 4).value) & "||" & CStr(wsPr.Cells(r, 5).value) & "||" & _
                   CStr(wsPr.Cells(r, 6).value) & "||" & CStr(wsPr.Cells(r, 7).value)
            If Not dict.Exists(nm) Then dict.Add nm, vals
        End If
    Next r

    ' Cargar NIVEL_1 valores (col C)
    Dim dictN1 As Object: Set dictN1 = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim lrN1 As Long: lrN1 = wsN1.Cells(wsN1.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 6 To lrN1
        Dim nm1 As String: nm1 = Trim(CStr(wsN1.Cells(r, 2).value))
        If nm1 <> "" And Not dictN1.Exists(nm1) Then
            dictN1.Add nm1, CStr(wsN1.Cells(r, 3).value)
        End If
    Next r

    ' Cargar NIVEL_3 valores (col D con prefijo capa en col B+C)
    Dim dictN3 As Object: Set dictN3 = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim lrN3 As Long: lrN3 = wsN3.Cells(wsN3.rows.count, 3).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 6 To lrN3
        Dim capaN3 As String: capaN3 = Trim(CStr(wsN3.Cells(r, 2).value))
        Dim keyN3 As String: keyN3 = Trim(CStr(wsN3.Cells(r, 3).value))
        If capaN3 <> "" And keyN3 <> "" Then
            Dim fullKey As String: fullKey = capaN3 & "." & keyN3
            If Not dictN3.Exists(fullKey) Then
                dictN3.Add fullKey, CStr(wsN3.Cells(r, 4).value)
            End If
        End If
    Next r

    ' Cargar scoring refinado (si existe)
    Dim dictSc As Object: Set dictSc = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim wsSc As Worksheet: Set wsSc = ThisWorkbook.sheets("34_BRAIN_SCORING")
    If Not wsSc Is Nothing Then
        Dim lrSc As Long: lrSc = wsSc.Cells(wsSc.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
        For r = 4 To lrSc
            Dim dSc As String: dSc = CStr(wsSc.Cells(r, 1).value)
            Dim lvSc As String: lvSc = CStr(wsSc.Cells(r, 4).value)
            Dim lySc As String: lySc = CStr(wsSc.Cells(r, 5).value)
            Dim kSc As String: kSc = dSc & "|" & lvSc & "|" & lySc
            Dim scR As String
            If isNumeric(wsSc.Cells(r, 15).value) Then
                scR = CStr(wsSc.Cells(r, 15).value)
            Else
                scR = CStr(wsSc.Cells(r, 6).value)
            End If
            If Not dictSc.Exists(kSc) Then dictSc.Add kSc, scR
        Next r
    End If
    On Error GoTo EH

    ' Crear/limpiar hoja 37
    Dim SHEET_UV As String: SHEET_UV = "37_VALORES_UNIFICADOS"
    Dim wsUV As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsUV = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_UV)
    On Error GoTo EH
    If wsUV Is Nothing Then
        Set wsUV = ThisWorkbook.sheets.Add(After:=ThisWorkbook.sheets(ThisWorkbook.sheets.count))
        wsUV.name = SHEET_UV
    Else
        wsUV.Cells.Clear
    End If

    ' Headers
    wsUV.Cells(1, 1).value = "VALORES UNIFICADOS - directivas × capas × niveles × valores × score"
    wsUV.Cells(1, 1).Font.Bold = True
    wsUV.Cells(1, 1).Font.size = 12

    Dim hdrs As Variant
    hdrs = Array("Directiva", "Capa", "Nivel", "Source", "Valor_N1", "Valor_N3", _
                 "P0 val", "P1 val", "P2 val", "P3 val", "P4 val", "P5 val", "Score_ref")
    Dim c As Long
    For c = 1 To UBound(hdrs) + 1
        With wsUV.Cells(3, c)
            .value = hdrs(c - 1)
            .Font.Bold = True
            .Interior.Color = RGB(31, 78, 120)
            .Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
            .HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
        End With
    Next c

    ' Iterar matrix: 1 fila por cada intersección con X
    Dim lrMx As Long: lrMx = wsMx.Cells(wsMx.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    Dim arr As Variant
    arr = wsMx.Range(wsMx.Cells(4, 1), wsMx.Cells(lrMx, 55)).value
    Dim nRows As Long: nRows = UBound(arr, 1)

    Dim layNames As Variant
    layNames = Array("VLC", "KOD", "HTT", "EAU", "EI", "EM", "SI", "MD", "SYS", "UQP", "UQJ", "UQB", "UQE", "COK", "LIC", "EXD")
    Dim longNames As Variant
    longNames = Array("EXTVLCOPT", "KODIPROP", "EXTHTTP", "EAU", "EXTINF", "EM", "STREAM-INF", "MD", "SYS", "UQP", "UQJ", "UQB", "UQE", "COK", "LIC", "EXD")
    Dim lvlCodes As Variant
    lvlCodes = Array("N1_HEADER", "N2_PROFILES", "N3_CHANNEL")

    Dim outArr() As Variant
    ReDim outArr(1 To 30000, 1 To 13)
    Dim oi As Long: oi = 0

    Dim i As Long, levNum As Long, layNum As Long
    For i = 1 To nRows
        Dim dName As String: dName = CStr(arr(i, 2))
        Dim src As String: src = CStr(arr(i, 3))

        For levNum = 0 To 2
            For layNum = 0 To 15
                If CStr(arr(i, 8 + levNum * 16 + layNum)) <> "X" Then GoTo NextCell
                oi = oi + 1
                If oi > UBound(outArr, 1) Then ReDim Preserve outArr(1 To oi + 5000, 1 To 13)

                outArr(oi, 1) = dName
                outArr(oi, 2) = layNames(layNum)
                outArr(oi, 3) = lvlCodes(levNum)
                outArr(oi, 4) = src

                ' N1 value
                If dictN1.Exists(dName) Then outArr(oi, 5) = dictN1(dName) Else outArr(oi, 5) = ""

                ' N3 value
                Dim searchKey As String: searchKey = longNames(layNum) & "." & dName
                If dictN3.Exists(searchKey) Then outArr(oi, 6) = dictN3(searchKey) Else outArr(oi, 6) = ""

                ' P0-P5 values desde NIVEL_2_PROFILES
                If dict.Exists(dName) Then
                    Dim parts() As String: parts = Split(CStr(dict(dName)), "||")
                    If UBound(parts) >= 5 Then
                        outArr(oi, 7) = parts(0)
                        outArr(oi, 8) = parts(1)
                        outArr(oi, 9) = parts(2)
                        outArr(oi, 10) = parts(3)
                        outArr(oi, 11) = parts(4)
                        outArr(oi, 12) = parts(5)
                    End If
                End If

                ' Score
                Dim kSc2 As String: kSc2 = dName & "|" & lvlCodes(levNum) & "|" & layNames(layNum)
                If dictSc.Exists(kSc2) Then outArr(oi, 13) = CDbl(dictSc(kSc2)) Else outArr(oi, 13) = ""
NextCell:
            Next layNum
        Next levNum
    Next i

    ' Volcar
    If oi > 0 Then
        wsUV.Range(wsUV.Cells(4, 1), wsUV.Cells(3 + oi, 13)).value = outArr

        ' Formato condicional score col M
        Dim scRng As Range
        Set scRng = wsUV.Range(wsUV.Cells(4, 13), wsUV.Cells(3 + oi, 13))
        scRng.FormatConditions.Delete
        scRng.FormatConditions.AddColorScale ColorScaleType:=3
        scRng.FormatConditions(1).ColorScaleCriteria(1).FormatColor.Color = RGB(248, 105, 107)
        scRng.FormatConditions(1).ColorScaleCriteria(2).FormatColor.Color = RGB(255, 235, 132)
        scRng.FormatConditions(1).ColorScaleCriteria(3).FormatColor.Color = RGB(99, 190, 123)

        ' Pintar celdas con valor (no vacías) en verde claro
        Dim valCols As Range
        Set valCols = wsUV.Range(wsUV.Cells(4, 5), wsUV.Cells(3 + oi, 12))
        valCols.FormatConditions.Delete
        Dim fcVal As FormatCondition
        Set fcVal = valCols.FormatConditions.Add(Type:=xlCellValue, Operator:=xlNotEqual, Formula1:="""""")
        fcVal.Interior.Color = RGB(226, 239, 218)

        ' Sort por score desc
        wsUV.Range(wsUV.Cells(3, 1), wsUV.Cells(3 + oi, 13)).Sort _
            Key1:=wsUV.Cells(3, 13), Order1:=xlDescending, header:=xlYes
    End If

    wsUV.Columns("A").ColumnWidth = 45
    wsUV.Columns("B:C").ColumnWidth = 12
    wsUV.Columns("D").ColumnWidth = 10
    wsUV.Columns("E:L").ColumnWidth = 18
    wsUV.Columns("M").ColumnWidth = 10
    wsUV.Range("A3:M3").AutoFilter
    wsUV.Activate
    wsUV.Cells(4, 1).Select
    ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = False
    ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = True

    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True

    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "VALORES UNIFICADOS construidos" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Filas: " & oi & vbCrLf & _
           "Cada fila = 1 interseccion (directiva × capa × nivel) con X" & vbCrLf & _
           "Cols E-F: valor configurado en NIVEL_1 / NIVEL_3" & vbCrLf & _
           "Cols G-L: valor por perfil P0-P5" & vbCrLf & _
           "Col M: score_ref del BRAIN" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Verde = celda con valor configurado" & vbCrLf & _
           "Vacio = no hay valor aun (sera generado en O5)" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Tiempo: " & Round(Timer - t0, 1) & "s", _
           vbInformation, "UNIFIED VALUES"
    ' === HOOK CASCADE: derivar NIVEL_1 + validar NIVEL_3 ===
    On Error Resume Next
    Call Brain_DeriveNivel1FromNivel2
    Call Brain_ValidateNivel3Placeholders
    On Error GoTo 0

    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "ERROR " & Err.Number & ": " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub


' ============================================================================
' DASHBOARD OMEGA - integra metricas OMEGA dentro de 9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD
' sin tocar las 10 garantias clasicas (doctrina OMEGA-NO-DELETE)
' ============================================================================
Public Sub Brain_DashboardOmegaRefresh()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False

    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_BRAIN)
    Dim startRow As Long: startRow = 36

    ' Limpiar zona OMEGA previa (row 36 en adelante)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(startRow, 1), ws.Cells(80, 7)).Clear

    ' ========== HEADER OMEGA ==========
    ws.Cells(startRow, 1).value = "?? BRAIN OMEGA - INTEGRATION DASHBOARD"
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(startRow, 1), ws.Cells(startRow, 7)).Merge
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(startRow, 1), ws.Cells(startRow, 7)).Font.size = 13
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(startRow, 1), ws.Cells(startRow, 7)).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(startRow, 1), ws.Cells(startRow, 7)).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(startRow, 1), ws.Cells(startRow, 7)).Interior.Color = RGB(192, 0, 0)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(startRow, 1), ws.Cells(startRow, 7)).HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    ws.rows(startRow).RowHeight = 26

    Dim r As Long: r = startRow + 2

    ' ========== 1. PIPELINE STATUS ==========
    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "PIPELINE OMEGA"
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Merge
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Interior.Color = RGB(47, 85, 151)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    r = r + 1

    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "#": ws.Cells(r, 2).value = "Motor"
    ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "Hoja output": ws.Cells(r, 4).value = "Status"
    ws.Cells(r, 5).value = "Decisiones": ws.Cells(r, 6).value = "Ultimo run"
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 6)).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 6)).Interior.Color = RGB(220, 230, 241)
    r = r + 1

    ' OMEGA (hoja 34_BRAIN_SCORING)
    Dim hoja34Exists As Boolean: hoja34Exists = False
    Dim decisiones As Long: decisiones = 0
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim wsSc As Worksheet: Set wsSc = ThisWorkbook.sheets("34_BRAIN_SCORING")
    If Not wsSc Is Nothing Then
        hoja34Exists = True
        decisiones = wsSc.Cells(wsSc.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row - 3
    End If
    On Error GoTo EH

    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = 1
    ws.Cells(r, 2).value = "Brain_RunOmega (generalista)"
    ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "34_BRAIN_SCORING"
    ws.Cells(r, 4).value = IIf(hoja34Exists, "READY", "PENDING")
    ws.Cells(r, 5).value = decisiones
    ws.Cells(r, 6).value = IIf(hoja34Exists, Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd"), "-")
    Brain_PaintStatus ws.Cells(r, 4), CStr(ws.Cells(r, 4).value)
    r = r + 1

    ' Refinador
    Dim refinedDone As Boolean: refinedDone = False
    Dim ge95 As Long: ge95 = 0
    If hoja34Exists Then
        On Error Resume Next
        If CStr(wsSc.Cells(3, 15).value) = "Score_ref" Then refinedDone = True
        On Error GoTo EH
        If refinedDone Then
            Dim ii As Long, lrX As Long
            lrX = wsSc.Cells(wsSc.rows.count, 15).End(xlUp).row
            For ii = 4 To lrX
                If isNumeric(wsSc.Cells(ii, 15).value) Then
                    If CDbl(wsSc.Cells(ii, 15).value) >= 95 Then ge95 = ge95 + 1
                End If
            Next ii
        End If
    End If

    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = 2
    ws.Cells(r, 2).value = "Brain_RefineToMax (refinador)"
    ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "34 cols O-V"
    ws.Cells(r, 4).value = IIf(refinedDone, "READY", "PENDING")
    ws.Cells(r, 5).value = IIf(refinedDone, "=95: " & ge95, 0)
    ws.Cells(r, 6).value = IIf(refinedDone, Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd"), "-")
    Brain_PaintStatus ws.Cells(r, 4), CStr(ws.Cells(r, 4).value)
    r = r + 1

    ' RunAll5 (asumimos que siempre es ejecutable si las hojas existen)
    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = 3
    ws.Cells(r, 2).value = "Brain_RunAll5 (especialista)"
    ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "5/7/18/9"
    ws.Cells(r, 4).value = "READY"
    ws.Cells(r, 5).value = "5 pasos"
    ws.Cells(r, 6).value = Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd")
    Brain_PaintStatus ws.Cells(r, 4), CStr(ws.Cells(r, 4).value)
    r = r + 2

    ' ========== 2. PANEL CONFIG ACTUAL ==========
    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "PANEL 36_BRAIN_SELECTOR - CONFIG ACTIVA"
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Merge
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Interior.Color = RGB(47, 85, 151)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    r = r + 1

    On Error Resume Next
    Dim wsSel As Worksheet: Set wsSel = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR")
    On Error GoTo EH

    If Not wsSel Is Nothing Then
        ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "Granularidad": ws.Cells(r, 2).value = CStr(wsSel.Range("C5").value)
        ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "Intervencion": ws.Cells(r, 4).value = CStr(wsSel.Range("C6").value)
        ws.Cells(r, 5).value = "Player": ws.Cells(r, 6).value = CStr(wsSel.Range("C7").value)
        r = r + 1
        ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "Nivel pref": ws.Cells(r, 2).value = CStr(wsSel.Range("C8").value)
        ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "Fallback": ws.Cells(r, 4).value = CStr(wsSel.Range("C9").value)
        ws.Cells(r, 5).value = "Subset": ws.Cells(r, 6).value = CStr(wsSel.Range("C12").value)
        r = r + 1

        ' Capas y niveles activos
        Dim capCnt As Long: capCnt = 0
        Dim ccc As Long
        For ccc = 0 To 15
            If CStr(wsSel.Cells(18, 2 + ccc).value) = "X" Then capCnt = capCnt + 1
        Next ccc
        Dim nivCnt As Long: nivCnt = 0
        For ccc = 0 To 2
            If CStr(wsSel.Cells(22, 2 + ccc * 2).value) = "X" Then nivCnt = nivCnt + 1
        Next ccc

        ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "Capas ON": ws.Cells(r, 2).value = capCnt & "/16"
        ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "Niveles ON": ws.Cells(r, 4).value = nivCnt & "/3"
        ws.Cells(r, 5).value = "Cap EXTHTTP": ws.Cells(r, 6).value = CLng(wsSel.Range("C33").value) & "B"
        r = r + 1

        ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "Peso Q": ws.Cells(r, 2).value = CLng(wsSel.Range("C25").value)
        ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "Peso S": ws.Cells(r, 4).value = CLng(wsSel.Range("C26").value)
        ws.Cells(r, 5).value = "Peso L": ws.Cells(r, 6).value = CLng(wsSel.Range("C27").value)
        r = r + 1
        ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "Peso R": ws.Cells(r, 2).value = CLng(wsSel.Range("C28").value)
        ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "Peso A": ws.Cells(r, 4).value = CLng(wsSel.Range("C29").value)
        ws.Cells(r, 5).value = "SUMA": ws.Cells(r, 6).Formula = "='36_BRAIN_SELECTOR'!C30"
        r = r + 2
    End If

    ' ========== 3. MATRIX STATS ==========
    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "8_MATRIX_3D - ESTADO"
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Merge
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Interior.Color = RGB(47, 85, 151)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    r = r + 1

    On Error Resume Next
    Dim wsMx As Worksheet: Set wsMx = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_MATRIX3D)
    On Error GoTo EH
    If Not wsMx Is Nothing Then
        Dim lrMx As Long: lrMx = wsMx.Cells(wsMx.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
        Dim totalDirs As Long: totalDirs = lrMx - 3
        ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "Directivas totales": ws.Cells(r, 2).value = totalDirs
        ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "Niveles": ws.Cells(r, 4).value = 3
        ws.Cells(r, 5).value = "Capas": ws.Cells(r, 6).value = 16
        r = r + 1
        ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "Intersecciones posibles": ws.Cells(r, 2).value = totalDirs * 48

        ' Contar X en matriz
        Dim arrMx As Variant
        arrMx = wsMx.Range(wsMx.Cells(4, 8), wsMx.Cells(lrMx, 55)).value
        Dim xCount As Long: xCount = 0
        Dim iR As Long, iC As Long
        For iR = 1 To UBound(arrMx, 1)
            For iC = 1 To UBound(arrMx, 2)
                If CStr(arrMx(iR, iC)) = "X" Then xCount = xCount + 1
            Next iC
        Next iR
        ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "X marcadas": ws.Cells(r, 4).value = xCount
        ws.Cells(r, 5).value = "% coverage"
        ws.Cells(r, 6).value = Format(xCount / (totalDirs * 48), "0.00%")
        r = r + 2
    End If

    ' ========== 4. CANALES + SERVERS ==========
    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "33_CHANNELS_FROM_FRONTEND - CANALES"
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Merge
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Interior.Color = RGB(47, 85, 151)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    r = r + 1

    On Error Resume Next
    Dim wsCh As Worksheet: Set wsCh = ThisWorkbook.sheets("33_CHANNELS_FROM_FRONTEND")
    On Error GoTo EH
    If Not wsCh Is Nothing Then
        Dim lrCh As Long: lrCh = wsCh.Cells(wsCh.rows.count, 3).End(xlUp).row
        Dim chCnt As Long: chCnt = lrCh - 3
        ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "Total canales": ws.Cells(r, 2).value = chCnt

        ' Verificacion OK/SLOW/FAIL (col AP = 42)
        Dim vOK As Long, vSLOW As Long, vFAIL As Long, vPEND As Long
        Dim rc As Long
        If chCnt > 0 Then
            For rc = 4 To lrCh
                Select Case CStr(wsCh.Cells(rc, 42).value)
                    Case "OK": vOK = vOK + 1
                    Case "SLOW": vSLOW = vSLOW + 1
                    Case "FAIL": vFAIL = vFAIL + 1
                    Case "PENDING": vPEND = vPEND + 1
                End Select
            Next rc
        End If
        ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "OK": ws.Cells(r, 4).value = vOK
        ws.Cells(r, 5).value = "SLOW": ws.Cells(r, 6).value = vSLOW
        r = r + 1
        ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "FAIL": ws.Cells(r, 2).value = vFAIL
        ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "PENDING": ws.Cells(r, 4).value = vPEND
        If chCnt > 0 Then
            ws.Cells(r, 5).value = "% OK": ws.Cells(r, 6).value = Format(vOK / chCnt, "0.0%")
        End If
        r = r + 2
    End If

    ' ========== 5. MAPEO OMEGA -> 10 GARANTIAS ==========
    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "MAPEO OMEGA -> 10 GARANTIAS (como el BRAIN empuja cada una)"
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Merge
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Interior.Color = RGB(0, 112, 192)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    r = r + 1

    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "Garantia": ws.Cells(r, 2).value = "Score actual"
    ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "Factor OMEGA": ws.Cells(r, 4).value = "Peso panel"
    ws.Cells(r, 5).value = "Directivas clave en 34": ws.Cells(r, 6).value = "Accion push"
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 6)).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 6)).Interior.Color = RGB(220, 230, 241)
    r = r + 1

    ' Garantia -> factor OMEGA (referencia simbolica)
    Dim mapping As Variant
    mapping = Array( _
        Array("0 FREEZES (gap-controller)", "C13", "Stability (S)", "C26", "nudge, retry"), _
        Array("0 CORTES (ABR + redundant URLs)", "C14", "Reconnect (R)", "C28", "fallback, abr, retry"), _
        Array("0 REBUFFER (4-layer buffer)", "C15", "Anti-freeze (A)", "C29", "buffer, cache, prefetch"), _
        Array("0 LAG (live catch-up)", "C16", "Latency (L)", "C27", "livesync, ttfb, edge"), _
        Array("MAX ABR", "C18", "Quality (Q) + A", "C25+C29", "bitrate, abr, bandwidth"), _
        Array("MAX IMAGEN", "C19", "Quality (Q)", "C25", "codec, resolution, hdr, hevc, av1") _
    )
    Dim m As Long
    For m = 0 To UBound(mapping)
        ws.Cells(r, 1).value = mapping(m)(0)
        ws.Cells(r, 2).Formula = "='9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD'!" & mapping(m)(1)
        ws.Cells(r, 3).value = mapping(m)(2)
        ws.Cells(r, 4).value = mapping(m)(3)
        ws.Cells(r, 5).value = mapping(m)(4)
        ws.Cells(r, 6).value = "Subir peso en panel"

        ' Pintar score con gradiente
        With ws.Cells(r, 2)
            .FormatConditions.Delete
            Dim fcLow As FormatCondition
            Set fcLow = .FormatConditions.Add(Type:=xlCellValue, Operator:=xlLess, Formula1:="85")
            fcLow.Interior.Color = RGB(248, 105, 107)
            fcLow.Font.Color = RGB(156, 0, 6)
            Dim fcOK As FormatCondition
            Set fcOK = .FormatConditions.Add(Type:=xlCellValue, Operator:=xlGreaterEqual, Formula1:="95")
            fcOK.Interior.Color = RGB(99, 190, 123)
            fcOK.Font.Color = RGB(0, 97, 0)
        End With
        r = r + 1
    Next m

    r = r + 1

    ' ========== 6. ALERTAS - items <95 ==========
    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "? ALERTAS - GARANTIAS QUE EL BRAIN DEBE EMPUJAR"
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Merge
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Font.Bold = True
