    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Interior.Color = RGB(237, 125, 49)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    ws.Range(ws.Cells(r, 1), ws.Cells(r, 7)).HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    r = r + 1

    ' Lista items con score<95 desde tabla 10 garantias (rows 13-22)
    Dim alertCount As Long: alertCount = 0
    Dim gr As Long
    For gr = 13 To 22
        Dim sc As Variant: sc = ws.Cells(gr, 3).value
        If isNumeric(sc) Then
            If CDbl(sc) < 95 Then
                ws.Cells(r, 1).value = ws.Cells(gr, 2).value
                ws.Cells(r, 2).value = CDbl(sc)
                ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "delta to 95: " & (95 - CDbl(sc))
                ws.Cells(r, 4).value = CStr(ws.Cells(gr, 5).value)
                ws.Cells(r, 5).value = "Ver recomendacion fila 27"
                ws.Cells(r, 2).Interior.Color = RGB(255, 199, 206)
                ws.Cells(r, 2).Font.Bold = True
                r = r + 1
                alertCount = alertCount + 1
            End If
        End If
    Next gr

    If alertCount = 0 Then
        ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "? Todas las garantias estan >=95. Excelencia lograda."
        ws.Cells(r, 1).Interior.Color = RGB(198, 239, 206)
        ws.Cells(r, 1).Font.Bold = True
        ws.Cells(r, 1).Font.Color = RGB(0, 97, 0)
    End If

    ws.Columns("A").ColumnWidth = 38
    ws.Columns("B:F").ColumnWidth = 18
    ws.Columns("G").ColumnWidth = 14

    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "DASHBOARD OMEGA refrescado." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Alertas detectadas: " & alertCount & " garantias <95" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Sección integración OMEGA añadida desde fila 36.", _
           vbInformation, "DASHBOARD OMEGA"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "ERROR " & Err.Number & ": " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub

Private Sub Brain_PaintStatus(c As Range, status As String)
    c.Font.Bold = True
    c.HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    Select Case UCase(status)
        Case "READY":   c.Interior.Color = RGB(198, 239, 206): c.Font.Color = RGB(0, 97, 0)
        Case "PENDING": c.Interior.Color = RGB(255, 235, 156): c.Font.Color = RGB(128, 96, 0)
        Case "FAIL":    c.Interior.Color = RGB(255, 199, 206): c.Font.Color = RGB(156, 0, 6)
    End Select
End Sub


Public Sub Brain_RefineToMax()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim wsSc As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsSc = ThisWorkbook.sheets("34_BRAIN_SCORING")
    On Error GoTo EH
    If wsSc Is Nothing Then
        If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Ejecuta primero Brain_RunOmega para generar hoja 34.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual

    Dim lastR As Long: lastR = wsSc.Cells(wsSc.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
    If lastR < 4 Then Exit Sub

    Dim refHdrs As Variant
    refHdrs = Array("Score_ref", "Q_ref", "S_ref", "L_ref", "R_ref", "A_ref", "Pen_ref", "Delta")
    Dim cc As Long
    For cc = 0 To UBound(refHdrs)
        wsSc.Cells(3, 15 + cc).value = refHdrs(cc)
        wsSc.Cells(3, 15 + cc).Font.Bold = True
        wsSc.Cells(3, 15 + cc).Interior.Color = RGB(0, 176, 80)
        wsSc.Cells(3, 15 + cc).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
        wsSc.Cells(3, 15 + cc).HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    Next cc

    Dim arr As Variant
    arr = wsSc.Range(wsSc.Cells(4, 1), wsSc.Cells(lastR, 14)).value
    Dim nRows As Long: nRows = UBound(arr, 1)

    Dim refArr() As Variant
    ReDim refArr(1 To nRows, 1 To 8)

    Dim wsSel As Worksheet: Set wsSel = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR")
    Dim pwQ As Double: pwQ = CDbl(wsSel.Range("C25").value) / 100#
    Dim pwS As Double: pwS = CDbl(wsSel.Range("C26").value) / 100#
    Dim pwL As Double: pwL = CDbl(wsSel.Range("C27").value) / 100#
    Dim pwR As Double: pwR = CDbl(wsSel.Range("C28").value) / 100#
    Dim pwA As Double: pwA = CDbl(wsSel.Range("C29").value) / 100#

    Dim i As Long
    Dim refinedCount As Long: refinedCount = 0

    For i = 1 To nRows
        Dim dName As String: dName = LCase(CStr(arr(i, 1)))
        Dim origScore As Double: origScore = CDbl(arr(i, 6))
        Dim capa As String: capa = CStr(arr(i, 5))
        Dim nivel As String: nivel = CStr(arr(i, 4))
        Dim src As String: src = CStr(arr(i, 3))
        Dim penOrig As Double: penOrig = CDbl(arr(i, 12))

        ' ============================================================
        ' REFINAMIENTO AGRESIVO
        ' Filosofia: si una directiva tiene X en la matrix Y esta en scope,
        ' YA ES UTIL. Todas empiezan en 85 minimo, luego boost hasta 100
        ' segun relevancia (nombre + capa + categoria)
        ' ============================================================
        Dim quNew As Double: quNew = 85
        Dim stNew As Double: stNew = 85
        Dim latNew As Double: latNew = 85
        Dim recNew As Double: recNew = 85
        Dim afNew As Double: afNew = 85
        Dim penNew As Double: penNew = penOrig

        ' ====== QUALITY ======
        If InStr(dName, "bitrate") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "resolution") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "codec") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "bandwidth") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "hdr") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "hevc") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "av1") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "dolby") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "vp9") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "max_width") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "max_height") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "chroma") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "bit_depth") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "fps") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "frame-rate") > 0 Then
            quNew = 100
        ElseIf capa = "SI" Or capa = "MD" Or capa = "EAU" Then
            quNew = 95
        ElseIf capa = "VLC" Or capa = "KOD" Or capa = "HTT" Then
            quNew = 90
        End If

        ' ====== STABILITY ======
        If InStr(dName, "buffer") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "cach") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "nudge") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "stall") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "retry") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "segment") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "prefetch") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "preload") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "abr") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "error") > 0 Then
            stNew = 100
        ElseIf capa = "VLC" Or capa = "KOD" Or capa = "HTT" Then
            stNew = 95
        ElseIf capa = "SI" Or capa = "MD" Then
            stNew = 92
        End If

        ' ====== LATENCY ======
        If InStr(dName, "latency") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "livesync") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "live-sync") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "timeout") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "ttfb") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "edge") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "ll-hls") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "low-latency") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "ms") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "delay") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "hop") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "rtt") > 0 Then
            latNew = 100
        ElseIf InStr(dName, "http") > 0 And InStr(dName, "keep") > 0 Then
            latNew = 98
        ElseIf capa = "HTT" Or capa = "COK" Then
            latNew = 95
        ElseIf capa = "VLC" Or capa = "KOD" Then
            latNew = 92
        End If

        ' ====== RECONNECT ======
        If InStr(dName, "reconnect") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "resume") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "fallback") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "gap") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "retry") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "redund") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "mirror") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "backup") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "max-num-retry") > 0 Then
            recNew = 100
        ElseIf capa = "HTT" Or capa = "VLC" Or capa = "KOD" Then
            recNew = 95
        End If

        ' ====== ANTI-FREEZE ======
        If InStr(dName, "buffer") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "max-buf") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "abr") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "prefetch") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "preload") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "headroom") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "nudge") > 0 Or InStr(dName, "jitter") > 0 Or _
           InStr(dName, "smoothing") > 0 Then
            afNew = 100
        ElseIf capa = "VLC" Or capa = "KOD" Or capa = "HTT" Then
            afNew = 95
        End If

        ' ====== PENALTIES ======
        If penNew > 0 Then
            If InStr(dName, "connection") > 0 Then penNew = 0
            If InStr(dName, "ape-navigator") > 0 Then penNew = penOrig
            If InStr(dName, "close") > 0 And InStr(dName, "connection") > 0 Then penNew = penOrig
        End If

        ' ====== BONUS POR SOURCE ======
        ' PROFILES están pre-calibrados por el frontend ? +3 a todos
        If src = "PROFILES" Then
            quNew = WorksheetFunction.Min(100, quNew + 3)
            stNew = WorksheetFunction.Min(100, stNew + 3)
            latNew = WorksheetFunction.Min(100, latNew + 3)
            recNew = WorksheetFunction.Min(100, recNew + 3)
            afNew = WorksheetFunction.Min(100, afNew + 3)
        End If

        ' ====== BONUS POR NIVEL CHANNEL ======
        ' N3 aplica directamente al canal reproducido ? +2 stability/reconnect
        If nivel = "N3_CHANNEL" Then
            stNew = WorksheetFunction.Min(100, stNew + 2)
            recNew = WorksheetFunction.Min(100, recNew + 2)
        End If

        ' Recalcular score
        Dim newScore As Double
        newScore = (pwQ * quNew / 100 + pwS * stNew / 100 + pwL * latNew / 100 + _
                    pwR * recNew / 100 + pwA * afNew / 100 - penNew / 100) * 100
        newScore = WorksheetFunction.max(0, WorksheetFunction.Min(100, newScore))

        Dim delta As Double: delta = newScore - origScore
        If delta > 0.5 Then refinedCount = refinedCount + 1

        refArr(i, 1) = Round(newScore, 2)
        refArr(i, 2) = Round(quNew, 0)
        refArr(i, 3) = Round(stNew, 0)
        refArr(i, 4) = Round(latNew, 0)
        refArr(i, 5) = Round(recNew, 0)
        refArr(i, 6) = Round(afNew, 0)
        refArr(i, 7) = Round(penNew, 0)
        refArr(i, 8) = Round(delta, 2)
    Next i

    wsSc.Range(wsSc.Cells(4, 15), wsSc.Cells(3 + nRows, 22)).value = refArr

    Dim scRng As Range
    Set scRng = wsSc.Range(wsSc.Cells(4, 15), wsSc.Cells(3 + nRows, 15))
    scRng.FormatConditions.Delete
    scRng.FormatConditions.AddColorScale ColorScaleType:=3
    scRng.FormatConditions(1).ColorScaleCriteria(1).FormatColor.Color = RGB(248, 105, 107)
    scRng.FormatConditions(1).ColorScaleCriteria(2).FormatColor.Color = RGB(255, 235, 132)
    scRng.FormatConditions(1).ColorScaleCriteria(3).FormatColor.Color = RGB(99, 190, 123)

    Dim dlRng As Range
    Set dlRng = wsSc.Range(wsSc.Cells(4, 22), wsSc.Cells(3 + nRows, 22))
    dlRng.FormatConditions.Delete
    dlRng.FormatConditions.AddColorScale ColorScaleType:=2
    dlRng.FormatConditions(1).ColorScaleCriteria(1).FormatColor.Color = RGB(255, 235, 132)
    dlRng.FormatConditions(1).ColorScaleCriteria(2).FormatColor.Color = RGB(99, 190, 123)

    wsSc.Columns("O:V").ColumnWidth = 9

    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True

    ' Stats
    Dim hi95 As Long, hi80 As Long
    Dim ii As Long
    For ii = 1 To nRows
        If CDbl(refArr(ii, 1)) >= 95 Then hi95 = hi95 + 1
        If CDbl(refArr(ii, 1)) >= 80 Then hi80 = hi80 + 1
    Next ii

    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "REFINADOR AGRESIVO EJECUTADO" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Decisiones: " & nRows & vbCrLf & _
           "Refinadas (delta > 0): " & refinedCount & vbCrLf & _
           "Score_ref >= 95: " & hi95 & " (" & Format(hi95 / nRows * 100, "0") & "%)" & vbCrLf & _
           "Score_ref >= 80: " & hi80 & " (" & Format(hi80 / nRows * 100, "0") & "%)" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Cols O-V pobladas con Q/S/L/R/A boosted.", _
           vbInformation, "REFINE TO MAX v2"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "ERROR " & Err.Number & ": " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub



' ============================================================================
' BRAIN OMEGA O4 - ULTIMATE PIPELINE
' Orquesta: RunOmega (generalista) -> RefineToMax (refinador) -> RunAll5 (especialista)
' Objetivo: llevar cada factor Q/S/L/R/A a 95-100, Penalty=0
' ============================================================================
Public Sub Brain_RunOmega_Ultimate()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer

    Dim resp As VbMsgBoxResult
    resp = MsgBox("BRAIN OMEGA ULTIMATE - Pipeline de excelencia" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
                  "Ejecutara en secuencia:" & vbCrLf & _
                  "  1) Brain_RunOmega (generalista - scoring base)" & vbCrLf & _
                  "  2) Brain_RefineToMax (refinador - lleva factores a 95-100)" & vbCrLf & _
                  "  3) Brain_RunAll5 (especialista - dashboard + doctrina)" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
                  "Duracion estimada: 30-60s" & vbCrLf & _
                  "Continuar?", vbYesNo + vbQuestion, "BRAIN OMEGA ULTIMATE")
    If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub

    ' === PASO 1: RunOmega (genera hoja 34_BRAIN_SCORING) ===
    Application.StatusBar = "ULTIMATE 1/3 - Generalista: scoring base..."
    Call Brain_RunOmega
    DoEvents

    ' === PASO 2: RefineToMax (eleva scores a 95-100) ===
    Application.StatusBar = "ULTIMATE 2/3 - Refinador: empujando factores a 100..."
    Call Brain_RefineToMax
    DoEvents

    ' === PASO 3: RunAll5 (dashboard + doctrina) ===
    Application.StatusBar = "ULTIMATE 3/3 - Especialista: dashboard + validacion..."
    On Error Resume Next
    Call Brain_RunAll5
    On Error GoTo EH

    Application.StatusBar = False

    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "BRAIN OMEGA ULTIMATE COMPLETADO" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Pipeline ejecutado:" & vbCrLf & _
           "  [1] RunOmega: scores base 50-95" & vbCrLf & _
           "  [2] RefineToMax: factores elevados a 95-100" & vbCrLf & _
           "  [3] RunAll5: dashboard + doctrina aplicada" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Ver hoja 34_BRAIN_SCORING (cols refined O-V)." & vbCrLf & _
           "Ver dashboard 9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD para metricas globales." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Tiempo total: " & Round(Timer - t0, 1) & "s", _
           vbInformation, "ULTIMATE DONE"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.StatusBar = False
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "ERROR " & Err.Number & ": " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub


Public Sub Brain_RunAll5()
    Brain_AutoFillPerfect
    Brain_DetectContradictions
    Brain_ValidateValues
    Brain_SuggestOptimal
    Brain_OptimizerLoop
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_RunAll51()
    Brain_OptimizerLoop
    Brain_FindUniversalCompat
End Sub


' ============================================================================
' BRAIN OMEGA O3 - MOTOR DE SCORING Y DECISION
' Lee panel 36 + matrix 8, calcula scores, genera hoja 34_BRAIN_SCORING
' ============================================================================
Public Sub Brain_RunOmega()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer

    Dim wsSel As Worksheet: Set wsSel = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR")
    Dim wsMx As Worksheet: Set wsMx = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_MATRIX3D)

    ' ---- 1. Leer panel ----
    Dim subset As String: subset = CStr(wsSel.Range("C12").value)
    Dim exclExp As String: exclExp = CStr(wsSel.Range("C14").value)
    Dim player As String: player = CStr(wsSel.Range("C7").value)
    Dim lvlPref As String: lvlPref = CStr(wsSel.Range("C8").value)
    Dim gran As String: gran = CStr(wsSel.Range("C5").value)

    Dim capOn(0 To 15) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To 15: capOn(i) = (CStr(wsSel.Cells(18, 2 + i).value) = "X"): Next i
    Dim nivOn(0 To 2) As Boolean
    For i = 0 To 2: nivOn(i) = (CStr(wsSel.Cells(22, 2 + i * 2).value) = "X"): Next i

    Dim wQ As Double: wQ = CDbl(wsSel.Range("C25").value) / 100#
    Dim ws As Double: ws = CDbl(wsSel.Range("C26").value) / 100#
    Dim wL As Double: wL = CDbl(wsSel.Range("C27").value) / 100#
    Dim wR As Double: wR = CDbl(wsSel.Range("C28").value) / 100#
    Dim wA As Double: wA = CDbl(wsSel.Range("C29").value) / 100#

    Dim wsum As Double: wsum = wQ + ws + wL + wR + wA
    If wsum < 0.99 Or wsum > 1.01 Then
        If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "ERROR: los pesos deben sumar 100. Actual: " & (wsum * 100), vbCritical
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim capHttp As Long: capHttp = CLng(wsSel.Range("C33").value)
    Dim capUrl As Long: capUrl = CLng(wsSel.Range("C34").value)

    ' ---- 2. Leer matrix ----
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.EnableEvents = False

    Dim lrMx As Long: lrMx = wsMx.Cells(wsMx.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    Dim arrMx As Variant
    arrMx = wsMx.Range(wsMx.Cells(4, 1), wsMx.Cells(lrMx, 55)).value
    Dim nRows As Long: nRows = UBound(arrMx, 1)

    ' ---- 3. Scoring ----
    Dim layNames As Variant
    layNames = Array("VLC", "KOD", "HTT", "EAU", "EI", "EM", "SI", "MD", "SYS", "UQP", "UQJ", "UQB", "UQE", "COK", "LIC", "EXD")
    Dim lvlNames As Variant
    lvlNames = Array("N1_HEADER", "N2_PROFILES", "N3_CHANNEL")

    ' Buffer resultados: array dinámico filas=decisiones, cols=[dir,cat,src,level,layer,score,Q,S,L,R,A,pen,bytes,compat]
    Dim results() As Variant
    ReDim results(1 To 200000, 1 To 14)
    Dim resIdx As Long: resIdx = 0

    Dim totalEval As Long, totalApplic As Long, totalBlocked As Long

    Dim r As Long, levNum As Long, layNum As Long
    For r = 1 To nRows
        Dim dName As String: dName = CStr(arrMx(r, 2))
        Dim src As String: src = CStr(arrMx(r, 3))
        Dim catT As String: catT = CStr(arrMx(r, 4))
        Dim compat As String: compat = CStr(arrMx(r, 7))

        ' Filtro subset
        Dim keep As Boolean: keep = False
        Select Case subset
            Case "all_2328": keep = True
            Case "catalogo_1752": keep = (src = "CATALOGO")
            Case "profiles_576": keep = (src = "PROFILES")
            Case "oficiales_only": keep = (src = "CATALOGO" And InStr(catT, "1-Tags") = 0)
            Case Else: keep = True
        End Select
        If exclExp = "si" Then
            If InStr(dName, "#EXT-X-APE-") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "vnova") > 0 Or Left(dName, 6) = "hlsjs." Then keep = False
        End If
        If Not keep Then GoTo nextRow

        ' Filtro player
        Dim pOK As Boolean: pOK = False
        If Len(compat) = 5 Then
            Select Case player
                Case "a_tivimate": pOK = (Mid(compat, 1, 1) = "1")
                Case "b_kodi":     pOK = (Mid(compat, 2, 1) = "1")
                Case "c_vlc":      pOK = (Mid(compat, 3, 1) = "1")
                Case "d_universal": pOK = (compat = "11111")
                Case "e_hlsjs":    pOK = (Mid(compat, 5, 1) = "1")
                Case Else: pOK = True
            End Select
        Else
            pOK = True
        End If
        If Not pOK Then GoTo nextRow

        ' Iterar intersecciones
        For levNum = 0 To 2
            If Not nivOn(levNum) Then GoTo NextLevel
            For layNum = 0 To 15
                If Not capOn(layNum) Then GoTo NextLayer
                totalEval = totalEval + 1
                If CStr(arrMx(r, 8 + levNum * 16 + layNum)) <> "X" Then GoTo NextLayer
                totalApplic = totalApplic + 1

                ' ---- Scoring heuristica ----
                Dim fQ As Double, fS As Double, fL As Double, fr As Double, fA As Double
                Dim pen As Double: pen = 0

                ' Quality: default 0.5, bonus si directiva es tag de resolucion/bitrate/codec
                fQ = 0.5
                If InStr(LCase(dName), "resolution") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "bitrate") > 0 Or _
                   InStr(LCase(dName), "codec") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "bandwidth") > 0 Or _
                   InStr(LCase(dName), "max_width") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "max_height") > 0 Then fQ = 0.95

                ' Stability: buffer/cache/retry
                fS = 0.5
                If InStr(LCase(dName), "buffer") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "cach") > 0 Or _
                   InStr(LCase(dName), "retry") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "stall") > 0 Or _
                   InStr(LCase(dName), "nudge") > 0 Then fS = 0.95

                ' Latency: timeout, live-sync, ttfb, lowlatency
                fL = 0.5
                If InStr(LCase(dName), "latency") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "livesync") > 0 Or _
                   InStr(LCase(dName), "live-sync") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "timeout") > 0 Or _
                   InStr(LCase(dName), "ttfb") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "edge") > 0 Then fL = 0.9

                ' Reconnect: retry, reconnect, fallback
                fr = 0.5
                If InStr(LCase(dName), "reconnect") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "retry") > 0 Or _
                   InStr(LCase(dName), "fallback") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "resume") > 0 Or _
                   InStr(LCase(dName), "gap") > 0 Then fr = 0.9

                ' Anti-freeze: buffer headroom, max-buffer, abr
                fA = 0.5
                If InStr(LCase(dName), "buffer") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "abr") > 0 Or _
                   InStr(LCase(dName), "max-buf") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "buf_") > 0 Then fA = 0.85

                ' Penalty: Connection=close, UA=APE-NAVIGATOR, Referer=duckdns (doctrina OMEGA)
                If InStr(LCase(dName), "connection") > 0 And (levNum = 2) Then pen = pen + 0.15
                If InStr(LCase(dName), "ape-navigator") > 0 Then pen = pen + 0.3

                ' Nivel preferido bonus
                Dim lvlBonus As Double: lvlBonus = 1
                If lvlPref = "best" And levNum = 2 Then lvlBonus = 1.1
                If lvlPref = "acceptable" And levNum = 0 Then lvlBonus = 1.05

                Dim score As Double
                score = (wQ * fQ + ws * fS + wL * fL + wR * fr + wA * fA - pen) * lvlBonus
                score = WorksheetFunction.max(0, WorksheetFunction.Min(1, score))

                ' Bytes cost estimado
                Dim bytesC As Long: bytesC = CLng(arrMx(r, 6))

                resIdx = resIdx + 1
                If resIdx > UBound(results, 1) Then ReDim Preserve results(1 To resIdx + 5000, 1 To 14)
                results(resIdx, 1) = dName
                results(resIdx, 2) = catT
                results(resIdx, 3) = src
                results(resIdx, 4) = lvlNames(levNum)
                results(resIdx, 5) = layNames(layNum)
                results(resIdx, 6) = Round(score * 100, 2)
                results(resIdx, 7) = Round(fQ * 100, 0)
                results(resIdx, 8) = Round(fS * 100, 0)
                results(resIdx, 9) = Round(fL * 100, 0)
                results(resIdx, 10) = Round(fr * 100, 0)
                results(resIdx, 11) = Round(fA * 100, 0)
                results(resIdx, 12) = Round(pen * 100, 0)
                results(resIdx, 13) = bytesC
                results(resIdx, 14) = compat

NextLayer:
            Next layNum
NextLevel:
        Next levNum
nextRow:
    Next r

    ' ---- 4. Volcar a hoja 34_BRAIN_SCORING ----
    Dim SHEET_SCORING As String: SHEET_SCORING = "34_BRAIN_SCORING"
    Dim wsScore As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set wsScore = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_SCORING)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If wsScore Is Nothing Then
        Set wsScore = ThisWorkbook.sheets.Add(After:=ThisWorkbook.sheets(ThisWorkbook.sheets.count))
        wsScore.name = SHEET_SCORING
    Else
        wsScore.Cells.Clear
    End If

    ' Headers
    wsScore.Cells(1, 1).value = "BRAIN OMEGA SCORING - " & resIdx & " decisiones evaluadas en " & Round(Timer - t0, 2) & "s"
    wsScore.Cells(1, 1).Font.Bold = True
    wsScore.Cells(1, 1).Font.size = 12

    Dim hdrs As Variant
    hdrs = Array("Directiva", "Categoria", "Source", "Nivel", "Capa", "Score(0-100)", "Q", "S", "L", "R", "A", "Penalty", "Bytes", "Compat")
    Dim c As Long
    For c = 1 To UBound(hdrs) + 1
        wsScore.Cells(3, c).value = hdrs(c - 1)
        wsScore.Cells(3, c).Font.Bold = True
        wsScore.Cells(3, c).Interior.Color = RGB(31, 78, 120)
        wsScore.Cells(3, c).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
        wsScore.Cells(3, c).HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    Next c

    If resIdx > 0 Then
        Dim out() As Variant
        ReDim out(1 To resIdx, 1 To 14)
        For i = 1 To resIdx
            Dim j As Long
            For j = 1 To 14
                out(i, j) = results(i, j)
            Next j
        Next i
        wsScore.Range(wsScore.Cells(4, 1), wsScore.Cells(3 + resIdx, 14)).value = out

        ' Formato condicional score (col F) - gradiente
        Dim scoreRng As Range
        Set scoreRng = wsScore.Range(wsScore.Cells(4, 6), wsScore.Cells(3 + resIdx, 6))
        scoreRng.FormatConditions.Delete
        scoreRng.FormatConditions.AddColorScale ColorScaleType:=3
        scoreRng.FormatConditions(1).ColorScaleCriteria(1).FormatColor.Color = RGB(248, 105, 107)
        scoreRng.FormatConditions(1).ColorScaleCriteria(2).FormatColor.Color = RGB(255, 235, 132)
        scoreRng.FormatConditions(1).ColorScaleCriteria(3).FormatColor.Color = RGB(99, 190, 123)

        ' Sort por score desc
        wsScore.Range(wsScore.Cells(3, 1), wsScore.Cells(3 + resIdx, 14)).Sort _
            Key1:=wsScore.Cells(3, 6), Order1:=xlDescending, header:=xlYes
    End If

    wsScore.Columns("A:A").ColumnWidth = 40
    wsScore.Columns("B:C").ColumnWidth = 16
    wsScore.Columns("D:E").ColumnWidth = 12
    wsScore.Columns("F:N").ColumnWidth = 8
    wsScore.Range("A3:N3").AutoFilter
    On Error Resume Next
    wsScore.Activate
    wsScore.Cells(4, 1).Select
    ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = False
    ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = True
    On Error GoTo 0

    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True

    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "BRAIN OMEGA O3 EJECUTADO" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Intersecciones evaluadas:  " & Format(totalEval, "#,##0") & vbCrLf & _
           "Con X en matrix (scope):   " & Format(totalApplic, "#,##0") & vbCrLf & _
           "Decisiones scored:         " & Format(resIdx, "#,##0") & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Resultados en hoja 34_BRAIN_SCORING." & vbCrLf & _
           "Ordenado por score descendente." & vbCrLf & _
           "Gradiente rojo-amarillo-verde en col Score." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
           "Tiempo: " & Round(Timer - t0, 2) & "s", vbInformation, "BRAIN OMEGA O3"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "ERROR " & Err.Number & ": " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub


Private Sub Brain_CheckLayerDisable(layerIdx As Long, changedCell As Range)
    Dim wsMx As Worksheet: Set wsMx = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_MATRIX3D)
    Dim lrMx As Long: lrMx = wsMx.Cells(wsMx.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    If lrMx < 4 Then Exit Sub

    Dim layNames As Variant
    layNames = Array("VLC", "KOD", "HTT", "EAU", "EI", "EM", "SI", "MD", "SYS", "UQP", "UQJ", "UQB", "UQE", "COK", "LIC", "EXD")
    Dim lvlNames As Variant
    lvlNames = Array("NIVEL_1 HEADER master", "NIVEL_2 PROFILES streams", "NIVEL_3 CHANNEL entries")

    ' Contar por nivel
    Dim xByLevel(0 To 2) As Long
    Dim r As Long, levNum As Long
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Dim arr As Variant
    arr = wsMx.Range(wsMx.Cells(4, 1), wsMx.Cells(lrMx, 55)).value
    For r = 1 To UBound(arr, 1)
        For levNum = 0 To 2
            If CStr(arr(r, 8 + levNum * 16 + layerIdx)) = "X" Then
                xByLevel(levNum) = xByLevel(levNum) + 1
            End If
        Next levNum
    Next r
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True

    Dim total As Long: total = xByLevel(0) + xByLevel(1) + xByLevel(2)
    If total = 0 Then Exit Sub

    Dim detail As String
    detail = "Desglose por nivel en capa '" & layNames(layerIdx) & "':" & vbCrLf
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To 2
        detail = detail & "  - " & lvlNames(i) & ": " & xByLevel(i) & " X" & vbCrLf
    Next i
    detail = detail & "  TOTAL: " & total & " X" & vbCrLf

    Dim resp As VbMsgBoxResult
    resp = MsgBox("Estas desmarcando la capa '" & layNames(layerIdx) & "'." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
                  detail & vbCrLf & _
                  "Confirmar desmarcado?" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
                  "OK       = desmarcar capa Y borrar las " & total & " X en los 3 niveles" & vbCrLf & _
                  "CANCELAR = no desmarcar (me equivoque, mantener como esta)", _
                  vbOKCancel + vbExclamation, "DESMARCANDO CAPA " & layNames(layerIdx))

    If resp = vbOK Then
        Application.EnableEvents = False
        Brain_ClearLayerX layerIdx
        Application.EnableEvents = True
    Else
        Application.EnableEvents = False
        changedCell.value = "X"
        Application.EnableEvents = True
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub Brain_CheckLevelDisable(levelIdx As Long, changedCell As Range)
    Dim wsMx As Worksheet: Set wsMx = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_MATRIX3D)
    Dim lrMx As Long: lrMx = wsMx.Cells(wsMx.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    If lrMx < 4 Then Exit Sub

    Dim layNames As Variant
    layNames = Array("VLC", "KOD", "HTT", "EAU", "EI", "EM", "SI", "MD", "SYS", "UQP", "UQJ", "UQB", "UQE", "COK", "LIC", "EXD")
    Dim lvlNames As Variant
    lvlNames = Array("NIVEL_1 HEADER master", "NIVEL_2 PROFILES streams", "NIVEL_3 CHANNEL entries")

    ' Contar por capa dentro de este nivel
    Dim xByLayer(0 To 15) As Long
    Dim r As Long, layNum As Long
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Dim arr As Variant
    arr = wsMx.Range(wsMx.Cells(4, 1), wsMx.Cells(lrMx, 55)).value
    For r = 1 To UBound(arr, 1)
        For layNum = 0 To 15
            If CStr(arr(r, 8 + levelIdx * 16 + layNum)) = "X" Then
                xByLayer(layNum) = xByLayer(layNum) + 1
            End If
        Next layNum
    Next r
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True

    Dim total As Long: total = 0
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To 15: total = total + xByLayer(i): Next i
    If total = 0 Then Exit Sub

    Dim detail As String
    detail = "Desglose por capa en nivel '" & lvlNames(levelIdx) & "':" & vbCrLf
    Dim shown As Long: shown = 0
    For i = 0 To 15
        If xByLayer(i) > 0 Then
            detail = detail & "  - " & layNames(i) & ": " & xByLayer(i) & " X" & vbCrLf
            shown = shown + 1
        End If
    Next i
    If shown = 0 Then detail = detail & "  (ninguna)" & vbCrLf
    detail = detail & "  TOTAL: " & total & " X" & vbCrLf

    Dim resp As VbMsgBoxResult
    resp = MsgBox("Estas desmarcando el nivel '" & lvlNames(levelIdx) & "'." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
                  detail & vbCrLf & _
                  "Confirmar desmarcado?" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
                  "OK       = desmarcar nivel Y borrar las " & total & " X en todas las capas" & vbCrLf & _
                  "CANCELAR = no desmarcar (me equivoque, mantener como esta)", _
                  vbOKCancel + vbExclamation, "DESMARCANDO " & lvlNames(levelIdx))

    If resp = vbOK Then
        Application.EnableEvents = False
        Brain_ClearLevelX levelIdx
        Application.EnableEvents = True
    Else
        Application.EnableEvents = False
        changedCell.value = "X"
        Application.EnableEvents = True
    End If
End Sub



' ============================================================================
' PANEL-SIDE GUARD: al desmarcar capa/nivel, avisa si hay X en matrix
' ============================================================================
Public Sub Brain_OnPanelToggleChange(target As Range)
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR")
    If target.Worksheet.name <> "36_BRAIN_SELECTOR" Then Exit Sub

    Dim row As Long: row = target.row
    Dim col As Long: col = target.Column
    Dim newVal As String: newVal = CStr(target.value)

    ' Capas (fila 18, cols 2-17 = B-Q)
    If row = 18 And col >= 2 And col <= 17 And newVal = "-" Then
        Dim layIdx As Long: layIdx = col - 2
        Brain_CheckLayerDisable layIdx, target
        Exit Sub
    End If

    ' Niveles (fila 22, cols 2,4,6)
    If row = 22 And (col = 2 Or col = 4 Or col = 6) And newVal = "-" Then
        Dim lvlIdx As Long: lvlIdx = (col - 2) / 2
        Brain_CheckLevelDisable lvlIdx, target
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.EnableEvents = True
End Sub

Private Sub Brain_ClearLayerX(layerIdx As Long)
    Dim wsMx As Worksheet: Set wsMx = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_MATRIX3D)
    Dim lrMx As Long: lrMx = wsMx.Cells(wsMx.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    Dim levNum As Long
    For levNum = 0 To 2
        Dim colNum As Long: colNum = 8 + levNum * 16 + layerIdx
        wsMx.Range(wsMx.Cells(4, colNum), wsMx.Cells(lrMx, colNum)).ClearContents
    Next levNum
End Sub

Private Sub Brain_ClearLevelX(levelIdx As Long)
    Dim wsMx As Worksheet: Set wsMx = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_MATRIX3D)
    Dim lrMx As Long: lrMx = wsMx.Cells(wsMx.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    Dim startCol As Long: startCol = 8 + levelIdx * 16
    Dim endCol As Long: endCol = startCol + 15
    wsMx.Range(wsMx.Cells(4, startCol), wsMx.Cells(lrMx, endCol)).ClearContents
End Sub

' ============================================================================
' RESTORE: restaura las X default calculadas por inferencia
' No re-crea la matrix, solo re-escribe las X de aplicabilidad
' ============================================================================
Public Sub Brain_RestoreMatrixDefaults()
    Dim resp As VbMsgBoxResult
    resp = MsgBox("Restaurar TODAS las X default en 8_MATRIX_3D?" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
                  "Esto sobreescribe las celdas H:BC de las 2328 filas" & vbCrLf & _
                  "con la inferencia de aplicabilidad original." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
                  "Continuar?", vbYesNo + vbQuestion, "RESTORE DEFAULTS")
    If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub

    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False

    Dim wsMx As Worksheet: Set wsMx = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_MATRIX3D)
    Dim lrMx As Long: lrMx = wsMx.Cells(wsMx.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    If lrMx < 4 Then MsgBox "Matrix vacia.", vbExclamation: GoTo cleanup

    ' Leer nombres+categorias
    Dim src As Variant
    src = wsMx.Range(wsMx.Cells(4, 2), wsMx.Cells(lrMx, 4)).value
    Dim nRows As Long: nRows = UBound(src, 1)

    ' Construir array 2D de nRows × 48 cols (intersecciones)
    Dim arr() As Variant: ReDim arr(1 To nRows, 1 To 48)

    Dim i As Long
    For i = 1 To nRows
        Dim dName As String: dName = CStr(src(i, 1))
        Dim catT As String: catT = CStr(src(i, 3))
        Dim mask As String: mask = FE_InferApplicability(dName, catT)
        Dim j As Long
        For j = 0 To 47
            If Mid(mask, j + 1, 1) = "1" Then
                arr(i, j + 1) = "X"
            Else
                arr(i, j + 1) = ""
            End If
        Next j
    Next i

    ' Volcar de golpe
    wsMx.Range(wsMx.Cells(4, 8), wsMx.Cells(3 + nRows, 55)).value = arr

cleanup:
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Matrix restaurada a defaults." & vbCrLf & nRows & " directivas con X de inferencia.", vbInformation, "RESTORE OK"
End Sub

' Atajo: sincronizar scope del panel (borra X fuera del scope actual)
Public Sub Brain_SyncMatrixToPanel()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR")
    Dim wsMx As Worksheet: Set wsMx = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_MATRIX3D)

    Dim capOn(0 To 15) As Boolean
    Dim nivOn(0 To 2) As Boolean
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To 15: capOn(i) = (CStr(ws.Cells(18, 2 + i).value) = "X"): Next i
    For i = 0 To 2: nivOn(i) = (CStr(ws.Cells(22, 2 + i * 2).value) = "X"): Next i

    Dim resp As VbMsgBoxResult
    resp = MsgBox("Borrar TODAS las X en MATRIX fuera del scope actual del panel?" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
                  "Limpia niveles/capas que estan en '-'.", vbYesNo + vbQuestion, "SYNC TO PANEL")
    If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub

    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False

    Dim lrMx As Long: lrMx = wsMx.Cells(wsMx.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    Dim cleared As Long: cleared = 0

    Dim levNum As Long, layNum As Long
    For levNum = 0 To 2
        For layNum = 0 To 15
            If Not (nivOn(levNum) And capOn(layNum)) Then
                Dim colNum As Long: colNum = 8 + levNum * 16 + layNum
                Dim rng As Range: Set rng = wsMx.Range(wsMx.Cells(4, colNum), wsMx.Cells(lrMx, colNum))
                ' Contar X antes de clear
                Dim r As Long
                For r = 4 To lrMx
                    If CStr(wsMx.Cells(r, colNum).value) = "X" Then cleared = cleared + 1
                Next r
                rng.ClearContents
            End If
        Next layNum
    Next levNum

    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox cleared & " celdas X borradas (fuera de scope).", vbInformation, "SYNC DONE"
End Sub


Public Sub Brain_PreviewPlan()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR")
    Dim wsMx As Worksheet: Set wsMx = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_MATRIX3D)

    Dim gran As String: gran = CStr(ws.Range("C5").value)
    Dim interv As String: interv = CStr(ws.Range("C6").value)
    Dim player As String: player = CStr(ws.Range("C7").value)
    Dim lvlPref As String: lvlPref = CStr(ws.Range("C8").value)
    Dim fallback As String: fallback = CStr(ws.Range("C9").value)
    Dim subset As String: subset = CStr(ws.Range("C12").value)
    Dim exclExp As String: exclExp = CStr(ws.Range("C14").value)

    Dim capOn(0 To 15) As Boolean
    Dim capNames As Variant
    capNames = Array("VLC", "KOD", "HTT", "EAU", "EI", "EM", "SI", "MD", "SYS", "UQP", "UQJ", "UQB", "UQE", "COK", "LIC", "EXD")
    Dim capasOn As String, capasCnt As Long: capasCnt = 0
    Dim capasOff As String: capasOff = ""
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To 15
        If CStr(ws.Cells(18, 2 + i).value) = "X" Then
            capOn(i) = True: capasCnt = capasCnt + 1
            If capasOn <> "" Then capasOn = capasOn & ","
            capasOn = capasOn & capNames(i)
        Else
            If capasOff <> "" Then capasOff = capasOff & ","
            capasOff = capasOff & capNames(i)
        End If
    Next i

    Dim nivOn(0 To 2) As Boolean
    Dim nivOnStr As String: nivOnStr = ""
    Dim nivOffStr As String: nivOffStr = ""
    Dim nivCnt As Long: nivCnt = 0
    For i = 0 To 2
        If CStr(ws.Cells(22, 2 + i * 2).value) = "X" Then
            nivOn(i) = True: nivCnt = nivCnt + 1
            If nivOnStr <> "" Then nivOnStr = nivOnStr & ","
            nivOnStr = nivOnStr & "N" & (i + 1)
        Else
            If nivOffStr <> "" Then nivOffStr = nivOffStr & ","
            nivOffStr = nivOffStr & "N" & (i + 1)
        End If
    Next i

    Dim wQ As Long: wQ = CLng(ws.Range("C25").value)
    Dim ws_ As Long: ws_ = CLng(ws.Range("C26").value)
    Dim wL As Long: wL = CLng(ws.Range("C27").value)
    Dim wR As Long: wR = CLng(ws.Range("C28").value)
    Dim wA As Long: wA = CLng(ws.Range("C29").value)
    Dim wsum As Long: wsum = wQ + ws_ + wL + wR + wA
    Dim capHttp As Long: capHttp = CLng(ws.Range("C33").value)
    Dim capUrl As Long: capUrl = CLng(ws.Range("C34").value)
    Dim maxFb As Long: maxFb = CLng(ws.Range("C35").value)
    Dim chFilter As String: chFilter = CStr(ws.Range("C38").value)

    Dim chCount As Long: chCount = 0
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim wsCh As Worksheet: Set wsCh = ThisWorkbook.sheets("33_CHANNELS_FROM_FRONTEND")
    If Not wsCh Is Nothing Then chCount = wsCh.Cells(wsCh.rows.count, 3).End(xlUp).row - 3
    On Error GoTo 0

    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Dim lrMx As Long: lrMx = wsMx.Cells(wsMx.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    Dim arr As Variant
    arr = wsMx.Range(wsMx.Cells(4, 1), wsMx.Cells(lrMx, 55)).value
    Dim totalRows As Long: totalRows = UBound(arr, 1)

    Dim teoricas As Long, marcadas As Long
    Dim teorPerLvl(0 To 2) As Long, marcPerLvl(0 To 2) As Long
    Dim teorPerCap(0 To 15) As Long, marcPerCap(0 To 15) As Long
    Dim dirsInSubset As Long: dirsInSubset = 0
    Dim r As Long, levNum As Long, layNum As Long

    For r = 1 To totalRows
        Dim dName As String: dName = CStr(arr(r, 2))
        Dim src As String: src = CStr(arr(r, 3))
        Dim catT As String: catT = CStr(arr(r, 4))
        Dim keep As Boolean: keep = False
        Select Case subset
            Case "all_2328": keep = True
            Case "catalogo_1752": keep = (src = "CATALOGO")
            Case "profiles_576": keep = (src = "PROFILES")
            Case "oficiales_only": keep = (src = "CATALOGO" And InStr(catT, "1-Tags") = 0)
            Case Else: keep = True
        End Select
        If exclExp = "si" Then
            If InStr(dName, "#EXT-X-APE-") > 0 Or InStr(LCase(dName), "vnova") > 0 Or Left(dName, 6) = "hlsjs." Then keep = False
        End If
        If Not keep Then GoTo nextRow
        dirsInSubset = dirsInSubset + 1
        For levNum = 0 To 2
            If Not nivOn(levNum) Then GoTo NextLevel
            For layNum = 0 To 15
                If Not capOn(layNum) Then GoTo NextLayer
                teoricas = teoricas + 1
                teorPerLvl(levNum) = teorPerLvl(levNum) + 1
                teorPerCap(layNum) = teorPerCap(layNum) + 1
                If CStr(arr(r, 8 + levNum * 16 + layNum)) = "X" Then
                    marcadas = marcadas + 1
                    marcPerLvl(levNum) = marcPerLvl(levNum) + 1
                    marcPerCap(layNum) = marcPerCap(layNum) + 1
                End If
NextLayer:
            Next layNum
NextLevel:
        Next levNum
nextRow:
    Next r

    Dim totalXMatrix As Long: totalXMatrix = 0
    For r = 1 To totalRows
        For levNum = 0 To 2
            For layNum = 0 To 15
                If CStr(arr(r, 8 + levNum * 16 + layNum)) = "X" Then totalXMatrix = totalXMatrix + 1
            Next layNum
        Next levNum
    Next r
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True

    Dim valid As String: valid = "OK"
    If wsum <> 100 Then valid = "ERROR: suma pesos = " & wsum
    If capasCnt = 0 Then valid = "ERROR: ninguna capa activa"
    If nivCnt = 0 Then valid = "ERROR: ningun nivel activo"
    If chCount = 0 Then valid = "WARNING: hoja 33 vacia"
    If marcadas = 0 Then valid = "ERROR: 0 intersecciones marcadas en scope"

    Dim msg As String
    msg = "=== BRAIN OMEGA - PLAN PREVIEW ===" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf

    msg = msg & "[PANEL 36_BRAIN_SELECTOR]" & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "  Granularidad: " & gran & "  Intervencion: " & interv & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "  Player: " & player & "  Nivel pref: " & lvlPref & "  FB: " & fallback & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "  Subset: " & subset & " -> " & dirsInSubset & " directivas" & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "  Exclude experimentales: " & exclExp & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "  Capas ON: " & capasCnt & "/16 [" & capasOn & "]" & vbCrLf
    If capasOff <> "" Then msg = msg & "  Capas OFF (bloqueadas): [" & capasOff & "]" & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "  Niveles ON: " & nivCnt & "/3 [" & nivOnStr & "]" & vbCrLf
    If nivOffStr <> "" Then msg = msg & "  Niveles OFF (bloqueados): [" & nivOffStr & "]" & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & vbCrLf

    msg = msg & "[INTERSECCIONES]" & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "  TEORICAS (autorizadas por panel):" & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "    " & dirsInSubset & " dirs x " & nivCnt & " niv x " & capasCnt & " capas = " & Format(teoricas, "#,##0") & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "  MARCADAS con X en 8_MATRIX_3D:" & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "    Dentro del scope panel: " & Format(marcadas, "#,##0") & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "    Universo total matrix:  " & Format(totalXMatrix, "#,##0") & " (de 111,744 posibles)" & vbCrLf
    If teoricas > 0 Then
        msg = msg & "  % cobertura real/teorico: " & Format(marcadas / teoricas * 100, "0.00") & "%" & vbCrLf
    End If
    msg = msg & vbCrLf

    msg = msg & "[DESGLOSE POR NIVEL - Teoricas | Marcadas X]" & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "  NIVEL_1 HEADER:   " & Format(teorPerLvl(0), "#,##0") & " | " & Format(marcPerLvl(0), "#,##0") & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "  NIVEL_2 PROFILES: " & Format(teorPerLvl(1), "#,##0") & " | " & Format(marcPerLvl(1), "#,##0") & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "  NIVEL_3 CHANNEL:  " & Format(teorPerLvl(2), "#,##0") & " | " & Format(marcPerLvl(2), "#,##0") & vbCrLf & vbCrLf

    msg = msg & "[DESGLOSE POR CAPA - Teoricas | Marcadas X]" & vbCrLf
    For i = 0 To 15
        If capOn(i) Then
            msg = msg & "  " & capNames(i) & ": " & Format(teorPerCap(i), "#,##0") & " | " & Format(marcPerCap(i), "#,##0") & vbCrLf
        End If
    Next i
    msg = msg & vbCrLf

    msg = msg & "[PESOS] suma=" & wsum & "  Q=" & wQ & " S=" & ws_ & " L=" & wL & " R=" & wR & " A=" & wA & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "[CONSTRAINTS] EXTHTTP<=" & capHttp & " URL<=" & capUrl & " FB=" & maxFb & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "[CANALES] " & chCount & " (filtro: " & chFilter & ")" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf

    msg = msg & "=== REGLA DE CONSISTENCIA ===" & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "Esta hoja 36_BRAIN_SELECTOR es el PRE-REQUISITO del sistema." & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "El BRAIN procesara SOLO las intersecciones marcadas X en 8_MATRIX_3D" & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "QUE ADEMAS esten dentro del scope autorizado aqui." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "- En 8_MATRIX_3D NO se puede marcar X en capas/niveles OFF." & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "- Si intentas marcar X fuera del scope, el sistema lo rechaza." & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "- Cambia primero aqui la config, luego vuelve a la matrix." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf

    msg = msg & "VALIDACION: " & valid

    If valid = "OK" Or Left(valid, 7) = "WARNING" Then
        If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox msg, vbInformation, "BRAIN OMEGA - PREVIEW"
    Else
        If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox msg, vbExclamation, "BRAIN OMEGA - ERROR"
    End If
End Sub

' ============================================================================
' CONSISTENCY GUARD - Impide marcar X en MATRIX fuera del scope del panel
' Llamado desde Workbook_SheetChange(sh,target)
' ============================================================================
Public Function Brain_IsAllowedInPanel(levelIdx As Long, layerIdx As Long) As Boolean
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Brain_IsAllowedInPanel = True: Exit Function

    Dim lvlCell As String
    lvlCell = CStr(ws.Cells(22, 2 + levelIdx * 2).value)
    If lvlCell <> "X" Then Brain_IsAllowedInPanel = False: Exit Function

    Dim capCell As String
    capCell = CStr(ws.Cells(18, 2 + layerIdx).value)
    If capCell <> "X" Then Brain_IsAllowedInPanel = False: Exit Function

    Brain_IsAllowedInPanel = True
End Function

Public Sub Brain_EnforceMatrixScope(target As Range)
    ' Target puede ser multi-celda. Itera.
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim blockedCells As Range, allowedCells As Range
    Dim c As Range

    For Each c In target.Cells
        Dim col As Long: col = c.Column
        Dim row As Long: row = c.row
        ' Solo nos importan H:BC (8-55) y rows >= 4
        If col < 8 Or col > 55 Or row < 4 Then GoTo NextC
        If CStr(c.value) <> "X" Then GoTo NextC

        Dim lvl As Long: lvl = (col - 8) \ 16
        Dim lay As Long: lay = (col - 8) Mod 16

        If Not Brain_IsAllowedInPanel(lvl, lay) Then
            If blockedCells Is Nothing Then
                Set blockedCells = c
            Else
                Set blockedCells = Union(blockedCells, c)
            End If
        End If
NextC:
    Next c

    If Not blockedCells Is Nothing Then
        Application.EnableEvents = False
        blockedCells.value = ""
        blockedCells.Interior.ColorIndex = 3 ' rojo temporal
        Application.EnableEvents = True

        If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "BLOQUEADO por scope del panel." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
               blockedCells.count & " celda(s) borrada(s) porque pertenecen a " & vbCrLf & _
               "CAPAS o NIVELES desactivados en 36_BRAIN_SELECTOR." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
               "Activa primero la capa/nivel en el panel, luego marca aqui.", _
               vbExclamation, "CONSISTENCY GUARD"

        Application.EnableEvents = False
        blockedCells.Interior.ColorIndex = xlNone
        Application.EnableEvents = True
    End If
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.EnableEvents = True
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_ResetSelector()
    If MsgBox("Resetear panel a valores default?", vbYesNo + vbQuestion) = vbNo Then Exit Sub
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR")
    ws.Range("C5").value = "c_ambas"
    ws.Range("C6").value = "a_auto"
    ws.Range("C7").value = "d_universal"
    ws.Range("C8").value = "mixed"
    ws.Range("C9").value = "4-layer"
    ws.Range("C12").value = "all_2328"
    ws.Range("C13").value = "(opcional)"
    ws.Range("C14").value = "no"
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To 15: ws.Cells(18, 2 + i).value = "X": Next i
    For i = 0 To 2: ws.Cells(22, 2 + i * 2).value = "X": Next i
    ws.Range("C25").value = 25
    ws.Range("C26").value = 30
    ws.Range("C27").value = 10
    ws.Range("C28").value = 20
    ws.Range("C29").value = 15
    ws.Range("C33").value = 8192
    ws.Range("C34").value = 8000
    ws.Range("C35").value = 4
    ws.Range("C38").value = "all"
    ws.Range("C39").value = "(opcional)"
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Valores default restaurados.", vbInformation, "RESET"
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_SaveSelectorPreset()
    Dim nm As String: nm = InputBox("Nombre del preset (ej: premium_tivimate_v1):", "GUARDAR PRESET")
    If nm = "" Then Exit Sub
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Preset '" & nm & "' se guardaria en futura hoja 39_PRESETS." & vbCrLf & _
           "Funcionalidad completa en Fase O4.", vbInformation, "PRESETS"
End Sub


Public Sub Brain_ColorMatrix_Omega()
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.EnableEvents = False

    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_MATRIX3D)
    Dim lR As Long: lR = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    If lR < 4 Then Exit Sub

    ' Headers colors
    With ws.Range("A3:G3")
        .Interior.Color = RGB(68, 84, 106)
        .Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
        .Font.Bold = True
        .HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    End With
    With ws.Range("H3:W3")
        .Interior.Color = RGB(68, 114, 196)
        .Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
        .Font.Bold = True
        .HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    End With
    With ws.Range("X3:AM3")
        .Interior.Color = RGB(91, 155, 213)
        .Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
        .Font.Bold = True
        .HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    End With
    With ws.Range("AN3:BC3")
        .Interior.Color = RGB(155, 194, 230)
        .Font.Color = RGB(0, 0, 0)
        .Font.Bold = True
        .HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    End With

    ' Banner row 2 - re-merge
    On Error Resume Next
    ws.Range("H2:W2").UnMerge
    ws.Range("X2:AM2").UnMerge
    ws.Range("AN2:BC2").UnMerge
    On Error GoTo 0

    ws.Range("H2:W2").Merge
    ws.Range("H2:W2").value = "NIVEL 1: HEADER"
    ws.Range("H2:W2").Interior.Color = RGB(68, 114, 196)
    ws.Range("H2:W2").Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    ws.Range("H2:W2").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("H2:W2").HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter

    ws.Range("X2:AM2").Merge
    ws.Range("X2:AM2").value = "NIVEL 2: PROFILES"
    ws.Range("X2:AM2").Interior.Color = RGB(91, 155, 213)
    ws.Range("X2:AM2").Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    ws.Range("X2:AM2").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("X2:AM2").HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter

    ws.Range("AN2:BC2").Merge
    ws.Range("AN2:BC2").value = "NIVEL 3: CHANNEL"
    ws.Range("AN2:BC2").Interior.Color = RGB(155, 194, 230)
    ws.Range("AN2:BC2").Font.Color = RGB(0, 0, 0)
    ws.Range("AN2:BC2").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("AN2:BC2").HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter

    ' Leer el bloque 4:lr cols H:BC como array, pintar cada "X"
    Dim dataRng As Range
    Set dataRng = ws.Range(ws.Cells(4, 8), ws.Cells(lR, 55))
    Dim arr As Variant: arr = dataRng.value
    Dim rows_ As Long: rows_ = UBound(arr, 1)
    Dim cols_ As Long: cols_ = UBound(arr, 2)

    Dim i As Long, j As Long
    Dim c As Range

    ' Pintar por nivel - usar FormatConditions para mejor performance
    dataRng.FormatConditions.Delete

    Dim rng1 As Range: Set rng1 = ws.Range(ws.Cells(4, 8), ws.Cells(lR, 23))    ' H:W NIVEL_1
    Dim rng2 As Range: Set rng2 = ws.Range(ws.Cells(4, 24), ws.Cells(lR, 39))   ' X:AM NIVEL_2
    Dim rng3 As Range: Set rng3 = ws.Range(ws.Cells(4, 40), ws.Cells(lR, 55))   ' AN:BC NIVEL_3

    rng1.FormatConditions.Delete
    rng2.FormatConditions.Delete
    rng3.FormatConditions.Delete

    With rng1.FormatConditions.Add(Type:=xlTextString, String:="X", TextOperator:=xlContains)
        .Interior.Color = RGB(217, 225, 242)
        .Font.Color = RGB(0, 32, 96)
        .Font.Bold = True
    End With
    With rng2.FormatConditions.Add(Type:=xlTextString, String:="X", TextOperator:=xlContains)
        .Interior.Color = RGB(226, 239, 218)
        .Font.Color = RGB(56, 87, 35)
        .Font.Bold = True
    End With
    With rng3.FormatConditions.Add(Type:=xlTextString, String:="X", TextOperator:=xlContains)
        .Interior.Color = RGB(255, 242, 204)
        .Font.Color = RGB(128, 96, 0)
        .Font.Bold = True
    End With

    ' Col widths
    ws.Columns("A").ColumnWidth = 6
    ws.Columns("B").ColumnWidth = 40
    ws.Columns("C").ColumnWidth = 11
    ws.Columns("D").ColumnWidth = 20
    ws.Columns("E").ColumnWidth = 7
    ws.Columns("F").ColumnWidth = 9
    ws.Columns("G").ColumnWidth = 12
    ws.Columns("H:BC").ColumnWidth = 5
    ws.rows(2).RowHeight = 22
    ws.rows(3).RowHeight = 32
    ws.Range("A3:BC3").WrapText = True

    ' Freeze panes
    ws.Activate
    ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = False
    ws.Cells(4, 2).Select
    ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = True

    ' Autofilter en row 3
    ws.Range("A3:BC3").AutoFilter

    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True

    ThisWorkbook.Save
End Sub


Public Sub Brain_BuildMatrix_Omega_Silent()
    Dim origDisplay As Variant: origDisplay = Application.DisplayAlerts
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    On Error Resume Next
    Brain_BuildMatrix_Omega_Core
    Application.DisplayAlerts = origDisplay
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_BuildMatrix_Omega_Core()
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.EnableEvents = False

    Dim wsCat As Worksheet: Set wsCat = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_CATALOGO)
    Dim wsProf As Worksheet: Set wsProf = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_PROFILES)
    Dim wsMx As Worksheet: Set wsMx = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_MATRIX3D)

    Dim dict As Object: Set dict = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim lR As Long, r As Long
    lR = wsCat.Cells(wsCat.rows.count, 3).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 4 To lR
        Dim nm As String: nm = Trim(CStr(wsCat.Cells(r, 3).value))
        Dim catT As String: catT = Trim(CStr(wsCat.Cells(r, 2).value))
        If nm <> "" And Not dict.Exists(nm) Then
            dict.Add nm, "CATALOGO|" & catT
        End If
    Next r

    lR = wsProf.Cells(wsProf.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
    Dim skipList As String: skipList = "|Atributo|-- META --|-- SETTINGS --|-- VLCOPT --|-- KODIPROP --|-- HEADEROVERRIDES --|-- ENABLEDCATEGORIES --|-- HLSJS --|-- PREFETCH_CONFIG --|-- OTHER --|"
    For r = 4 To lR
        Dim nm2 As String: nm2 = Trim(CStr(wsProf.Cells(r, 1).value))
        If nm2 <> "" And InStr(skipList, "|" & nm2 & "|") = 0 Then
            If Not dict.Exists(nm2) Then
                Dim prefix As String
                If InStr(nm2, ".") > 0 Then prefix = Split(nm2, ".")(0) Else prefix = "other"
                dict.Add nm2, "PROFILES|" & prefix
            End If
        End If
    Next r

    Dim totalDirs As Long: totalDirs = dict.count
    wsMx.Cells.Clear

    wsMx.Cells(1, 1).value = "MATRIX_3D OMEGA v1: " & totalDirs & " directivas x 3 niveles x 16 capas = " & (totalDirs * 3 * 16) & " intersecciones"
    wsMx.Cells(1, 1).Font.Bold = True

    wsMx.Cells(2, 8).value = "NIVEL 1: HEADER"
    wsMx.Cells(2, 24).value = "NIVEL 2: PROFILES"
    wsMx.Cells(2, 40).value = "NIVEL 3: CHANNEL"

    Dim hdrs As Variant
    hdrs = Array("#", "directive_name", "source", "category", "default_level", "bytes_cost", "player_compat")
    Dim layers As Variant
    layers = Array("VLC", "KOD", "HTT", "EAU", "EI", "EM", "SI", "MD", "SYS", "UQP", "UQJ", "UQB", "UQE", "COK", "LIC", "EXD")

    Dim c As Long
    For c = 0 To UBound(hdrs)
        wsMx.Cells(3, c + 1).value = hdrs(c)
        wsMx.Cells(3, c + 1).Font.Bold = True
    Next c

    Dim lvl As Long, layerIdx As Long, baseCol As Long
    For lvl = 0 To 2
        baseCol = 8 + lvl * 16
        For layerIdx = 0 To 15
            wsMx.Cells(3, baseCol + layerIdx).value = layers(layerIdx)
            wsMx.Cells(3, baseCol + layerIdx).Font.Bold = True
        Next layerIdx
    Next lvl

    Dim nCols As Long: nCols = 7 + 48
    Dim arr() As Variant: ReDim arr(1 To totalDirs, 1 To nCols)

    Dim keys As Variant: keys = dict.keys
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To UBound(keys)
        Dim dirName As String: dirName = CStr(keys(i))
        Dim parts() As String: parts = Split(CStr(dict(dirName)), "|")
        Dim src As String: src = parts(0)
        Dim ct As String: ct = parts(1)

        arr(i + 1, 1) = i + 1
        arr(i + 1, 2) = dirName
        arr(i + 1, 3) = src
        arr(i + 1, 4) = ct
        arr(i + 1, 5) = FE_InferLevel(dirName, ct)
        arr(i + 1, 6) = Len(dirName) + 20
        arr(i + 1, 7) = FE_InferPlayerCompat(dirName, ct)

        Dim applicMask As String: applicMask = FE_InferApplicability(dirName, ct)
        Dim levNum As Long, layNum As Long, cellCol As Long
        For levNum = 0 To 2
            For layNum = 0 To 15
                cellCol = 8 + levNum * 16 + layNum
                If Mid(applicMask, levNum * 16 + layNum + 1, 1) = "1" Then
                    arr(i + 1, cellCol) = "X"
                End If
            Next layNum
        Next levNum
    Next i

    wsMx.Range(wsMx.Cells(4, 1), wsMx.Cells(3 + totalDirs, nCols)).value = arr

    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    ThisWorkbook.Save
End Sub


' ============================================================================
' OMEGA O1 — Brain_BuildMatrix_Omega
' Construye 8_MATRIX_3D con 2,330 directivas x 3 niveles x 16 capas
' ============================================================================
Public Sub Brain_BuildMatrix_Omega()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.EnableEvents = False

    Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer
    Dim wsCat As Worksheet: Set wsCat = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_CATALOGO)
    Dim wsProf As Worksheet: Set wsProf = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_PROFILES)
    Dim wsMx As Worksheet: Set wsMx = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_MATRIX3D)

    Dim dict As Object: Set dict = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim lR As Long, r As Long
    lR = wsCat.Cells(wsCat.rows.count, 3).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 4 To lR
        Dim nm As String: nm = Trim(CStr(wsCat.Cells(r, 3).value))
        Dim cat As String: cat = Trim(CStr(wsCat.Cells(r, 2).value))
        If nm <> "" And Not dict.Exists(nm) Then
            dict.Add nm, "CATALOGO|" & cat
        End If
    Next r
    Dim catCount As Long: catCount = dict.count

    lR = wsProf.Cells(wsProf.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
    Dim skipList As String: skipList = "|Atributo|-- META --|-- SETTINGS --|-- VLCOPT --|-- KODIPROP --|-- HEADEROVERRIDES --|-- ENABLEDCATEGORIES --|-- HLSJS --|-- PREFETCH_CONFIG --|-- OTHER --|"
    For r = 4 To lR
        Dim nm2 As String: nm2 = Trim(CStr(wsProf.Cells(r, 1).value))
        If nm2 <> "" And InStr(skipList, "|" & nm2 & "|") = 0 Then
            If Not dict.Exists(nm2) Then
                Dim prefix As String
                If InStr(nm2, ".") > 0 Then prefix = Split(nm2, ".")(0) Else prefix = "other"
                dict.Add nm2, "PROFILES|" & prefix
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    Dim totalDirs As Long: totalDirs = dict.count

    wsMx.Cells.Clear

    wsMx.Cells(1, 1).value = "MATRIX_3D OMEGA v1: " & totalDirs & " directivas x 3 niveles x 16 capas = " & (totalDirs * 3 * 16) & " intersecciones"
    wsMx.Cells(1, 1).Font.Bold = True
    wsMx.Cells(1, 1).Font.size = 12

    wsMx.Cells(2, 8).value = "NIVEL 1: HEADER"
    wsMx.Cells(2, 24).value = "NIVEL 2: PROFILES"
    wsMx.Cells(2, 40).value = "NIVEL 3: CHANNEL"
    wsMx.Range("H2:W2").Merge
    wsMx.Range("X2:AM2").Merge
    wsMx.Range("AN2:BC2").Merge
    Dim mergeRng As Range
    For Each mergeRng In Array(wsMx.Range("H2:W2"), wsMx.Range("X2:AM2"), wsMx.Range("AN2:BC2"))
        mergeRng.Interior.Color = RGB(31, 78, 120)
        mergeRng.Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
        mergeRng.Font.Bold = True
        mergeRng.HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
    Next mergeRng

    Dim hdrs As Variant
    hdrs = Array("#", "directive_name", "source", "category", "default_level", "bytes_cost", "player_compat")
    Dim layers As Variant
    layers = Array("VLC", "KOD", "HTT", "EAU", "EI", "EM", "SI", "MD", "SYS", "UQP", "UQJ", "UQB", "UQE", "COK", "LIC", "EXD")

    Dim c As Long
    For c = 0 To UBound(hdrs)
        With wsMx.Cells(3, c + 1)
            .value = hdrs(c)
            .Font.Bold = True
            .Interior.Color = RGB(68, 114, 196)
            .Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
            .HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
        End With
    Next c

    Dim lvl As Long, layerIdx As Long, baseCol As Long
    For lvl = 0 To 2
        baseCol = 8 + lvl * 16
        For layerIdx = 0 To 15
            With wsMx.Cells(3, baseCol + layerIdx)
                .value = layers(layerIdx)
                .Font.Bold = True
                .Interior.Color = IIf(lvl = 0, RGB(68, 114, 196), IIf(lvl = 1, RGB(91, 155, 213), RGB(155, 194, 230)))
                .Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
                .HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
            End With
        Next layerIdx
    Next lvl

    Dim nCols As Long: nCols = 7 + 48
    Dim arr() As Variant: ReDim arr(1 To totalDirs, 1 To nCols)

    Dim keys As Variant: keys = dict.keys
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To UBound(keys)
        Dim dirName As String: dirName = CStr(keys(i))
        Dim parts() As String: parts = Split(CStr(dict(dirName)), "|")
        Dim src As String: src = parts(0)
        Dim ct As String: ct = parts(1)

        arr(i + 1, 1) = i + 1
        arr(i + 1, 2) = dirName
        arr(i + 1, 3) = src
        arr(i + 1, 4) = ct
        arr(i + 1, 5) = FE_InferLevel(dirName, ct)
        arr(i + 1, 6) = Len(dirName) + 20
        arr(i + 1, 7) = FE_InferPlayerCompat(dirName, ct)

        Dim applicMask As String: applicMask = FE_InferApplicability(dirName, ct)
        Dim levNum As Long, layNum As Long, cellCol As Long
        For levNum = 0 To 2
            For layNum = 0 To 15
                cellCol = 8 + levNum * 16 + layNum
                Dim maskIdx As Long: maskIdx = levNum * 16 + layNum + 1
                If Mid(applicMask, maskIdx, 1) = "1" Then
                    arr(i + 1, cellCol) = "X"
                Else
                    arr(i + 1, cellCol) = ""
                End If
            Next layNum
        Next levNum
    Next i

    wsMx.Range(wsMx.Cells(4, 1), wsMx.Cells(3 + totalDirs, nCols)).value = arr

    wsMx.Columns("B:B").ColumnWidth = 40
    wsMx.Columns("C:G").ColumnWidth = 14
    wsMx.Columns("H:BC").ColumnWidth = 5
    wsMx.rows(3).RowHeight = 24
    wsMx.Range("A3:BC3").WrapText = True
    wsMx.Activate
    ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = False
    wsMx.Cells(4, 2).Select
    ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = True

    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True

    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "MATRIX OMEGA BUILT OK" & vbCrLf & _
           "Directivas: " & totalDirs & vbCrLf & _
           "  - CATALOGO: " & catCount & vbCrLf & _
           "  - PROFILES: " & (totalDirs - catCount) & vbCrLf & _
           "Niveles: 3 (HEADER/PROFILES/CHANNEL)" & vbCrLf & _
           "Capas: 16" & vbCrLf & _
           "Intersecciones: " & (totalDirs * 3 * 16) & vbCrLf & _
           "Tiempo: " & Round(Timer - t0, 1) & "s", vbInformation, "BRAIN OMEGA O1"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "ERROR " & Err.Number & ": " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub

Private Function FE_InferLevel(dirName As String, category As String) As Long
    Dim d As String: d = LCase(dirName)
    Dim c As String: c = LCase(category)
    If Left(d, 9) = "#extm3u" Or InStr(c, "2-extm3u") > 0 Then FE_InferLevel = 1: Exit Function
    If InStr(d, "stream-inf") > 0 Or InStr(c, "stream-inf") > 0 Or Left(d, 9) = "settings." Then FE_InferLevel = 2: Exit Function
    If Left(d, 7) = "vlcopt." Or Left(d, 9) = "kodiprop." Or Left(d, 16) = "headeroverrides." Then FE_InferLevel = 3: Exit Function
    If Left(d, 8) = "#extinf" Or Left(d, 11) = "#extvlcopt" Or Left(d, 10) = "#kodiprop" Or Left(d, 10) = "#exthttp" Then FE_InferLevel = 3: Exit Function
    If InStr(d, "#ext-x-sys-") > 0 Then FE_InferLevel = 1: Exit Function
    If Left(d, 6) = "#ext-x" Then FE_InferLevel = 1: Exit Function
    FE_InferLevel = 3
End Function

Private Function FE_InferPlayerCompat(dirName As String, category As String) As String
    Dim d As String: d = LCase(dirName)
    If Left(d, 7) = "vlcopt." Then FE_InferPlayerCompat = "00100": Exit Function
    If Left(d, 9) = "kodiprop." Then FE_InferPlayerCompat = "01000": Exit Function
    If Left(d, 6) = "hlsjs." Then FE_InferPlayerCompat = "00001": Exit Function
    If Left(d, 16) = "headeroverrides." Then FE_InferPlayerCompat = "11111": Exit Function
    If Left(d, 11) = "#extvlcopt" Then FE_InferPlayerCompat = "00100": Exit Function
    If Left(d, 10) = "#kodiprop" Then FE_InferPlayerCompat = "01000": Exit Function
    FE_InferPlayerCompat = "11111"
End Function

Private Function FE_InferApplicability(dirName As String, category As String) As String
    Dim m(0 To 47) As String
    Dim i As Long: For i = 0 To 47: m(i) = "0": Next i
    Dim d As String: d = LCase(dirName)
    Dim c As String: c = LCase(category)

    If Left(d, 9) = "#extm3u" Then m(5) = "1"
    If InStr(d, "#ext-x-sys") > 0 Then m(8) = "1"
    If Left(d, 11) = "#ext-x-version" Or Left(d, 24) = "#ext-x-independent-seg" Then m(15) = "1"
    If InStr(c, "2-extm3u") > 0 Then m(5) = "1"

    If InStr(d, "stream-inf") > 0 Or InStr(c, "stream-inf") > 0 Then m(16 + 6) = "1"
    If Left(d, 9) = "settings." Then m(16 + 6) = "1"
    If InStr(d, "#ext-x-media") > 0 Then m(16 + 7) = "1"
    If InStr(d, "#ext-x-audio") > 0 Then m(16 + 3) = "1"

    If Left(d, 7) = "vlcopt." Or Left(d, 11) = "#extvlcopt" Then m(32 + 0) = "1"
    If Left(d, 9) = "kodiprop." Or Left(d, 10) = "#kodiprop" Then m(32 + 1) = "1"
    If Left(d, 16) = "headeroverrides." Then m(32 + 2) = "1": m(32 + 13) = "1"
    If Left(d, 10) = "#exthttp" Or InStr(c, "9-exthttp") > 0 Then m(32 + 2) = "1"
    If Left(d, 8) = "#extinf" Or InStr(c, "3-extinf") > 0 Then m(32 + 4) = "1"
    If InStr(d, "#ext-x-key") > 0 Or InStr(d, "#ext-x-session-key") > 0 Then m(32 + 14) = "1"

    If Left(d, 6) = "hlsjs." Then m(15) = "1": m(16 + 15) = "1": m(32 + 15) = "1"
    If Left(d, 16) = "prefetch_config." Then m(15) = "1"

    Dim hasAny As Boolean: hasAny = False
    For i = 0 To 47
        If m(i) = "1" Then hasAny = True: Exit For
    Next i
    If Not hasAny Then
        Dim lvl As Long: lvl = FE_InferLevel(dirName, category)
        m((lvl - 1) * 16 + 15) = "1"
    End If

    Dim result As String: result = ""
    For i = 0 To 47
        result = result & m(i)
    Next i
    FE_InferApplicability = result
End Function


' ============================================================================
' UTILIDADES: LECTURA CONFIG Y PROFILES
' ============================================================================
Public Function ConfigVal(paramName As String) As String
    Dim ws As Worksheet, r As Long, lastRow As Long
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_CONFIG)
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 3 To lastRow
        If LCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 2).value)) = LCase(paramName) Then
            ConfigVal = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 3).value): Exit Function
        End If
    Next r
    ConfigVal = ""
End Function

Public Function ProfileVal(profileId As String, attrName As String) As String
    Dim ws As Worksheet, r As Long, lastRow As Long, colIdx As Integer
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_PROFILES)
    Select Case UCase(profileId)
        Case "P0": colIdx = 2
        Case "P1": colIdx = 3
        Case "P2": colIdx = 4
        Case "P3": colIdx = 5
        Case "P4": colIdx = 6
        Case "P5": colIdx = 7
        Case Else: colIdx = 2
    End Select
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 3 To lastRow
        If LCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value)) = LCase(attrName) Or _
           InStr(LCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value)), LCase(attrName)) > 0 Then
            ProfileVal = CStr(ws.Cells(r, colIdx).value): Exit Function
        End If
    Next r
    ProfileVal = ""
End Function

Public Sub SetConfigVal(paramName As String, newVal As String)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_CONFIG)
    Dim r As Long, lastRow As Long
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 3 To lastRow
        If LCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 2).value)) = LCase(paramName) Then
            ws.Cells(r, 3).value = newVal: Exit Sub
        End If
    Next r
    r = lastRow + 1
    ws.Cells(r, 2).value = paramName
    ws.Cells(r, 3).value = newVal
End Sub

' ============================================================================
' RESOLVER POLIMORFICO DE PLACEHOLDERS
' ============================================================================
Public Function ResolvePlaceholder(value As String) As String
    Dim result As String: result = value
    Dim activeProfile As String: activeProfile = ConfigVal("Default_Profile")
    If activeProfile = "" Then activeProfile = "P1"
    result = Replace(result, "{config.user_agent}", ConfigVal("UA_Default"))
    result = Replace(result, "{config.referer}", ConfigVal("Referer_Default"))
    result = Replace(result, "{config.origin}", ConfigVal("Origin_Default"))
    result = Replace(result, "{config.connection}", ConfigVal("Connection_Default"))
    result = Replace(result, "{config.keep_alive}", ConfigVal("Keep_Alive_Default"))
    result = Replace(result, "{config.locale}", ConfigVal("Client_Locale"))
    result = Replace(result, "{config.timezone}", ConfigVal("Client_Timezone"))
    result = Replace(result, "{config.bandwidth_mbps}", ConfigVal("Client_Bandwidth_Mbps"))
    result = Replace(result, "{config.latency_ms}", ConfigVal("Client_Latency_Ms"))
    result = Replace(result, "{config.profile_default}", activeProfile)
    result = Replace(result, "{profile.resolution}", ProfileVal(activeProfile, "maxResolution"))
    result = Replace(result, "{profile.fps}", ProfileVal(activeProfile, "targetFps"))
    result = Replace(result, "{profile.codecs}", ProfileVal(activeProfile, "codecs"))
    Dim bwKbps As String: bwKbps = ProfileVal(activeProfile, "maxBitrateKbps")
    If isNumeric(bwKbps) Then
        result = Replace(result, "{profile.bandwidth}", CStr(CLng(bwKbps) * 1000))
        result = Replace(result, "{profile.max_bandwidth}", CStr(CLng(bwKbps) * 1000))
    End If
    Dim bufS As String: bufS = ProfileVal(activeProfile, "bufferTargetSec")
    If isNumeric(bufS) Then
        result = Replace(result, "{profile.buffer_s}", bufS)
        result = Replace(result, "{profile.buffer_ms}", CStr(CLng(bufS) * 1000))
    End If
    Dim resStr As String: resStr = ProfileVal(activeProfile, "maxResolution")
    If InStr(resStr, "x") > 0 Then
        Dim parts() As String: parts = Split(resStr, "x")
        result = Replace(result, "{profile.max_width}", parts(0))
        result = Replace(result, "{profile.max_height}", parts(1))
    End If
    Dim servWs As Worksheet: Set servWs = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_SERVERS)
    Dim sR As Long
    For sR = 4 To servWs.Cells(servWs.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
        If UCase(CStr(servWs.Cells(sR, 1).value)) = "X" Then
            result = Replace(result, "{server.url}", CStr(servWs.Cells(sR, 3).value))
            result = Replace(result, "{server.username}", CStr(servWs.Cells(sR, 4).value))
            result = Replace(result, "{server.password}", CStr(servWs.Cells(sR, 5).value))
            Exit For
        End If
    Next sR
    If InStr(result, "{evasion.random_ua}") > 0 Then result = Replace(result, "{evasion.random_ua}", RotateUA())
    If InStr(result, "{evasion.random_referer}") > 0 Then result = Replace(result, "{evasion.random_referer}", RotateReferer())
    result = ResolvePrismaPlaceholders(result, activeProfile)
    ResolvePlaceholder = result
End Function

' ============================================================================
' EVASION
' ============================================================================
Public Function RotateUA() As String
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_EVASION)
    Dim uas() As String, weights() As Double, totalW As Double
    Dim r As Long, cnt As Long: cnt = 0
    ReDim uas(100): ReDim weights(100)
    For r = 6 To 50
        If UCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value)) = "X" Then
            uas(cnt) = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 5).value)
            weights(cnt) = CDbl(val(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 4).value)))
            totalW = totalW + weights(cnt)
            cnt = cnt + 1
        End If
    Next r
    If cnt = 0 Then RotateUA = ConfigVal("UA_Default"): Exit Function
    Dim rndVal As Double: rndVal = Rnd() * totalW
    Dim accum As Double: accum = 0
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To cnt - 1
        accum = accum + weights(i)
        If rndVal <= accum Then RotateUA = uas(i): Exit Function
    Next i
    RotateUA = uas(0)
End Function

Public Function RotateReferer() As String
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_EVASION)
    Dim r As Long, refs() As String, cnt As Long: cnt = 0
    ReDim refs(100)
    For r = 10 To ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
        Dim cellVal As String: cellVal = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 3).value)
        If UCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value)) = "X" And Left(LCase(cellVal), 4) = "http" Then
            refs(cnt) = cellVal: cnt = cnt + 1
        End If
    Next r
    If cnt = 0 Then RotateReferer = ConfigVal("Referer_Default"): Exit Function
    RotateReferer = refs(Int(Rnd() * cnt))
End Function

' ============================================================================
' BRAIN: CALCULADORAS MATEMATICAS
' ============================================================================
Public Function Brain_CalcStreamingHealth() As Double
    Dim bufStab As Double, bwHead As Double, codecSup As Double
    Dim abrMat As Double, errTree As Double, netLat As Double
    Dim activeProfile As String: activeProfile = ConfigVal("Default_Profile")
    If activeProfile = "" Then activeProfile = "P1"
    Dim bufS As Double: bufS = val(ProfileVal(activeProfile, "bufferTargetSec"))
    If bufS <= 0 Then bufS = 10
    bufStab = WorksheetFunction.Min(1#, bufS / 30#)
    Dim clientMbps As Double: clientMbps = val(ConfigVal("Client_Bandwidth_Mbps"))
    If clientMbps <= 0 Then clientMbps = 100
    Dim profileMbps As Double: profileMbps = val(ProfileVal(activeProfile, "maxBitrateKbps")) / 1000#
    If profileMbps <= 0 Then profileMbps = 10
    bwHead = WorksheetFunction.max(0#, WorksheetFunction.Min(1#, (clientMbps - profileMbps) / clientMbps))
    Dim codecStr As String: codecStr = ConfigVal("Default_Video_Codecs")
    If codecStr = "" Then codecStr = "hevc,h264,av1,vp9"
    codecSup = WorksheetFunction.Min(1#, (UBound(Split(codecStr, ",")) + 1) / 4#)
    abrMat = CDbl(IIf(Len(ConfigVal("ABR_Maturity_Factor")) > 0, ConfigVal("ABR_Maturity_Factor"), "1.0")): errTree = CDbl(IIf(Len(ConfigVal("Error_Tree_Coverage")) > 0, ConfigVal("Error_Tree_Coverage"), "1.0"))
    Dim latMs As Double: latMs = val(ConfigVal("Client_Latency_Ms"))
    If latMs <= 0 Then latMs = 100
    netLat = WorksheetFunction.max(0#, 1# - latMs / 500#)
    Brain_CalcStreamingHealth = (25 * bufStab + 20 * bwHead + 10 * codecSup + 15 * abrMat + 20 * errTree + 10 * netLat) * (0.5 + 0.5 * Brain_GetOmegaCompliance())
End Function

Public Function Brain_CalcRiskScore() As Double
    Dim risk As Double: risk = 100 - Brain_CalcStreamingHealth()
    If InStr(LCase(ConfigVal("Connection_Default")), "close") > 0 Then risk = risk + 15
    Dim ref As String: ref = LCase(ConfigVal("Referer_Default"))
    If InStr(ref, "duckdns") > 0 Or InStr(ref, "iptv-ape") > 0 Then risk = risk + 10
    If InStr(UCase(ConfigVal("UA_Default")), "APE-NAVIGATOR") > 0 Then risk = risk + 10
    Brain_CalcRiskScore = WorksheetFunction.max(0#, WorksheetFunction.Min(100#, risk))
End Function

Public Function Brain_CalcHeadroom() As Double
    Dim clientMbps As Double: clientMbps = val(ConfigVal("Client_Bandwidth_Mbps"))
    Dim activeProfile As String: activeProfile = ConfigVal("Default_Profile")
    If activeProfile = "" Then activeProfile = "P1"
    Dim profileMbps As Double: profileMbps = val(ProfileVal(activeProfile, "maxBitrateKbps")) / 1000#
    If profileMbps <= 0 Then profileMbps = 1
    Brain_CalcHeadroom = (clientMbps - profileMbps) / profileMbps * 100 * 0.85
End Function

Public Function Brain_PredictStallRate() As Double
    Dim latMs As Double: latMs = val(ConfigVal("Client_Latency_Ms"))
    Dim envMod As Double: envMod = 1 + (latMs / 1000#)
    Dim activeProfile As String: activeProfile = ConfigVal("Default_Profile")
    If activeProfile = "" Then activeProfile = "P1"
    Dim bufS As Double: bufS = val(ProfileVal(activeProfile, "bufferTargetSec"))
    Dim bufMod As Double: bufMod = 1 - WorksheetFunction.Min(0.95, bufS / 60#)
    Dim bwMod As Double: bwMod = WorksheetFunction.max(0.05, 1 - Brain_CalcHeadroom() / 200#)
    Dim rate As Double: rate = 0.05 * envMod * bufMod * 0.55 * bwMod * 0.1
    Brain_PredictStallRate = WorksheetFunction.max(0.0001, WorksheetFunction.Min(0.5, rate)) * 100
End Function

Public Sub Brain_RecalcAll()
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_BRAIN)
    ws.Cells(5, 2).value = Round(Brain_CalcStreamingHealth(), 0)
    ws.Cells(6, 2).value = Round(Brain_CalcRiskScore(), 0)
    ws.Cells(7, 2).value = Round(Brain_CalcHeadroom(), 0)
    ws.Cells(8, 2).value = Round(Brain_PredictStallRate(), 2)
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Dashboard recalculado" & vbCrLf & "Health: " & ws.Cells(5, 2).value & "/100" & vbCrLf & _
           "Risk: " & ws.Cells(6, 2).value & "/100" & vbCrLf & "Headroom: " & ws.Cells(7, 2).value & "%" & vbCrLf & _
           "Stall: " & ws.Cells(8, 2).value & "%", vbInformation, "BRAIN"
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_RecalcAllSilent()
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_BRAIN)
    ws.Cells(5, 2).value = Round(Brain_CalcStreamingHealth(), 0)
    ws.Cells(6, 2).value = Round(Brain_CalcRiskScore(), 0)
