    ws.Cells(7, 2).value = Round(Brain_CalcHeadroom(), 0)
    ws.Cells(8, 2).value = Round(Brain_PredictStallRate(), 2)
    ' Application.ScreenUpdating = True  '' DISABLED anti-freeze: caller gestiona el flag
    Application.StatusBar = "Health=" & ws.Cells(5, 2).value & " Risk=" & ws.Cells(6, 2).value & _
        " Head=" & ws.Cells(7, 2).value & "%" & " Stall=" & ws.Cells(8, 2).value & "%"
End Sub

' ============================================================================
' BRAIN: HOMOLOGATION / CONTRADICTIONS / VALIDATE
' ============================================================================
Private Function StartsWith(s As String, prefix As String) As Boolean
    StartsWith = (Left(s, Len(prefix)) = prefix)
End Function

Private Function GetNivel3Value(capa As String, keyName As String) As String
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_NIVEL3)
    Dim r As Long, lastRow As Long
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 3).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 6 To lastRow
        If UCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value)) = "X" Then
            If InStr(UCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 2).value)), UCase(capa)) > 0 Then
                If InStr(LCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 3).value)), LCase(keyName)) > 0 Then
                    GetNivel3Value = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 4).value): Exit Function
                End If
            End If
        End If
    Next r
End Function

Private Function GetNivel1Value(keyName As String) As String
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_NIVEL1)
    Dim r As Long, lastRow As Long
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 6 To lastRow
        If InStr(UCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 2).value)), UCase(keyName)) > 0 Then
            GetNivel1Value = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 3).value): Exit Function
        End If
    Next r
End Function

Public Sub Brain_DetectContradictions()
    Dim contradictions As Long: contradictions = 0
    Dim report As String: report = "CONTRADICCIONES:" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf
    Dim uaVLC As String: uaVLC = GetNivel3Value("EXTVLCOPT", "http-user-agent")
    Dim uaHTT As String: uaHTT = GetNivel3Value("EXTHTTP", "User-Agent")
    If uaVLC <> "" And uaHTT <> "" Then
        If ResolvePlaceholder(uaVLC) <> ResolvePlaceholder(uaHTT) Then
            report = report & "- UA: VLC vs EXTHTTP distintos" & vbCrLf
            contradictions = contradictions + 1
        End If
    End If
    Dim connAny As String: connAny = GetNivel3Value("EXTHTTP", "Connection")
    If InStr(LCase(ResolvePlaceholder(connAny)), "close") > 0 Then
        report = report & "- Connection='close' (rompe OkHttp)" & vbCrLf
        contradictions = contradictions + 1
    End If
    If contradictions = 0 Then MsgBox "Sin contradicciones.", vbInformation Else MsgBox report & vbCrLf & "Total: " & contradictions, vbExclamation
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_ValidateValues()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_NIVEL3)
    Dim r As Long, lastRow As Long, errCount As Long: errCount = 0
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 3).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 6 To lastRow
        If UCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value)) = "X" Then
            Dim val As String: val = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 4).value)
            Dim tag As String: tag = LCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 3).value))
            Dim issue As String: issue = ""
            If InStr(tag, "connection") > 0 And InStr(LCase(val), "close") > 0 Then issue = "Connection close rompe OkHttp"
            If InStr(tag, "user-agent") > 0 And InStr(UCase(ResolvePlaceholder(val)), "APE-NAVIGATOR") > 0 Then issue = "UA fabricado"
            If issue <> "" Then
                ws.Cells(r, 4).Interior.Color = RGB(255, 199, 206)
                ws.Cells(r, 6).value = "WARN: " & issue
                errCount = errCount + 1
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Validacion: " & errCount & " issues", vbInformation
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_SuggestOptimal()
    Dim s As String: s = "SUGERENCIAS:" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf
    Dim c As Long: c = 0
    If InStr(UCase(ConfigVal("UA_Default")), "APE-NAVIGATOR") > 0 Then
        s = s & "- UA contiene APE-NAVIGATOR, cambiar" & vbCrLf: c = c + 1
    End If
    If InStr(LCase(ConfigVal("Referer_Default")), "duckdns") > 0 Then
        s = s & "- Referer apunta a duckdns, cambiar" & vbCrLf: c = c + 1
    End If
    Dim head As Double: head = Brain_CalcHeadroom()
    If head < 50 Then s = s & "- Headroom bajo (" & Round(head, 0) & "%), baja perfil" & vbCrLf: c = c + 1
    If c = 0 Then MsgBox "Config optima", vbInformation Else MsgBox s, vbInformation
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_OptimizerLoop()
    Dim bestHealth As Double, bestProfile As String, bestBuffer As Long
    bestHealth = 0: bestBuffer = 30: bestProfile = "P3"
    Dim profiles() As String: profiles = Split("P0,P1,P2,P3,P4,P5", ",")
    Dim buffers As Variant: buffers = Array(15, 30, 45, 60, 90)
    Dim origProfile As String: origProfile = ConfigVal("Default_Profile")
    Dim i As Long, j As Long
    For i = 0 To UBound(profiles)
        SetConfigVal "Default_Profile", profiles(i)
        For j = 0 To UBound(buffers)
            Dim h As Double: h = Brain_CalcStreamingHealth()
            If h > bestHealth Then bestHealth = h: bestBuffer = CLng(buffers(j)): bestProfile = profiles(i)
        Next j
    Next i
    SetConfigVal "Default_Profile", origProfile
    If MsgBox("Mejor: " & bestProfile & " buf=" & bestBuffer & "s Health=" & Round(bestHealth, 1) & vbCrLf & "Aplicar?", vbYesNo + vbQuestion) = vbYes Then
        SetConfigVal "Default_Profile", bestProfile
        Brain_RecalcAll
    End If
End Sub

' ============================================================================
' AUTO-FILL
' ============================================================================
Private Function SuggestPlaceholderForTag(tag As String) As String
    tag = LCase(tag)
    If InStr(tag, "user-agent") > 0 Then SuggestPlaceholderForTag = "{config.user_agent}"
    If InStr(tag, "referer") > 0 Or InStr(tag, "referrer") > 0 Then SuggestPlaceholderForTag = "{config.referer}"
    If InStr(tag, "origin") > 0 Then SuggestPlaceholderForTag = "{config.origin}"
    If InStr(tag, "connection") > 0 Then SuggestPlaceholderForTag = "{config.connection}"
    If InStr(tag, "network-caching") > 0 Then SuggestPlaceholderForTag = "{profile.buffer_ms}"
    If InStr(tag, "max_bandwidth") > 0 Then SuggestPlaceholderForTag = "{profile.max_bandwidth}"
    If InStr(tag, "resolution") > 0 Then SuggestPlaceholderForTag = "{profile.resolution}"
End Function

Public Sub Brain_AutoFillNivel3()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_NIVEL3)
    Dim r As Long, lastRow As Long, filled As Long
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 3).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 6 To lastRow
        If UCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value)) = "X" And Trim(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 4).value)) = "" Then
            Dim sugg As String: sugg = SuggestPlaceholderForTag(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 3).value))
            If sugg <> "" Then
                ws.Cells(r, 4).value = sugg
                ws.Cells(r, 4).Interior.Color = RGB(226, 239, 218)
                filled = filled + 1
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Auto-filled " & filled & " cells", vbInformation
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_AutoFillNivel1()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_NIVEL1)
    Dim r As Long, lastRow As Long, filled As Long
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 6 To lastRow
        If UCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value)) = "X" And Trim(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 3).value)) = "" Then
            Dim sugg As String: sugg = SuggestPlaceholderForTag(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 2).value))
            If sugg <> "" Then
                ws.Cells(r, 3).value = sugg
                ws.Cells(r, 3).Interior.Color = RGB(226, 239, 218)
                filled = filled + 1
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Auto-filled " & filled & " en NIVEL_1", vbInformation
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_AutoFillPerfect()
    Brain_AutoFillNivel1
    Brain_AutoFillNivel3
    Brain_DetectContradictions
    Brain_RecalcAll
End Sub

' ============================================================================
' URL BUILDER / TEST / VLC

Public Sub BuildSampleURL()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_URLBUILDER)
    Dim serv As String: serv = ResolvePlaceholder(CStr(ws.Cells(6, 2).value))
    Dim user As String: user = CStr(ws.Cells(7, 2).value)
    Dim pass As String: pass = CStr(ws.Cells(8, 2).value)
    Dim sid As String: sid = CStr(ws.Cells(9, 2).value)
    Dim pathPref As String: pathPref = CStr(ws.Cells(10, 2).value)
    Dim ext As String: ext = CStr(ws.Cells(11, 2).value)
    Dim queryMode As String: queryMode = CStr(ws.Cells(12, 2).value)
    Dim url As String: url = serv & pathPref & user & "/" & pass & "/" & sid & ext
    Select Case LCase(queryMode)
        Case "jwt": url = url & "?ape_jwt=" & BuildJWT_HS256_Simple()
        Case "b64": url = url & "?ape_data=" & Base64Encode("profile=" & ConfigVal("Default_Profile"))
        Case "custom"
            Dim cu As String: cu = CStr(ws.Cells(14, 2).value)
            If cu <> "" Then url = url & "?" & cu
    End Select
    ws.Cells(19, 2).value = url
    ws.Cells(20, 2).value = Len(url)
    ws.Cells(21, 2).value = IIf(Len(url) > 8000, "SI - REDUCIR", "OK")
End Sub

Public Sub TestURL()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_URLBUILDER)
    Dim url As String: url = CStr(ws.Cells(19, 2).value)
    If url = "" Then MsgBox "Primero construye URL", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Dim probeWs As Worksheet: Set probeWs = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_URLPROBE)
    Dim http As Object, t0 As Single: t0 = Timer
    On Error Resume Next
    Set http = CreateObject("MSXML2.ServerXMLHTTP.6.0")
    http.setOption 2, 13056
    http.setTimeouts 5000, 5000, 8000, 10000
    http.Open "GET", url, False
    http.setRequestHeader "User-Agent", ResolvePlaceholder("{config.user_agent}")
    http.setRequestHeader "Referer", ResolvePlaceholder("{config.referer}")
    http.setRequestHeader "Range", "bytes=0-32767"
    http.send
    Dim elapsed As Single: elapsed = (Timer - t0) * 1000
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then
        probeWs.Cells(4, 2).value = "ERROR"
        probeWs.Cells(5, 2).value = Err.description
        If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Error: " & Err.description, vbCritical
        On Error GoTo 0: Exit Sub
    End If
    probeWs.Cells(4, 2).value = http.status
    probeWs.Cells(5, 2).value = http.statusText
    probeWs.Cells(6, 2).value = http.getResponseHeader("Content-Type")
    probeWs.Cells(7, 2).value = http.getResponseHeader("Content-Length")
    probeWs.Cells(8, 2).value = http.getResponseHeader("Server")
    probeWs.Cells(9, 2).value = Round(elapsed, 0)
    probeWs.Cells(13, 2).value = Mid(http.responseText, 1, 200)
    probeWs.Cells(17, 2).value = "Test OK " & http.status
    On Error GoTo 0
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Status: " & http.status & " TTFB: " & Round(elapsed, 0) & "ms", vbInformation
End Sub

Public Sub CopyURL()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_URLBUILDER)
    Dim url As String: url = CStr(ws.Cells(19, 2).value)
    If url = "" Then MsgBox "Primero construye URL", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Dim dobj As Object
    Set dobj = CreateObject("new:{1C3B4210-F441-11CE-B9EA-00AA006B1A69}")
    dobj.SetText url: dobj.PutInClipboard
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "URL copiada", vbInformation
End Sub

Public Sub OpenInVLC()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_URLBUILDER)
    Dim url As String: url = CStr(ws.Cells(19, 2).value)
    If url = "" Then MsgBox "Primero construye URL", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Dim paths As Variant
    paths = Array("C:\Program Files\VideoLAN\VLC\vlc.exe", "C:\Program Files (x86)\VideoLAN\VLC\vlc.exe")
    Dim vlcPath As String, p As Variant
    For Each p In paths
        If dir(CStr(p)) <> "" Then vlcPath = CStr(p): Exit For
    Next p
    If vlcPath = "" Then MsgBox "VLC no encontrado", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Shell """" & vlcPath & """ """ & url & """" & _
          " --http-user-agent=""" & ResolvePlaceholder("{config.user_agent}") & """" & _
          " --http-referrer=""" & ResolvePlaceholder("{config.referer}") & """" & _
          " --network-caching=10000 --http-reconnect", vbNormalFocus
End Sub

Public Sub MeasureWaterfall()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_URLBUILDER)
    Dim url As String: url = CStr(ws.Cells(19, 2).value)
    If url = "" Then MsgBox "Primero construye URL", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    If dir(Environ("SystemRoot") & "\System32\curl.exe") = "" Then MsgBox "curl.exe no encontrado", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Dim tmpFile As String: tmpFile = Environ("TEMP") & "\ape_wf_" & Format(Now, "hhmmss") & ".txt"
    Dim cmd As String
    cmd = "cmd /c curl.exe -s -o NUL --max-time 10 -A """ & ResolvePlaceholder("{config.user_agent}") & """" & _
          " -e """ & ResolvePlaceholder("{config.referer}") & """" & _
          " -w ""dns=%{time_namelookup}|tcp=%{time_connect}|tls=%{time_appconnect}|ttfb=%{time_starttransfer}|total=%{time_total}|status=%{http_code}""" & _
          " """ & url & """ > """ & tmpFile & """"
    Shell cmd, vbHide
    Application.Wait Now + TimeValue("0:00:12")
    If dir(tmpFile) = "" Then MsgBox "curl sin output", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Dim fNum As Integer, content As String
    fNum = FreeFile: Open tmpFile For Input As #fNum
    content = Input(LOF(fNum), fNum): Close #fNum
    Kill tmpFile
    Dim wfWs As Worksheet: Set wfWs = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_WATERFALL)
    Dim pairs() As String: pairs = Split(content, "|")
    Dim kv As Variant
    For Each kv In pairs
        Dim eq As Long: eq = InStr(CStr(kv), "=")
        If eq > 0 Then
            Dim k As String: k = Left(CStr(kv), eq - 1)
            Dim v As String: v = Mid(CStr(kv), eq + 1)
            If isNumeric(v) Then
                Dim ms As Double: ms = CDbl(v) * 1000
                Select Case k
                    Case "dns": wfWs.Cells(5, 2).value = Round(ms, 0)
                    Case "tcp": wfWs.Cells(6, 2).value = Round(ms, 0)
                    Case "tls": wfWs.Cells(7, 2).value = Round(ms, 0)
                    Case "ttfb": wfWs.Cells(9, 2).value = Round(ms, 0)
                    Case "total": wfWs.Cells(12, 2).value = Round(ms, 0)
                End Select
            End If
        End If
    Next kv
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Waterfall medido", vbInformation
End Sub

' ============================================================================
' JWT + BASE64
' ============================================================================
Public Function BuildJWT_HS256_Simple() As String
    Dim payload As String
    payload = "{""iss"":""APE_LAB"",""iat"":" & DateDiff("s", #1/1/1970#, Now) & _
              ",""exp"":" & (DateDiff("s", #1/1/1970#, Now) + CLng(val(ConfigVal("JWT_Default_TTL_Sec")))) & _
              ",""profile"":""" & ConfigVal("Default_Profile") & """}"
    Dim header As String: header = "{""alg"":""HS256"",""typ"":""JWT""}"
    Dim headerB64 As String: headerB64 = Base64Encode(header)
    Dim payloadB64 As String: payloadB64 = Base64Encode(payload)
    Dim sig As String: sig = Base64Encode(headerB64 & "." & payloadB64 & ".secret")
    sig = Replace(Replace(Replace(sig, "+", "-"), "/", "_"), "=", "")
    BuildJWT_HS256_Simple = headerB64 & "." & payloadB64 & "." & sig
End Function

Public Function Base64Encode(txt As String) As String
    Dim arr() As Byte: arr = StrConv(txt, vbFromUnicode)
    Dim xml As Object: Set xml = CreateObject("MSXML2.DOMDocument")
    Dim node As Object: Set node = xml.createElement("b64")
    node.DataType = "bin.base64": node.nodeTypedValue = arr
    Base64Encode = node.Text
End Function

Public Function Base64Decode(b64 As String) As String
    Dim s As String: s = Replace(Replace(b64, "-", "+"), "_", "/")
    Do While Len(s) Mod 4 <> 0: s = s & "=": Loop
    Dim xml As Object: Set xml = CreateObject("MSXML2.DOMDocument")
    Dim node As Object: Set node = xml.createElement("b64")
    node.DataType = "bin.base64": node.Text = s
    Base64Decode = StrConv(node.nodeTypedValue, vbUnicode)
End Function

Public Sub DecodeJWT()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_URLBUILDER)
    Dim url As String: url = CStr(ws.Cells(19, 2).value)
    If InStr(url, "ape_jwt=") = 0 Then MsgBox "URL sin ape_jwt=", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Dim token As String: token = Mid(url, InStr(url, "ape_jwt=") + 8)
    Dim amp As Long: amp = InStr(token, "&")
    If amp > 0 Then token = Left(token, amp - 1)
    Dim parts() As String: parts = Split(token, ".")
    If UBound(parts) < 2 Then MsgBox "JWT mal formado", vbCritical: Exit Sub
    ws.Cells(22, 2).value = Base64Decode(parts(1))
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "HEADER: " & Base64Decode(parts(0)) & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "PAYLOAD: " & Base64Decode(parts(1)), vbInformation, "JWT"
End Sub

Public Sub TestBatch()
    Dim wsSrv As Worksheet: Set wsSrv = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_SERVERS)
    Dim wsURL As Worksheet: Set wsURL = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_URLBUILDER)
    Dim sid As String: sid = CStr(wsURL.Cells(9, 2).value)
    Dim pathPref As String: pathPref = CStr(wsURL.Cells(10, 2).value)
    Dim ext As String: ext = CStr(wsURL.Cells(11, 2).value)
    If sid = "" Then MsgBox "Define Stream ID", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Dim results As String: results = "BATCH:" & vbCrLf
    Dim okC As Long, failC As Long
    Dim r As Long, lR As Long
    lR = wsSrv.Cells(wsSrv.rows.count, 3).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 4 To lR
        If UCase(CStr(wsSrv.Cells(r, 1).value)) = "X" Then
            Dim srv As String: srv = CStr(wsSrv.Cells(r, 3).value)
            Dim usr As String: usr = CStr(wsSrv.Cells(r, 4).value)
            Dim pwd As String: pwd = CStr(wsSrv.Cells(r, 5).value)
            If srv <> "" And usr <> "" Then
                Dim url As String: url = srv & pathPref & usr & "/" & pwd & "/" & sid & ext
                Dim http As Object
                On Error Resume Next
                Set http = CreateObject("MSXML2.ServerXMLHTTP.6.0")
                http.setTimeouts 3000, 3000, 5000, 8000
                http.Open "GET", url, False
                http.setRequestHeader "User-Agent", ResolvePlaceholder("{config.user_agent}")
                http.setRequestHeader "Range", "bytes=0-4095"
                http.send
                If Err.Number = 0 And http.status >= 200 And http.status < 400 Then
                    results = results & "[OK " & http.status & "] " & srv & vbCrLf: okC = okC + 1
                Else
                    results = results & "[FAIL] " & srv & vbCrLf: failC = failC + 1
                End If
                Err.Clear: On Error GoTo 0
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox results & vbCrLf & "OK=" & okC & " FAIL=" & failC, vbInformation
End Sub

' ============================================================================
' SESSION B+C — FRONTEND BRIDGE v1.1 MULTI-SERVER
' ============================================================================
Public Sub ImportFromFrontend()
    On Error GoTo ErrHandler
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual

    Dim filePath As String: filePath = FE_BrowseForJSON()
    If filePath = "" Then GoTo cleanup
    Dim jsonText As String: jsonText = FE_ReadFileUTF8(filePath)
    If Len(jsonText) = 0 Then MsgBox "Archivo vacio.", vbExclamation: GoTo cleanup

    ' Init htmlfile JScript (compatible 64-bit)
    Dim htmlDoc As Object
    Set htmlDoc = CreateObject("htmlfile")
    htmlDoc.write "<meta http-equiv=""X-UA-Compatible"" content=""IE=9"">"
    Set FE_sc = htmlDoc.parentWindow

    ' JScript helpers v1.1 — multi-server
    FE_sc.execScript _
        "var __data=null;" & _
        "function initData(s){__data=JSON.parse(s);return 1;}" & _
        "function getMeta(k){return (__data&&__data[k]!==undefined&&__data[k]!==null)?String(__data[k]):'';}" & _
        "function chCount(){return (__data&&__data.channels)?__data.channels.length:0;}" & _
        "function chStr(i,k){var c=__data.channels[i];return (c&&c[k]!==undefined&&c[k]!==null)?String(c[k]):'';}" & _
        "function chBool(i,k){var c=__data.channels[i];return (c&&c[k])?1:0;}" & _
        "function chTags(i){var c=__data.channels[i];return (c&&c.quality_tags&&c.quality_tags.join)?c.quality_tags.join(','):'';}" & _
        "function srvCount(){return (__data&&__data.servers)?__data.servers.length:0;}" & _
        "function srvStrByIdx(i,k){var s=__data.servers[i];return (s&&s[k]!==undefined&&s[k]!==null)?String(s[k]):'';}" & _
        "function srvStrById(id,k){if(!__data||!__data.servers)return '';for(var i=0;i<__data.servers.length;i++){var s=__data.servers[i];if(s&&s.id===id)return (s[k]!==undefined&&s[k]!==null)?String(s[k]):'';}return '';}" & _
        "function hasProfiles(){return (__data&&__data.profiles_snapshot)?1:0;}" & _
        "function profStr(pid,k){var p=__data.profiles_snapshot[pid];if(!p)return '';var s=p.settings;return (s&&s[k]!==undefined&&s[k]!==null)?String(s[k]):'';}", _
        "JScript"

    FE_sc.execScript "__data = " & jsonText & ";", "JScript"

    ' ========= PARCHE DUAL JSON - auto-deteccion tipo de archivo =========
    FE_sc.execScript "function fe_chCount(){return (__data&&__data.channels)?__data.channels.length:0;}", "JScript"
    FE_sc.execScript "function fe_hasProfilesTopLevel(){return (__data&&__data.profiles&&typeof __data.profiles==='object'&&!__data.channels)?1:0;}", "JScript"
    Dim fe_chC As Long: fe_chC = CLng(FE_sc.eval("fe_chCount()"))
    Dim fe_hasProfTL As Long: fe_hasProfTL = CLng(FE_sc.eval("fe_hasProfilesTopLevel()"))
    If fe_chC = 0 And fe_hasProfTL = 1 Then
        ' === TIPO PROFILES-ONLY ===
        Call FE_SyncProfilesFromTopLevel
        Application.StatusBar = False
        If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "PROFILES-ONLY OK" & vbCrLf & "Sincronizados P0-P5 desde " & filePath, vbInformation, "IMPORT PERFILES"
        GoTo cleanup
    ElseIf fe_chC = 0 And fe_hasProfTL = 0 Then
        If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "JSON no reconocido. Esperaba 'channels' (snapshot) o 'profiles' (perfiles).", vbCritical, "IMPORT"
        GoTo cleanup
    End If
    ' ========= FIN PARCHE DUAL JSON =========


    Dim ver As String: ver = CStr(FE_sc.eval("getMeta('lab_bridge_version')"))
    If ver <> "1.1" And ver <> "1.0" Then
        If MsgBox("Version " & ver & " (esperada 1.1). Continuar?", vbYesNo + vbQuestion) = vbNo Then GoTo cleanup
    End If

    Dim chCount As Long: chCount = CLng(FE_sc.eval("chCount()"))
    If chCount = 0 Then MsgBox "JSON sin 'channels'.", vbCritical: GoTo cleanup

    Dim srvCount As Long: srvCount = CLng(FE_sc.eval("srvCount()"))

    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = FE_EnsureSheet()
    FE_ClearDataSimple ws

    ws.Cells(2, 1).value = "Import: " & Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss") & _
        " | Ver: " & ver & " | Canales: " & chCount & " | Servers: " & srvCount & _
        " | ActiveProfile: " & CStr(FE_sc.eval("getMeta('active_profile_id')")) & _
        " | File: " & filePath
    ws.Cells(2, 1).Font.Italic = True
    ws.Cells(2, 1).Font.Color = RGB(100, 100, 100)

    Dim batchId As String: batchId = "BATCH_" & Format(Now, "yyyymmdd_hhmmss")
    Dim importedAt As String: importedAt = Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss")

    ' Sincronizar TODOS los servers del array a 17_SERVERS_POOL (v1.1)
    Dim si As Long
    For si = 0 To srvCount - 1
        FE_SyncServerJS _
            CStr(FE_sc.eval("srvStrByIdx(" & si & ",'name')")), _
            CStr(FE_sc.eval("srvStrByIdx(" & si & ",'base_url')")), _
            CStr(FE_sc.eval("srvStrByIdx(" & si & ",'username')")), _
            CStr(FE_sc.eval("srvStrByIdx(" & si & ",'password')"))
    Next si

    Dim r As Long: r = 4
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To chCount - 1
        FE_WriteRowJS ws, r, i, batchId, importedAt
        r = r + 1
        If (i Mod 100) = 0 Then Application.StatusBar = "Importando " & (i + 1) & "/" & chCount
    Next i

    Dim newHash As String: newHash = CStr(FE_sc.eval("getMeta('profiles_hash')"))
    Dim oldHash As String: oldHash = ConfigVal("Frontend_Profiles_Hash")
    Dim synced As Boolean: synced = False
    If newHash <> "" And newHash <> oldHash And CLng(FE_sc.eval("hasProfiles()")) = 1 Then
        If MsgBox("Perfiles cambiaron. Sincronizar P0-P5?", vbYesNo + vbQuestion) = vbYes Then
            FE_SyncProfilesJS
            SetConfigVal "Frontend_Profiles_Hash", newHash
            synced = True
        End If
    End If

    FE_FormatSheetSimple ws, chCount
    ws.Activate
    ws.Cells(4, 1).Select
    Brain_RecalcAllSilent

    Application.StatusBar = False
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "IMPORT OK v1.1" & vbCrLf & _
           "Canales: " & chCount & vbCrLf & _
           "Servers: " & srvCount & vbCrLf & _
           "Perfiles sync: " & IIf(synced, "SI", "NO") & vbCrLf & _
           "Batch: " & batchId, vbInformation, "IMPORT FRONTEND"

cleanup:
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.StatusBar = False
    Exit Sub
ErrHandler:
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.StatusBar = False
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "ERROR " & Err.Number & ": " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub

Private Function FE_ReadFileUTF8(filePath As String) As String
    Dim stream As Object
    Set stream = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
    stream.Charset = "utf-8"
    stream.Open
    stream.LoadFromFile filePath
    FE_ReadFileUTF8 = stream.ReadText
    stream.Close
End Function

Private Function FE_EnsureSheet() As Worksheet
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_FRONTEND)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets.Add(After:=ThisWorkbook.sheets(ThisWorkbook.sheets.count))
        ws.name = SHEET_FRONTEND
    End If
    ws.Cells(1, 1).value = "33_CHANNELS_FROM_FRONTEND"
    ws.Cells(1, 1).Font.Bold = True
    Dim headers As Variant
    headers = Array("Activo", "#", "stream_id", "name", "tvg_id", "logo", "group", _
        "country", "language", "timezone", _
        "is_sports", "is_movie", "is_series", "is_uhd", "is_hd", "is_sd", _
        "resolution", "width", "height", "codec", "bitrate_kbps", "fps", "quality_tier", "quality_tags", _
        "quality_score_fe", "profile_auto", "profile_override", "class_original", "class_override", _
        "server_id", "server_name", "server_base_url", "url_playback", "url_length", _
        "last_status", "status_text", "ttfb_ms", "content_type", "content_length", "server_hdr", "verified_at", "veredicto", _
        "brain_health", "brain_risk", "brain_headroom_%", "brain_stall_%", _
        "rec_buffer_s", "rec_ua", "rec_referer", "rec_conn", "fallback_srv", "anti_403_profile", _
        "batch_id", "imported_at", "notes")
    Dim c As Long
    For c = 1 To UBound(headers) + 1
        With ws.Cells(3, c)
            .value = headers(c - 1)
            .Font.Bold = True
            .Interior.Color = RGB(31, 78, 120)
            .Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
            .HorizontalAlignment = xlCenter
        End With
    Next c
    ws.rows(3).RowHeight = 30
    ws.Range("A3:BC3").WrapText = True
    Set FE_EnsureSheet = ws
End Function

Private Sub FE_ClearDataSimple(ws As Worksheet)
    Dim lastRow As Long
    lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, FE_COL_STREAMID).End(xlUp).row
    If lastRow >= 4 Then ws.Range("A4:BC" & lastRow).Clear
    ws.Range("A2:BC2").Clear
End Sub

Private Sub FE_FormatSheetSimple(ws As Worksheet, chCount As Long)
    ws.Activate
    ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = False
    ws.Cells(4, 4).Select
    ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = True
    ws.Columns("D:D").ColumnWidth = 35
    ws.Columns("F:F").ColumnWidth = 25
    ws.Columns("AG:AG").ColumnWidth = 50
    If chCount <= 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim rng As Range
    Set rng = ws.Range(ws.Cells(4, FE_COL_VEREDICTO), ws.Cells(3 + chCount, FE_COL_VEREDICTO))
    rng.FormatConditions.Delete
    With rng.FormatConditions.Add(Type:=xlTextString, String:="OK", TextOperator:=xlContains)
        .Interior.Color = RGB(198, 239, 206)
    End With
    With rng.FormatConditions.Add(Type:=xlTextString, String:="FAIL", TextOperator:=xlContains)
        .Interior.Color = RGB(255, 199, 206)
    End With
End Sub

Private Function FE_CountryToTZ(country As String) As String
    Select Case UCase(country)
        Case "ES": FE_CountryToTZ = "Europe/Madrid"
        Case "US": FE_CountryToTZ = "America/New_York"
        Case "MX": FE_CountryToTZ = "America/Mexico_City"
        Case "AR": FE_CountryToTZ = "America/Buenos_Aires"
        Case "CO": FE_CountryToTZ = "America/Bogota"
        Case "BR": FE_CountryToTZ = "America/Sao_Paulo"
        Case "GB", "UK": FE_CountryToTZ = "Europe/London"
        Case "FR": FE_CountryToTZ = "Europe/Paris"
        Case "DE": FE_CountryToTZ = "Europe/Berlin"
        Case Else: FE_CountryToTZ = "UTC"
    End Select
End Function

Private Function FE_EscapeJS(s As String) As String
    FE_EscapeJS = Replace(Replace(s, "\", "\\"), "'", "\'")
End Function

Private Sub FE_WriteRowJS(ws As Worksheet, r As Long, i As Long, _
                          batchId As String, importedAt As String)
    Dim idx As String: idx = CStr(i)
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_ACTIVO).value = "X"
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_NUM).value = r - 3
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_STREAMID).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'stream_id')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_NAME).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'name')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_TVGID).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'tvg_id')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_LOGO).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'logo')"))
    Dim grp As String: grp = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'group')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_GROUP).value = grp
    Dim cty As String: cty = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'country')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_COUNTRY).value = cty
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_LANG).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'language')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_TZ).value = FE_CountryToTZ(cty)
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_SPORTS).value = IIf(CLng(FE_sc.eval("chBool(" & idx & ",'is_sports')")) = 1, "X", "-")
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_MOVIE).value = IIf(CLng(FE_sc.eval("chBool(" & idx & ",'is_movie')")) = 1, "X", "-")
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_SERIES).value = IIf(CLng(FE_sc.eval("chBool(" & idx & ",'is_series')")) = 1, "X", "-")
    Dim isUHD As Boolean: isUHD = (CLng(FE_sc.eval("chBool(" & idx & ",'is_uhd')")) = 1)
    Dim isHD As Boolean: isHD = (CLng(FE_sc.eval("chBool(" & idx & ",'is_hd')")) = 1)
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_UHD).value = IIf(isUHD, "X", "-")
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_HD).value = IIf(isHD, "X", "-")
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_SD).value = IIf(CLng(FE_sc.eval("chBool(" & idx & ",'is_sd')")) = 1, "X", "-")
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_RESOLUTION).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'resolution')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_WIDTH).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'width')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_HEIGHT).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'height')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_CODEC).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'codec')"))
    Dim br As String: br = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'bitrate')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_BITRATE).value = br
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_FPS).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'fps')"))
    Dim tier As String: tier = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'quality_tier')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_QTIER).value = tier
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_QTAGS).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chTags(" & idx & ")"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_QSCORE).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'quality_score')"))

    ' Auto-suggest profile
    Dim brN As Double: brN = val(br)
    Dim sugg As String
    If InStr(UCase(tier), "8K") > 0 Or brN >= 50000 Then
        sugg = "P0"
    ElseIf isUHD Or InStr(UCase(tier), "4K") > 0 Or InStr(UCase(tier), "UHD") > 0 Or brN >= 20000 Then
        sugg = "P1"
    ElseIf InStr(UCase(tier), "2K") > 0 Or InStr(UCase(tier), "QHD") > 0 Or brN >= 10000 Then
        sugg = "P2"
    ElseIf isHD Or InStr(UCase(tier), "FHD") > 0 Or InStr(UCase(tier), "1080") > 0 Or brN >= 4000 Then
        sugg = "P3"
    ElseIf InStr(UCase(tier), "HD") > 0 Or InStr(UCase(tier), "720") > 0 Or brN >= 2000 Then
        sugg = "P4"
    Else
        sugg = "P5"
    End If
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_PROFILE_AUTO).value = sugg
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_CLASS_ORIG).value = grp

    ' v1.1 — Lookup por server_id del canal
    Dim chSid As String: chSid = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'server_id')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_SRV_ID).value = chSid
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_SRV_NAME).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("srvStrById('" & FE_EscapeJS(chSid) & "','name')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_SRV_BASE).value = CStr(FE_sc.eval("srvStrById('" & FE_EscapeJS(chSid) & "','base_url')"))

    Dim urlVal As String: urlVal = CStr(FE_sc.eval("chStr(" & idx & ",'url')"))
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_URL).value = urlVal
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_URL_LEN).value = Len(urlVal)
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_VEREDICTO).value = "PENDING"
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_BATCH_ID).value = batchId
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_IMPORTED_AT).value = importedAt
End Sub

Private Sub FE_UpdateProfileCell(ws As Worksheet, pid As String, attr As String, newVal As String)
    If newVal = "" Then Exit Sub
    Dim col As Integer
    Select Case UCase(pid)
        Case "P0": col = 2
        Case "P1": col = 3
        Case "P2": col = 4
        Case "P3": col = 5
        Case "P4": col = 6
        Case "P5": col = 7
        Case Else: Exit Sub
    End Select
    Dim r As Long, lR As Long
    lR = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 3 To lR
        If InStr(LCase(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value)), LCase(attr)) > 0 Then
            ws.Cells(r, col).value = newVal
            ws.Cells(r, col).Interior.Color = RGB(226, 239, 218)
            Exit Sub
        End If
    Next r
End Sub

Private Sub FE_SyncServerJS(nm As String, u As String, usr As String, pw As String)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_SERVERS)
    If u = "" Then Exit Sub
    Dim r As Long, lR As Long, found As Boolean
    lR = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 3).End(xlUp).row
    For r = 4 To lR
        If CStr(ws.Cells(r, 3).value) = u Then
            ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "X"
            ws.Cells(r, 4).value = usr
            ws.Cells(r, 5).value = pw
            If nm <> "" Then ws.Cells(r, 9).value = nm & " (from frontend)"
            found = True: Exit For
        End If
    Next r
    If Not found Then
        r = lR + 1
        ws.Cells(r, 1).value = "X"
        ws.Cells(r, 2).value = r - 3
        ws.Cells(r, 3).value = u
        ws.Cells(r, 4).value = usr
        ws.Cells(r, 5).value = pw
        ws.Cells(r, 6).value = 1
        ws.Cells(r, 8).value = "Active"
        ws.Cells(r, 9).value = nm & " (from frontend)"
    End If
End Sub

Public Function FE_ParseJSON(jsonText As String) As Object
    Dim htmlDoc As Object
    Set htmlDoc = CreateObject("htmlfile")
    htmlDoc.write "<meta http-equiv=""X-UA-Compatible"" content=""IE=9"">"
    htmlDoc.parentWindow.execScript "var __j = " & jsonText & ";", "JScript"
    Set FE_ParseJSON = htmlDoc.parentWindow.eval("__j")
End Function

' ============================================================================
' SESSION D — VERIFY CHANNELS HTTP (batch + individual)
' ============================================================================
Public Sub VerifyChannelSelected()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_FRONTEND)
    Dim r As Long: r = ActiveCell.row
    If r < 4 Then MsgBox "Selecciona una fila de canal (>= 4)", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    Dim url As String: url = CStr(ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_URL).value)
    If url = "" Or Not url Like "http*" Then MsgBox "Fila sin URL valida", vbExclamation: Exit Sub
    FE_VerifyOneRow ws, r
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Canal " & ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_STREAMID).value & " verificado." & vbCrLf & _
           "Status: " & ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_STATUS).value & vbCrLf & _
           "TTFB: " & ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_TTFB).value & " ms" & vbCrLf & _
           "Veredicto: " & ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_VEREDICTO).value, vbInformation
End Sub

Public Sub VerifyChannelsBatch()
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_FRONTEND)
    Dim lastRow As Long: lastRow = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, FE_COL_STREAMID).End(xlUp).row
    If lastRow < 4 Then MsgBox "Hoja 33 vacia. Importa primero.", vbExclamation: Exit Sub

    Dim totalRows As Long: totalRows = lastRow - 3
    Dim answer As String
    answer = InputBox("Verificar cuantos canales?" & vbCrLf & _
                      "Total disponibles: " & totalRows & vbCrLf & _
                      "Ingresa numero o rango (ej: 50, 1-100, ALL)", _
                      "VERIFY BATCH", "50")
    If answer = "" Then Exit Sub

    Dim rStart As Long, rEnd As Long
    rStart = 4
    If UCase(answer) = "ALL" Then
        rEnd = lastRow
    ElseIf InStr(answer, "-") > 0 Then
        Dim parts() As String: parts = Split(answer, "-")
        rStart = 3 + CLng(val(parts(0)))
        rEnd = 3 + CLng(val(parts(1)))
    Else
        rEnd = 3 + CLng(val(answer))
    End If
    If rEnd > lastRow Then rEnd = lastRow
    If rStart < 4 Then rStart = 4

    Dim total As Long: total = rEnd - rStart + 1
    If MsgBox("Verificar " & total & " canales (filas " & rStart & "-" & rEnd & ")?" & vbCrLf & _
              "Estimado: " & Round(total * 2 / 60, 1) & " min", _
              vbYesNo + vbQuestion, "CONFIRMAR") = vbNo Then Exit Sub

    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.EnableEvents = False

    Dim okC As Long, failC As Long, slowC As Long, errC As Long
    Dim r As Long, i As Long: i = 0
    Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer

    For r = rStart To rEnd
        Dim v As String: v = FE_VerifyOneRow(ws, r)
        Select Case v
            Case "OK": okC = okC + 1
            Case "SLOW": slowC = slowC + 1
            Case "FAIL": failC = failC + 1
            Case Else: errC = errC + 1
        End Select
        i = i + 1
        If (i Mod 10) = 0 Then
            Dim elapsed As Double: elapsed = Timer - t0
            Dim etaSec As Double: etaSec = (elapsed / i) * (total - i)
            Application.StatusBar = "Verificando " & i & "/" & total & _
                " | OK=" & okC & " SLOW=" & slowC & " FAIL=" & failC & " ERR=" & errC & _
                " | ETA: " & Round(etaSec, 0) & "s"
            DoEvents
        End If
    Next r

    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.StatusBar = False

    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "VERIFY COMPLETO" & vbCrLf & _
           "Total: " & total & vbCrLf & _
           "OK: " & okC & " (" & Round(okC * 100 / total, 1) & "%)" & vbCrLf & _
           "SLOW: " & slowC & vbCrLf & _
           "FAIL: " & failC & vbCrLf & _
           "ERR: " & errC & vbCrLf & _
           "Tiempo: " & Round((Timer - t0) / 60, 1) & " min", vbInformation, "VERIFY BATCH"
End Sub

Private Function FE_VerifyOneRow(ws As Worksheet, r As Long) As String
    Dim url As String: url = CStr(ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_URL).value)
    If url = "" Or Not (Left(LCase(url), 4) = "http") Then
        ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_VEREDICTO).value = "ERR"
        FE_VerifyOneRow = "ERR"
        Exit Function
    End If

    Dim ua As String: ua = ResolvePlaceholder("{config.user_agent}")
    Dim ref As String: ref = ResolvePlaceholder("{config.referer}")
    If ua = "" Or ua = "{config.user_agent}" Then ua = "Mozilla/5.0 (Web0S; Linux/SmartTV) AppleWebKit/537.36"
    If ref = "" Or ref = "{config.referer}" Then ref = ""

    Dim http As Object
    Dim t0 As Single: t0 = Timer
    On Error Resume Next
    Set http = CreateObject("MSXML2.ServerXMLHTTP.6.0")
    http.setOption 2, 13056
    http.setTimeouts 3000, 3000, 5000, 8000
    http.Open "GET", url, False
    http.setRequestHeader "User-Agent", ua
    If ref <> "" Then http.setRequestHeader "Referer", ref
    http.setRequestHeader "Range", "bytes=0-8191"
    http.setRequestHeader "Connection", "keep-alive"
    http.send

    Dim elapsed As Long: elapsed = CLng((Timer - t0) * 1000)
    Dim veredicto As String

    If Err.Number <> 0 Then
        ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_STATUS).value = "ERR"
        ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_STATUS_TXT).value = Left(Err.description, 100)
        ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_TTFB).value = elapsed
        veredicto = "ERR"
        Err.Clear
    Else
        Dim st As Long: st = http.status
        ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_STATUS).value = st
        ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_STATUS_TXT).value = http.statusText
        ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_TTFB).value = elapsed
        ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_CTYPE).value = http.getResponseHeader("Content-Type")
        ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_CLENGTH).value = http.getResponseHeader("Content-Length")
        ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_SRVHDR).value = http.getResponseHeader("Server")

        If st >= 200 And st < 400 Then
            If elapsed > 1500 Then veredicto = "SLOW" Else veredicto = "OK"
        Else
            veredicto = "FAIL"
        End If
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0

    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_VERIFIED_AT).value = Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:nn:ss")
    ws.Cells(r, FE_COL_VEREDICTO).value = veredicto
    FE_VerifyOneRow = veredicto
End Function

VBA MACRO APE_OMEGA_MATH.bas 
in file: xl/vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/APE_OMEGA_MATH'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
'============================================================
' APE_OMEGA_MATH
' Motor matematico multi-fase para busqueda de perfeccion
' en el espacio 7-dim de configuracion del player IPTV.
'
' Pipeline:
'   1. Latin Hypercube Sampling   (cobertura uniforme global)
'   2. Genetic Algorithm adaptivo (exploracion guiada)
'   3. Simulated Annealing        (refinamiento estocastico)
'   4. Nelder-Mead Simplex        (pulido determinista)
'
' Multi-objetivo: Desirability Derringer-Suich (media geometrica
' de 7 desirabilidades en [0,1]). Fitness final en [0,1], 1=perfecto.
'
' Modelo heuristico: simula 7 players (TiviMate, Kodi, VLC, HLS.js,
' Web0S, Smarters, ExoPlayer) con interacciones no-lineales entre
' las 7 variables de control.
'============================================================
Option Explicit

Public Const OMEGA_N_DIMS As Long = 7
Public Const OMEGA_MAX_TIME_SEC As Double = 180#
Public Const OMEGA_TARGET_FIT As Double = 0.999

Private Type OmegaBounds
    lo As Double
    hi As Double
    isInt As Boolean
End Type

'============================================================
' ENTRY POINT PRINCIPAL
'============================================================
Public Sub Brain_OmegaOptimizer()
    Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer
    On Error GoTo EH
    WIRING_SILENT = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.StatusBar = "Omega: init..."

    Dim bounds(0 To 6) As OmegaBounds
    bounds(0).lo = 10: bounds(0).hi = 180: bounds(0).isInt = True   ' buffer_seconds
    bounds(1).lo = 3:  bounds(1).hi = 60:  bounds(1).isInt = True   ' live_delay_seconds
    bounds(2).lo = 10: bounds(2).hi = 200: bounds(2).isInt = True   ' reconnect_attempts
    bounds(3).lo = 1:  bounds(3).hi = 20:  bounds(3).isInt = True   ' fragLoadPolicy_maxNumRetry
    bounds(4).lo = 3:  bounds(4).hi = 15:  bounds(4).isInt = True   ' nudgeMaxRetry
    bounds(5).lo = 0.5: bounds(5).hi = 1#: bounds(5).isInt = False  ' abrBandWidthFactor
    bounds(6).lo = 1#:  bounds(6).hi = 2#: bounds(6).isInt = False  ' maxLiveSyncPlaybackRate

    Dim diary As Worksheet
    Set diary = OmegaDiarySetup()
    OmegaLog diary, "INIT", "Motor Omega arrancado. Target fitness >= " & OMEGA_TARGET_FIT

    Randomize

    Const POP_SIZE As Long = 40
    Dim pop(1 To POP_SIZE, 0 To 6) As Double
    Dim fit(1 To POP_SIZE) As Double

    ' === FASE 1: Latin Hypercube Sampling ===
    OmegaLog diary, "F1-START", "Latin Hypercube Sampling (POP=" & POP_SIZE & ", cubre 7-dim uniformemente)"
    Call OmegaLHS(pop, POP_SIZE, bounds)

    ' Seed: incluir config ACTUAL del workbook como individuo 1
    pop(1, 0) = OmegaClampBound(ReadCell("18_CONFIG", "C31", 60), bounds(0))
    pop(1, 1) = OmegaClampBound(ReadCell("18_CONFIG", "C32", 10), bounds(1))
    pop(1, 2) = OmegaClampBound(ReadCell("18_CONFIG", "C33", 60), bounds(2))
    pop(1, 3) = OmegaClampBound(ReadCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H10", 10), bounds(3))
    pop(1, 4) = OmegaClampBound(ReadCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H11", 9), bounds(4))
    pop(1, 5) = OmegaClampBound(ReadCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H12", 0.85), bounds(5))
    pop(1, 6) = OmegaClampBound(ReadCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H13", 1.3), bounds(6))

    Dim i As Long
    For i = 1 To POP_SIZE: fit(i) = OmegaFitness(pop, i): Next i
    Call OmegaSortDesc(pop, fit)
    OmegaLog diary, "F1-END", "Mejor LHS fitness=" & Format(fit(1), "0.0000") & " (de 1.0)"
    Call OmegaSnapshotToDiary(diary, pop, fit(1), "LHS-best")

    ' === FASE 2: Genetic Algorithm adaptivo ===
    OmegaLog diary, "F2-START", "Genetic Algorithm adaptivo (mutRate autoajustable)"
    Call OmegaGA(pop, fit, bounds, 80, t0, diary)
    Call OmegaSnapshotToDiary(diary, pop, fit(1), "GA-best")

    ' === FASE 3: Simulated Annealing sobre mejor ===
    OmegaLog diary, "F3-START", "Simulated Annealing (Metropolis, T0=0.5, 300 steps)"
    Dim xBest(0 To 6) As Double, j As Long
    For j = 0 To 6: xBest(j) = pop(1, j): Next j
    Call OmegaSA(xBest, bounds, 300, 0.5, t0, diary)
    Dim fSA As Double: fSA = OmegaFitnessVec(xBest)
    If fSA > fit(1) Then
        For j = 0 To 6: pop(1, j) = xBest(j): Next j
        fit(1) = fSA
    End If
    Call OmegaSnapshotToDiary(diary, pop, fit(1), "SA-best")

    ' === FASE 4: Nelder-Mead Simplex ===
    OmegaLog diary, "F4-START", "Nelder-Mead Simplex (reflect/expand/contract/shrink)"
    For j = 0 To 6: xBest(j) = pop(1, j): Next j
    Call OmegaNelderMead(xBest, bounds, 150, t0, diary)
    Dim fNM As Double: fNM = OmegaFitnessVec(xBest)
    If fNM > fit(1) Then
        For j = 0 To 6: pop(1, j) = xBest(j): Next j
        fit(1) = fNM
    End If
    Call OmegaSnapshotToDiary(diary, pop, fit(1), "NM-best")

    ' === Aplicar resultado ganador al libro ===
    Call OmegaApplyConfig(pop, 1)

    OmegaLog diary, "DONE", "Omega finalizo en " & Format(Timer - t0, "0.0") & "s | fitness FINAL=" & Format(fit(1), "0.000000")

    diary.Columns("A:D").AutoFit

    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.StatusBar = False
    WIRING_SILENT = False
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.StatusBar = False
    WIRING_SILENT = False
    Call WB_LogMsg("Brain_OmegaOptimizer ERROR: " & Err.Number & " - " & Err.description)
End Sub

'============================================================
' FITNESS (Derringer-Suich multi-objetivo)
'============================================================
Private Function OmegaFitness(ByRef pop() As Double, idx As Long) As Double
    Dim x(0 To 6) As Double, j As Long
    For j = 0 To 6: x(j) = pop(idx, j): Next j
    OmegaFitness = OmegaFitnessVec(x)
End Function

Private Function OmegaFitnessVec(ByRef x() As Double) As Double
    Dim bufSec As Double: bufSec = x(0)
    Dim liveDelay As Double: liveDelay = x(1)
    Dim recAtt As Double: recAtt = x(2)
    Dim fragRetry As Double: fragRetry = x(3)
    Dim nudgeRetry As Double: nudgeRetry = x(4)
    Dim abrFactor As Double: abrFactor = x(5)
    Dim maxSync As Double: maxSync = x(6)

    ' Metricas base
    Dim bufStab As Double, bwHead As Double, errTree As Double, netLat As Double
    bufStab = (Bell(bufSec, 55, 35) ^ 0.8) * 0.55 + Bell(liveDelay, 10, 5) * 0.45
    bwHead = Clamp01((1# - abrFactor) * 0.55 + Bell(maxSync, 1.25, 0.45) * 0.45)
    errTree = Clamp01((recAtt / 200#) * 0.45 + (fragRetry / 20#) * 0.35 + (nudgeRetry / 15#) * 0.2)
    netLat = Bell(liveDelay, 10, 6)

    ' Interacciones no-lineales
    bufStab = bufStab * (0.7 + 0.3 * bwHead)          ' buffer exige ancho de banda
    errTree = errTree * (1 + 0.2 * LogSafe(recAtt / 20#))  ' reconnect efectivo escala log

    ' Promedios por 7 players
    Dim p As Long
    Dim healthSum As Double, riskSum As Double, stallSum As Double
    Dim universalOK As Long: universalOK = 0
    For p = 1 To 7
        Dim h As Double, r As Double, s As Double
        h = PlayerHealth(p, bufStab, bwHead, errTree, netLat)
        r = PlayerRisk(p, bufSec, recAtt, abrFactor, maxSync)
        s = PlayerStall(p, bufStab, errTree)
        healthSum = healthSum + h
        riskSum = riskSum + r
        stallSum = stallSum + s
        If h >= 85 And r <= 20 And s <= 0.08 Then universalOK = universalOK + 1
    Next p

    Dim healthAvg As Double: healthAvg = healthSum / 7#
    Dim riskAvg As Double: riskAvg = riskSum / 7#
    Dim stallAvg As Double: stallAvg = stallSum / 7#
    Dim compatPct As Double: compatPct = universalOK / 7# * 100#
    Dim headroomEst As Double: headroomEst = 300 + bwHead * 900#
    Dim ttfbEst As Double: ttfbEst = 450 + (1# - netLat) * 700#
    Dim exthttpEst As Double: exthttpEst = 57 + 2.5 * (recAtt + fragRetry * 3 + nudgeRetry * 2)

    ' Desirabilities (Derringer-Suich)
    Dim d(1 To 7) As Double
    d(1) = Desire_Max(healthAvg, 95, 40, 1.5)
    d(2) = Desire_Min(riskAvg, 10, 60, 1.5)
    d(3) = Desire_Max(headroomEst, 300, 100, 1#)
    d(4) = Desire_Min(stallAvg, 0.05, 0.3, 1#)
    d(5) = Desire_Max(compatPct, 100, 0, 2#)
    d(6) = Desire_Min(ttfbEst, 500, 1500, 1#)
    d(7) = Desire_Min(exthttpEst, 8000, 20000, 0.8)

    ' Media geometrica
    Dim prod As Double: prod = 1#
    For p = 1 To 7
        If d(p) < 0.0001 Then d(p) = 0.0001
        prod = prod * d(p)
    Next p
    OmegaFitnessVec = prod ^ (1# / 7#)
End Function

Private Function Desire_Max(val As Double, target As Double, worst As Double, expo As Double) As Double
    If val >= target Then Desire_Max = 1#: Exit Function
    If val <= worst Then Desire_Max = 0#: Exit Function
    Desire_Max = ((val - worst) / (target - worst)) ^ expo
End Function

Private Function Desire_Min(val As Double, target As Double, worst As Double, expo As Double) As Double
    If val <= target Then Desire_Min = 1#: Exit Function
    If val >= worst Then Desire_Min = 0#: Exit Function
    Desire_Min = ((worst - val) / (worst - target)) ^ expo
End Function

'============================================================
' PLAYER MODELS (7 players con perfiles diferenciados)
'============================================================
Private Function PlayerHealth(p As Long, bufStab As Double, bwHead As Double, _
                               errTree As Double, netLat As Double) As Double
    Dim w(1 To 4) As Double
    Select Case p
        Case 1: w(1) = 30: w(2) = 25: w(3) = 25: w(4) = 20  ' TiviMate
        Case 2: w(1) = 35: w(2) = 20: w(3) = 30: w(4) = 15  ' Kodi
        Case 3: w(1) = 20: w(2) = 30: w(3) = 20: w(4) = 30  ' VLC
        Case 4: w(1) = 25: w(2) = 30: w(3) = 25: w(4) = 20  ' HLS.js
        Case 5: w(1) = 40: w(2) = 20: w(3) = 25: w(4) = 15  ' Web0S
        Case 6: w(1) = 30: w(2) = 25: w(3) = 30: w(4) = 15  ' Smarters
        Case 7: w(1) = 35: w(2) = 20: w(3) = 30: w(4) = 15  ' ExoPlayer
    End Select
    PlayerHealth = bufStab * w(1) + bwHead * w(2) + errTree * w(3) + netLat * w(4)
End Function

Private Function PlayerRisk(p As Long, bufSec As Double, recAtt As Double, _
                             abrFactor As Double, maxSync As Double) As Double
    Dim base As Double: base = 0
    If bufSec < 20 Then base = base + 25
    If bufSec > 150 Then base = base + 15
    If recAtt < 20 Then base = base + 10
    If abrFactor > 0.95 Then base = base + 12
    If abrFactor < 0.55 Then base = base + 8
    If maxSync > 1.8 Then base = base + 6
    If p = 5 Or p = 6 Or p = 7 Then
        If bufSec > 120 Then base = base + 10    ' Smart TV / mobile = menos memoria
    End If
    PlayerRisk = base
End Function

Private Function PlayerStall(p As Long, bufStab As Double, errTree As Double) As Double
    Dim base As Double: base = 0.3 - (bufStab * 0.15) - (errTree * 0.12)
    If base < 0 Then base = 0
    If p = 4 Or p = 5 Then base = base + 0.03
    PlayerStall = base
End Function

'============================================================
' LATIN HYPERCUBE SAMPLING
'============================================================
Private Sub OmegaLHS(ByRef pop() As Double, n As Long, ByRef bounds() As OmegaBounds)
    Dim d As Long, i As Long, k As Long, tmp As Long
    Dim perm() As Long
    For d = 0 To 6
        ReDim perm(1 To n)
        For i = 1 To n: perm(i) = i: Next i
        For i = n To 2 Step -1
            k = Int(Rnd * i) + 1
            tmp = perm(i): perm(i) = perm(k): perm(k) = tmp
        Next i
        For i = 1 To n
            Dim frac As Double, v As Double
            frac = (perm(i) - 1 + Rnd) / n
            v = bounds(d).lo + frac * (bounds(d).hi - bounds(d).lo)
            If bounds(d).isInt Then v = CDbl(CLng(v))
            pop(i, d) = v
        Next i
    Next d
End Sub

'============================================================
' GENETIC ALGORITHM ADAPTIVO
'============================================================
Private Sub OmegaGA(ByRef pop() As Double, ByRef fit() As Double, _
                    ByRef bounds() As OmegaBounds, nGen As Long, _
                    t0 As Double, diary As Worksheet)
    Dim n As Long: n = UBound(fit)
    Dim mutRate As Double: mutRate = 0.3
    Const cxRate As Double = 0.75
    Const eliteN As Long = 3
    Const tournK As Long = 3

    Dim newPop() As Double: ReDim newPop(1 To n, 0 To 6)
    Dim newFit() As Double: ReDim newFit(1 To n)
    Dim prevBest As Double: prevBest = fit(1)
    Dim stagnation As Long: stagnation = 0

    Dim gen As Long, i As Long, j As Long
    For gen = 1 To nGen
        If (Timer - t0) > OMEGA_MAX_TIME_SEC Then Exit For
        Call OmegaSortDesc(pop, fit)

        If fit(1) - prevBest > 0.001 Then
            stagnation = 0
            mutRate = mutRate * 0.95
            If mutRate < 0.08 Then mutRate = 0.08
        Else
            stagnation = stagnation + 1
            If stagnation >= 5 Then
                mutRate = mutRate * 1.3
                If mutRate > 0.5 Then mutRate = 0.5
                stagnation = 0
            End If
        End If
        prevBest = fit(1)

        If gen = 1 Or gen Mod 10 = 0 Then
            OmegaLog diary, "GA-g" & gen, "bestFit=" & Format(fit(1), "0.0000") & " mutRate=" & Format(mutRate, "0.00") & " stag=" & stagnation
        End If
        If fit(1) >= OMEGA_TARGET_FIT Then Exit For

        For i = 1 To eliteN
            For j = 0 To 6: newPop(i, j) = pop(i, j): Next j
            newFit(i) = fit(i)
        Next i

        Dim p1 As Long, p2 As Long
        For i = eliteN + 1 To n
            p1 = OmegaTournament(fit, tournK)
            p2 = OmegaTournament(fit, tournK)
            If Rnd < cxRate Then
                Call OmegaBLXAlpha(pop, p1, p2, newPop, i, bounds, 0.3)
            Else
                For j = 0 To 6: newPop(i, j) = pop(p1, j): Next j
            End If
            Call OmegaMutate(newPop, i, bounds, mutRate)
            newFit(i) = OmegaFitness(newPop, i)
        Next i

        For i = 1 To n
            For j = 0 To 6: pop(i, j) = newPop(i, j): Next j
            fit(i) = newFit(i)
        Next i
    Next gen
End Sub

Private Function OmegaTournament(ByRef fit() As Double, k As Long) As Long
    Dim n As Long: n = UBound(fit)
    Dim best As Long, cand As Long, i As Long
    best = Int(Rnd * n) + 1
    For i = 2 To k
        cand = Int(Rnd * n) + 1
        If fit(cand) > fit(best) Then best = cand
    Next i
    OmegaTournament = best
End Function

Private Sub OmegaBLXAlpha(ByRef pop() As Double, p1 As Long, p2 As Long, _
                           ByRef newPop() As Double, idx As Long, _
                           ByRef bounds() As OmegaBounds, alpha As Double)
    Dim j As Long, lo As Double, hi As Double, rng As Double, v As Double
    For j = 0 To 6
        If pop(p1, j) < pop(p2, j) Then
            lo = pop(p1, j): hi = pop(p2, j)
        Else
            lo = pop(p2, j): hi = pop(p1, j)
        End If
        rng = hi - lo
        v = (lo - alpha * rng) + Rnd * (rng * (1 + 2 * alpha))
        newPop(idx, j) = OmegaClampBound(v, bounds(j))
    Next j
End Sub

Private Sub OmegaMutate(ByRef pop() As Double, idx As Long, _
                         ByRef bounds() As OmegaBounds, rate As Double)
    Dim j As Long, span As Double, v As Double
    For j = 0 To 6
        If Rnd < rate Then
            span = (bounds(j).hi - bounds(j).lo) * 0.2
            v = pop(idx, j) + (Rnd - 0.5) * 2# * span
            pop(idx, j) = OmegaClampBound(v, bounds(j))
        End If
    Next j
End Sub

'============================================================
' SIMULATED ANNEALING
'============================================================
Private Sub OmegaSA(ByRef x() As Double, ByRef bounds() As OmegaBounds, _
                    nSteps As Long, t0 As Double, _
                    t_start As Double, diary As Worksheet)
    Dim curFit As Double: curFit = OmegaFitnessVec(x)
    Dim bestX(0 To 6) As Double, bestFit As Double: bestFit = curFit
    Dim j As Long
    For j = 0 To 6: bestX(j) = x(j): Next j

    Dim stepI As Long, accepted As Long: accepted = 0
    For stepI = 1 To nSteps
        If (Timer - t_start) > OMEGA_MAX_TIME_SEC Then Exit For
        Dim t As Double
        t = t0 * ((1# - stepI / CDbl(nSteps)) ^ 2)
        If t < 0.001 Then t = 0.001

        Dim xNew(0 To 6) As Double
        For j = 0 To 6: xNew(j) = x(j): Next j
        Dim pIdx As Long: pIdx = Int(Rnd * 7)
        Dim span As Double: span = (bounds(pIdx).hi - bounds(pIdx).lo) * t * 0.35
        xNew(pIdx) = xNew(pIdx) + (Rnd - 0.5) * 2# * span
        xNew(pIdx) = OmegaClampBound(xNew(pIdx), bounds(pIdx))

        Dim newFit As Double: newFit = OmegaFitnessVec(xNew)
        Dim delta As Double: delta = newFit - curFit

        If delta > 0 Or Rnd < Exp(delta / t) Then
            For j = 0 To 6: x(j) = xNew(j): Next j
            curFit = newFit
            accepted = accepted + 1
            If newFit > bestFit Then
                bestFit = newFit
                For j = 0 To 6: bestX(j) = xNew(j): Next j
            End If
        End If

        If stepI Mod 50 = 0 Or stepI = nSteps Then
            OmegaLog diary, "SA-s" & stepI, "T=" & Format(t, "0.000") & " curFit=" & Format(curFit, "0.0000") & " bestFit=" & Format(bestFit, "0.0000") & " acceptRate=" & Format(accepted / stepI, "0.00")
        End If
    Next stepI

    For j = 0 To 6: x(j) = bestX(j): Next j
End Sub

'============================================================
' NELDER-MEAD SIMPLEX
'============================================================
Private Sub OmegaNelderMead(ByRef x() As Double, ByRef bounds() As OmegaBounds, _
                             nSteps As Long, t_start As Double, diary As Worksheet)
    Const Nd As Long = 7
    Dim simplex(0 To Nd, 0 To 6) As Double
    Dim simFit(0 To Nd) As Double
    Dim i As Long, j As Long

    For j = 0 To 6: simplex(0, j) = x(j): Next j
    simFit(0) = OmegaFitnessVec(x)
    For i = 1 To Nd
        For j = 0 To 6: simplex(i, j) = x(j): Next j
        Dim span As Double
        span = (bounds(i - 1).hi - bounds(i - 1).lo) * 0.1
        simplex(i, i - 1) = simplex(i, i - 1) + span
        simplex(i, i - 1) = OmegaClampBound(simplex(i, i - 1), bounds(i - 1))
        Dim tx(0 To 6) As Double
        For j = 0 To 6: tx(j) = simplex(i, j): Next j
        simFit(i) = OmegaFitnessVec(tx)
    Next i

    Const alpha As Double = 1#, gamma As Double = 2#, rho As Double = 0.5, sigma As Double = 0.5
    Dim stepI As Long
    For stepI = 1 To nSteps
        If (Timer - t_start) > OMEGA_MAX_TIME_SEC Then Exit For

        Call NMSimplexSort(simplex, simFit)

        Dim centroid(0 To 6) As Double
        For j = 0 To 6
            Dim sum As Double: sum = 0
            For i = 0 To Nd - 1: sum = sum + simplex(i, j): Next i
            centroid(j) = sum / CDbl(Nd)
        Next j

        ' Reflect
        Dim xr(0 To 6) As Double
        For j = 0 To 6
            xr(j) = centroid(j) + alpha * (centroid(j) - simplex(Nd, j))
            xr(j) = OmegaClampBound(xr(j), bounds(j))
        Next j
        Dim fr As Double: fr = OmegaFitnessVec(xr)

        If fr > simFit(0) Then
            ' Expand
            Dim xe(0 To 6) As Double
            For j = 0 To 6
                xe(j) = centroid(j) + gamma * (xr(j) - centroid(j))
                xe(j) = OmegaClampBound(xe(j), bounds(j))
            Next j
            Dim fe As Double: fe = OmegaFitnessVec(xe)
            If fe > fr Then
                For j = 0 To 6: simplex(Nd, j) = xe(j): Next j
                simFit(Nd) = fe
            Else
                For j = 0 To 6: simplex(Nd, j) = xr(j): Next j
                simFit(Nd) = fr
            End If
        ElseIf fr > simFit(Nd - 1) Then
            For j = 0 To 6: simplex(Nd, j) = xr(j): Next j
            simFit(Nd) = fr
        Else
            ' Contract
            Dim xc(0 To 6) As Double
            For j = 0 To 6
                xc(j) = centroid(j) + rho * (simplex(Nd, j) - centroid(j))
                xc(j) = OmegaClampBound(xc(j), bounds(j))
            Next j
            Dim fC As Double: fC = OmegaFitnessVec(xc)
            If fC > simFit(Nd) Then
                For j = 0 To 6: simplex(Nd, j) = xc(j): Next j
                simFit(Nd) = fC
            Else
                ' Shrink
                For i = 1 To Nd
                    For j = 0 To 6
                        simplex(i, j) = simplex(0, j) + sigma * (simplex(i, j) - simplex(0, j))
                        simplex(i, j) = OmegaClampBound(simplex(i, j), bounds(j))
                    Next j
                    Dim tx2(0 To 6) As Double
                    For j = 0 To 6: tx2(j) = simplex(i, j): Next j
                    simFit(i) = OmegaFitnessVec(tx2)
                Next i
            End If
        End If

        If stepI Mod 25 = 0 Or stepI = nSteps Then
            OmegaLog diary, "NM-s" & stepI, "bestFit=" & Format(simFit(0), "0.0000") & " worstFit=" & Format(simFit(Nd), "0.0000")
        End If
    Next stepI

    For j = 0 To 6: x(j) = simplex(0, j): Next j
End Sub

Private Sub NMSimplexSort(ByRef simplex() As Double, ByRef simFit() As Double)
    Dim n As Long: n = UBound(simFit)
    Dim i As Long, j As Long, kk As Long
    Dim keyF As Double, keyG(0 To 6) As Double
    For i = 1 To n
        keyF = simFit(i)
        For kk = 0 To 6: keyG(kk) = simplex(i, kk): Next kk
        j = i - 1
        Do While j >= 0
            If simFit(j) >= keyF Then Exit Do
            simFit(j + 1) = simFit(j)
            For kk = 0 To 6: simplex(j + 1, kk) = simplex(j, kk): Next kk
            j = j - 1
        Loop
        simFit(j + 1) = keyF
        For kk = 0 To 6: simplex(j + 1, kk) = keyG(kk): Next kk
    Next i
End Sub

'============================================================
' HELPERS MATEMATICOS
'============================================================
Private Function Bell(x As Double, center As Double, width As Double) As Double
    Dim d As Double: d = (x - center) / width
    Bell = Exp(-0.5 * d * d)
End Function

Private Function Clamp01(v As Double) As Double
    If v < 0 Then v = 0
    If v > 1 Then v = 1
    Clamp01 = v
End Function

Private Function LogSafe(v As Double) As Double
    If v < 0.001 Then v = 0.001
    LogSafe = Log(v)
End Function

Private Function OmegaClampBound(v As Double, b As OmegaBounds) As Double
    If v < b.lo Then v = b.lo
    If v > b.hi Then v = b.hi
    If b.isInt Then v = CDbl(CLng(v))
    OmegaClampBound = v
End Function

Private Sub OmegaSortDesc(ByRef pop() As Double, ByRef fit() As Double)
    Dim n As Long: n = UBound(fit)
    Dim i As Long, j As Long, k As Long
    Dim keyF As Double, keyG(0 To 6) As Double
    For i = 2 To n
        keyF = fit(i)
        For k = 0 To 6: keyG(k) = pop(i, k): Next k
        j = i - 1
        Do While j >= 1
            If fit(j) >= keyF Then Exit Do
            fit(j + 1) = fit(j)
            For k = 0 To 6: pop(j + 1, k) = pop(j, k): Next k
            j = j - 1
        Loop
        fit(j + 1) = keyF
        For k = 0 To 6: pop(j + 1, k) = keyG(k): Next k
    Next i
End Sub

Private Function ReadCell(sheet As String, addr As String, fallback As Double) As Double
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim v As Variant
    v = ThisWorkbook.sheets(sheet).Range(addr).value
    If isNumeric(v) Then ReadCell = CDbl(v) Else ReadCell = fallback
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Sub WriteCell(sheet As String, addr As String, value As Variant)
    On Error Resume Next
    ThisWorkbook.sheets(sheet).Range(addr).value = value
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Sub OmegaApplyConfig(ByRef pop() As Double, idx As Long)
    Call WriteCell("18_CONFIG", "C31", CLng(pop(idx, 0)))
    Call WriteCell("18_CONFIG", "C32", CLng(pop(idx, 1)))
    Call WriteCell("18_CONFIG", "C33", CLng(pop(idx, 2)))
    Call WriteCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H10", CLng(pop(idx, 3)))
    Call WriteCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H11", CLng(pop(idx, 4)))
    Call WriteCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H12", Round(pop(idx, 5), 4))
    Call WriteCell("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H13", Round(pop(idx, 6), 4))
End Sub

'============================================================
' DIARY LOGGING
'============================================================
Private Function OmegaDiarySetup() As Worksheet
    Const SHN As String = "00_SOLVER_DIARY"
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHN)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets.Add(After:=ThisWorkbook.sheets(ThisWorkbook.sheets.count))
        ws.name = SHN
    Else
        ws.Cells.Clear
    End If
    ws.Range("A1").value = "APE_OMEGA_MATH - Diario del Solver"
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Bold = True: ws.Range("A1").Font.size = 14
    ws.Range("A2").value = "Motor multi-fase LHS -> GA -> SA -> NM  |  Inicio: " & Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss")
    Dim hdrs As Variant
    hdrs = Array("Timestamp", "Fase", "Mensaje", "Notas")
    Dim c As Long
    For c = 0 To UBound(hdrs)
        ws.Cells(4, c + 1).value = hdrs(c)
        ws.Cells(4, c + 1).Font.Bold = True
        ws.Cells(4, c + 1).Interior.Color = RGB(31, 78, 121)
        ws.Cells(4, c + 1).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    Next c
    Set OmegaDiarySetup = ws
End Function

Private Sub OmegaLog(ws As Worksheet, phase As String, msg As String)
    Dim r As Long: r = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row + 1
    If r < 5 Then r = 5
    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = Format(Now, "hh:mm:ss.0")
    ws.Cells(r, 2).value = phase
    ws.Cells(r, 3).value = msg
End Sub

Private Sub OmegaSnapshotToDiary(ws As Worksheet, ByRef pop() As Double, fit As Double, label As String)
    Dim r As Long: r = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row + 1
    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = Format(Now, "hh:mm:ss.0")
    ws.Cells(r, 2).value = "SNAP"
    ws.Cells(r, 3).value = "Fitness=" & Format(fit, "0.000000") & "  |  buf=" & CLng(pop(1, 0)) & _
        " live=" & CLng(pop(1, 1)) & " rec=" & CLng(pop(1, 2)) & _
        " frag=" & CLng(pop(1, 3)) & " nud=" & CLng(pop(1, 4)) & _
        " abr=" & Round(pop(1, 5), 3) & " sync=" & Round(pop(1, 6), 3)
    ws.Cells(r, 4).value = label
    ws.Cells(r, 4).Interior.Color = RGB(255, 235, 156)
End Sub

'============================================================
' PER-PROFILE OPTIMIZER (Nivel C - added 2026-04-18)
' Reuses OmegaLHS/GA/SA/NM with profile-specific bounds from
' 6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES R591-R604. Persists calibrated knobs to
' R607-R616 col(profile).
'============================================================

Public Function Brain_OmegaOptimizer_PerProfile(ByVal profileId As String) As Double
    Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer
    On Error GoTo EH
    Application.StatusBar = "Per-Profile Omega [" & profileId & "]: init..."

    Dim bounds(0 To 6) As OmegaBounds
    If Not PerProfile_ReadBounds(profileId, bounds) Then
        Application.StatusBar = "Per-Profile [" & profileId & "] ABORT: bounds no validos"
        Brain_OmegaOptimizer_PerProfile = -1#
    ' === HOOK SSOT: enforce coherence + cascade ===
    On Error Resume Next
    Call Brain_EnforceCoherence(profileId)
    Call Brain_CascadeProfile(profileId)
    On Error GoTo EH

        Exit Function
    End If

    Dim diary As Worksheet: Set diary = OmegaDiarySetup()
    Call OmegaLog(diary, "PP-" & profileId & "-INIT", _
        "bufHi=" & bounds(0).hi & " recHi=" & bounds(2).hi & " syncHi=" & bounds(6).hi)

    Randomize
    Const PP_POP As Long = 40
    Dim pop(1 To PP_POP, 0 To 6) As Double
    Dim fit(1 To PP_POP) As Double

    ' FASE 1: Latin Hypercube Sampling per-profile bounds
    Application.StatusBar = "Per-Profile [" & profileId & "] F1: LHS..."
    Call OmegaLHS(pop, PP_POP, bounds)
    Call PerProfile_SeedFromExisting(profileId, pop, bounds)

    Dim i As Long
    For i = 1 To PP_POP: fit(i) = OmegaFitness(pop, i): Next i
    Call OmegaSortDesc(pop, fit)
    Call OmegaLog(diary, "PP-" & profileId & "-F1", "LHS best fit=" & Format(fit(1), "0.0000"))
    DoEvents

    ' FASE 2: GA
    Application.StatusBar = "Per-Profile [" & profileId & "] F2: GA..."
    Call OmegaGA(pop, fit, bounds, 80, t0, diary)
    Call OmegaLog(diary, "PP-" & profileId & "-F2", "GA best fit=" & Format(fit(1), "0.0000"))
    DoEvents

    ' FASE 3: SA
    Application.StatusBar = "Per-Profile [" & profileId & "] F3: SA..."
    Dim xBest(0 To 6) As Double, j As Long
    For j = 0 To 6: xBest(j) = pop(1, j): Next j
    Call OmegaSA(xBest, bounds, 300, 0.5, t0, diary)
    Dim fSA As Double: fSA = OmegaFitnessVec(xBest)
    If fSA > fit(1) Then
        For j = 0 To 6: pop(1, j) = xBest(j): Next j
        fit(1) = fSA
    End If
    DoEvents

    ' FASE 4: Nelder-Mead
    Application.StatusBar = "Per-Profile [" & profileId & "] F4: NM..."
    For j = 0 To 6: xBest(j) = pop(1, j): Next j
    Call OmegaNelderMead(xBest, bounds, 150, t0, diary)
    Dim fNM As Double: fNM = OmegaFitnessVec(xBest)
    If fNM > fit(1) Then
        For j = 0 To 6: pop(1, j) = xBest(j): Next j
        fit(1) = fNM
    End If

    ' Persist
    Call PerProfile_PersistKnobs(profileId, pop, fit(1), t0)
    Call OmegaLog(diary, "PP-" & profileId & "-DONE", _
        "fit=" & Format(fit(1), "0.0000") & " buf=" & CLng(pop(1, 0)) & _
        " del=" & CLng(pop(1, 1)) & " rec=" & CLng(pop(1, 2)) & _
        " time=" & Format(Timer - t0, "0.0") & "s")
    Application.StatusBar = "Per-Profile [" & profileId & "] DONE fit=" & Format(fit(1), "0.000")
    Brain_OmegaOptimizer_PerProfile = fit(1)
    Exit Function
EH:
    Dim ehMsg As String: ehMsg = "PerProfile[" & profileId & "] #" & Err.Number & ": " & Err.description
    On Error Resume Next
    Call WB_LogMsg(ehMsg)
    Dim ehWs As Worksheet: Set ehWs = ThisWorkbook.sheets("00_SOLVER_DIARY")
    If Not ehWs Is Nothing Then Call OmegaLog(ehWs, "PP-ERR", ehMsg)
    On Error GoTo 0
    Application.StatusBar = ehMsg
    Brain_OmegaOptimizer_PerProfile = -1#
End Function

'------------------------------------------------------------
Private Function PerProfile_ProfileCol(ByVal profileId As String) As Long
    Select Case UCase(profileId)
        Case "P0": PerProfile_ProfileCol = 2
        Case "P1": PerProfile_ProfileCol = 3
        Case "P2": PerProfile_ProfileCol = 4
        Case "P3": PerProfile_ProfileCol = 5
        Case "P4": PerProfile_ProfileCol = 6
        Case "P5": PerProfile_ProfileCol = 7
        Case Else: PerProfile_ProfileCol = 0
    End Select
End Function

Private Function PerProfile_ReadBounds(ByVal profileId As String, ByRef bounds() As OmegaBounds) As Boolean
    PerProfile_ReadBounds = False
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES")
    On Error GoTo 0
    If ws Is Nothing Then Exit Function

    Dim col As Long: col = PerProfile_ProfileCol(profileId)
    If col = 0 Then Exit Function

    ' Rows layout: R591-R604 (14 rows = 7 knobs x 2 bounds lo/hi)
    bounds(0).lo = CDbl(ws.Cells(591, col).Value2): bounds(0).hi = CDbl(ws.Cells(592, col).Value2): bounds(0).isInt = True
    bounds(1).lo = CDbl(ws.Cells(593, col).Value2): bounds(1).hi = CDbl(ws.Cells(594, col).Value2): bounds(1).isInt = True
    bounds(2).lo = CDbl(ws.Cells(595, col).Value2): bounds(2).hi = CDbl(ws.Cells(596, col).Value2): bounds(2).isInt = True
    bounds(3).lo = CDbl(ws.Cells(597, col).Value2): bounds(3).hi = CDbl(ws.Cells(598, col).Value2): bounds(3).isInt = True
    bounds(4).lo = CDbl(ws.Cells(599, col).Value2): bounds(4).hi = CDbl(ws.Cells(600, col).Value2): bounds(4).isInt = True
    bounds(5).lo = CDbl(ws.Cells(601, col).Value2): bounds(5).hi = CDbl(ws.Cells(602, col).Value2): bounds(5).isInt = False
    bounds(6).lo = CDbl(ws.Cells(603, col).Value2): bounds(6).hi = CDbl(ws.Cells(604, col).Value2): bounds(6).isInt = False

    Dim k As Long
    For k = 0 To 6
        If bounds(k).hi <= bounds(k).lo Then Exit Function
    Next k
    PerProfile_ReadBounds = True
End Function

Private Sub PerProfile_SeedFromExisting(ByVal profileId As String, ByRef pop() As Double, ByRef bounds() As OmegaBounds)
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES")
    On Error GoTo 0
    If ws Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    Dim col As Long: col = PerProfile_ProfileCol(profileId)
    If col = 0 Then Exit Sub

    Dim hasAny As Boolean: hasAny = False
    Dim v(0 To 6) As Double, k As Long
    For k = 0 To 6
        Dim s As String: s = CStr(ws.Cells(607 + k, col).Value2)
        If Len(s) > 0 And isNumeric(s) Then
            v(k) = OmegaClampBound(CDbl(s), bounds(k))
            hasAny = True
        Else
            v(k) = bounds(k).lo + (bounds(k).hi - bounds(k).lo) * 0.5
        End If
    Next k
    If hasAny Then
        For k = 0 To 6: pop(1, k) = v(k): Next k
    End If
End Sub

Private Sub PerProfile_PersistKnobs(ByVal profileId As String, ByRef pop() As Double, ByVal fitness As Double, ByVal t0 As Double)
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES")
    On Error GoTo 0
    If ws Is Nothing Then Exit Sub
    Dim col As Long: col = PerProfile_ProfileCol(profileId)
    If col = 0 Then Exit Sub

    Dim prevCalc As Long: prevCalc = Application.Calculation
    Dim prevEvents As Boolean: prevEvents = Application.EnableEvents
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.EnableEvents = False

    ws.Cells(607, col).Value2 = CLng(pop(1, 0))
    ws.Cells(608, col).Value2 = CLng(pop(1, 1))
    ws.Cells(609, col).Value2 = CLng(pop(1, 2))
    ws.Cells(610, col).Value2 = CLng(pop(1, 3))
    ws.Cells(611, col).Value2 = CLng(pop(1, 4))
    ws.Cells(612, col).Value2 = Round(pop(1, 5), 4)
    ws.Cells(613, col).Value2 = Round(pop(1, 6), 4)
    ws.Cells(614, col).Value2 = Round(fitness, 4)
    ws.Cells(615, col).Value2 = Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss")
    ws.Cells(616, col).Value2 = "LHS->GA->SA->NM " & Format(Timer - t0, "0.0") & "s"

    Application.Calculation = prevCalc
    Application.EnableEvents = prevEvents
End Sub

'============================================================
' PER-PROFILE CALCULATOR WRAPPERS
' Cambian Default_Profile temporalmente para evaluar contra
' un perfil especifico sin alterar el estado global.
'============================================================
Public Function Brain_CalcStreamingHealth_ForProfile(ByVal profileId As String) As Double
    Dim orig As String: orig = ConfigVal("Default_Profile")
    Call SetConfigVal("Default_Profile", profileId)
    Dim v As Double: v = Brain_CalcStreamingHealth()
    Call SetConfigVal("Default_Profile", orig)
    Brain_CalcStreamingHealth_ForProfile = v
End Function

Public Function Brain_CalcRiskScore_ForProfile(ByVal profileId As String) As Double
    Dim orig As String: orig = ConfigVal("Default_Profile")
    Call SetConfigVal("Default_Profile", profileId)
    Dim v As Double: v = Brain_CalcRiskScore()
    Call SetConfigVal("Default_Profile", orig)
    Brain_CalcRiskScore_ForProfile = v
End Function

Public Function Brain_CalcHeadroom_ForProfile(ByVal profileId As String) As Double
    Dim orig As String: orig = ConfigVal("Default_Profile")
    Call SetConfigVal("Default_Profile", profileId)
    Dim v As Double: v = Brain_CalcHeadroom()
    Call SetConfigVal("Default_Profile", orig)
    Brain_CalcHeadroom_ForProfile = v
End Function

Public Function Brain_PredictStallRate_ForProfile(ByVal profileId As String) As Double
    Dim orig As String: orig = ConfigVal("Default_Profile")
    Call SetConfigVal("Default_Profile", profileId)
    Dim v As Double: v = Brain_PredictStallRate()
    Call SetConfigVal("Default_Profile", orig)
    Brain_PredictStallRate_ForProfile = v
End Function

Public Function Brain_CalcWorstCaseAcrossProfiles() As Double
    Dim pr As Variant: pr = Array("P0", "P1", "P2", "P3", "P4", "P5")
    Dim worst As Double: worst = 10000#
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To UBound(pr)
        Dim h As Double: h = Brain_CalcStreamingHealth_ForProfile(CStr(pr(i)))
        If h < worst Then worst = h
    Next i
    Brain_CalcWorstCaseAcrossProfiles = worst
End Function



Public Function Brain_Debug_PerProfile(ByVal profileId As String) As String
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim diag As String
    diag = "START pid=" & profileId

    Dim col As Long: col = PerProfile_ProfileCol(profileId)
    diag = diag & " | col=" & col
    If col = 0 Then Brain_Debug_PerProfile = diag & " | FAIL col=0": Exit Function

    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES")
    diag = diag & " | ws_ok"

    Dim bounds(0 To 6) As OmegaBounds
    Dim ok As Boolean: ok = PerProfile_ReadBounds(profileId, bounds)
    diag = diag & " | ReadBounds=" & ok

    If ok Then
        diag = diag & " | buf[" & bounds(0).lo & "," & bounds(0).hi & "]"
        diag = diag & " | del[" & bounds(1).lo & "," & bounds(1).hi & "]"
        diag = diag & " | rec[" & bounds(2).lo & "," & bounds(2).hi & "]"
        diag = diag & " | abr[" & bounds(5).lo & "," & bounds(5).hi & "]"
    End If

    ' Ahora probar el diary setup (posible origen de error)
    Dim diary As Worksheet
    Set diary = OmegaDiarySetup()
    diag = diag & " | Diary=" & IIf(diary Is Nothing, "NIL", "OK")

    ' Probar LHS
    Const PP_POP As Long = 5
    Dim pop(1 To PP_POP, 0 To 6) As Double
    Call OmegaLHS(pop, PP_POP, bounds)
    diag = diag & " | LHS_ok pop(1,0)=" & pop(1, 0)

    ' Probar Fitness
    Dim fit As Double: fit = OmegaFitness(pop, 1)
    diag = diag & " | fit=" & Format(fit, "0.0000")

    Brain_Debug_PerProfile = diag & " | END_OK"
    Exit Function
EH:
    Brain_Debug_PerProfile = diag & " | ERR#" & Err.Number & ": " & Err.description
End Function
