VBA MACRO APE_CONVERGE_SIMPLE.bas 
in file: xl/vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/APE_CONVERGE_SIMPLE'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
'============================================================
' APE_CONVERGE_SIMPLE
' Motor de convergencia simplificado: aplica cambios predefinidos
' uno por uno, mide impacto real via Brain_RecalcAllSilent, y
' revierte el cambio si empeora. Al final ejecuta Brain_ExportToFrontend
' para producir el JSON calibrado que refleja el estado actual.
'
' Entrypoints:
'   Brain_ConvergeSimple   - auto, sin MsgBox
'   Brain_ConvergeStepByStep - pregunta cada cambio (para uso desde Excel)
'============================================================
Option Explicit

'============================================================
' Brain_ConvergeMatrix - knobs MATRIX 8 + NIVELES 2/3
' Usa Brain_FastRecalc (30x mas rapido que Brain_RecalcAllSilent)
'============================================================
Public Sub Brain_ConvergeMatrix()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Dim prevCalc As Long: prevCalc = Application.Calculation
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual

    Dim diary As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set diary = ThisWorkbook.sheets("00_CONVERGENCE_DIARY")
    On Error GoTo EH
    If diary Is Nothing Then
        Set diary = ThisWorkbook.sheets.Add(After:=ThisWorkbook.sheets(ThisWorkbook.sheets.count))
        diary.name = "00_CONVERGENCE_DIARY"
    End If

    Dim r As Long: r = diary.Cells(diary.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row + 1
    If r < 5 Then r = 5
    diary.Cells(r, 1).value = Format(Now, "hh:mm:ss")
    diary.Cells(r, 2).value = "MATRIX-START"
    diary.Cells(r, 3).value = "Brain_ConvergeMatrix: toca NIVEL 2 + NIVEL 3 + MATRIX 3D"
    r = r + 1

    Call Brain_FastRecalc
    Dim h0 As Double, r0 As Double
    h0 = ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD").Range("B5").value
    r0 = ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD").Range("B6").value
    diary.Cells(r, 1).value = Format(Now, "hh:mm:ss")
    diary.Cells(r, 2).value = "BASELINE"
    diary.Cells(r, 3).value = "H=" & h0 & "  R=" & r0
    r = r + 1

    Dim knobs As Variant
    knobs = Array( _
        Array("6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES", "C57", 60, "P1 bufferTargetSec -> 60 (saturar bufStab)"), _
        Array("6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES", "B57", 60, "P0 bufferTargetSec -> 60"), _
        Array("6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES", "D57", 60, "P2 bufferTargetSec -> 60"), _
        Array("6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES", "E57", 60, "P3 bufferTargetSec -> 60"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C36", 1#, "ABR_Maturity_Factor -> 1.0"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C37", 1#, "Error_Tree_Coverage -> 1.0"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C6", 500, "Client_Bandwidth_Mbps -> 500"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C31", 90, "buffer_seconds -> 90"), _
        Array("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H10", 20, "fragLoadPolicy_maxNumRetry -> 20"), _
        Array("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H11", 15, "nudgeMaxRetry -> 15"), _
        Array("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H12", 0.7, "abrBandWidthFactor -> 0.7"), _
        Array("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H13", 1.5, "maxLiveSyncPlaybackRate -> 1.5") _
    )

    Dim applied As Long, reverted As Long, skipped As Long
    applied = 0: reverted = 0: skipped = 0
    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(knobs) To UBound(knobs)
        DoEvents  '' anti-freeze: ceder control a Windows cada iter
        Application.StatusBar = "Converge knob " & (i + 1) & " de " & (UBound(knobs) + 1) & ": " & CStr(knobs(i)(3))
        Dim sheet As String: sheet = CStr(knobs(i)(0))
        Dim addr As String: addr = CStr(knobs(i)(1))
        Dim newV As Variant: newV = knobs(i)(2)
        Dim desc As String: desc = CStr(knobs(i)(3))
        Dim oldV As Variant
        On Error Resume Next
        oldV = ThisWorkbook.sheets(sheet).Range(addr).value
        On Error GoTo EH
        If CStr(oldV) = CStr(newV) Then
            diary.Cells(r, 1).value = Format(Now, "hh:mm:ss")
            diary.Cells(r, 2).value = "SKIP"
            diary.Cells(r, 3).value = desc & " (ya = " & CStr(newV) & ")"
            r = r + 1: skipped = skipped + 1: GoTo NextK
        End If
        ThisWorkbook.sheets(sheet).Range(addr).value = newV
        Call Brain_FastRecalc
        Dim h1 As Double, r1 As Double
        h1 = ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD").Range("B5").value
        r1 = ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD").Range("B6").value
        Dim score0 As Double: score0 = h0 - r0
        Dim score1 As Double: score1 = h1 - r1
        If score1 >= score0 - 0.01 Then
            diary.Cells(r, 1).value = Format(Now, "hh:mm:ss")
            diary.Cells(r, 2).value = "APPLIED"
            diary.Cells(r, 3).value = desc
            diary.Cells(r, 5).value = "H:" & h0 & "->" & h1 & "  R:" & r0 & "->" & r1
            applied = applied + 1: h0 = h1: r0 = r1
        Else
            ThisWorkbook.sheets(sheet).Range(addr).value = oldV
            Call Brain_FastRecalc
            diary.Cells(r, 1).value = Format(Now, "hh:mm:ss")
            diary.Cells(r, 2).value = "REVERT"
            diary.Cells(r, 3).value = desc
            diary.Cells(r, 5).value = "empeoro score " & Format(score1 - score0, "0.0")
            reverted = reverted + 1
        End If
        r = r + 1
NextK:
    Next i
    diary.Cells(r, 1).value = Format(Now, "hh:mm:ss")
    diary.Cells(r, 2).value = "MATRIX-END"
    diary.Cells(r, 3).value = "applied=" & applied & " reverted=" & reverted & " skipped=" & skipped
    diary.Cells(r, 5).value = "H=" & h0 & " R=" & r0
    diary.Columns("A:E").AutoFit

    Application.Calculation = prevCalc
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.Calculation = prevCalc
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_ConvergeSimple()
    Call ConvSimpleCore(True, False)
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_ConvergeStepByStep()
    Call ConvSimpleCore(False, False)
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_ConvergeAndExport()
    Call ConvSimpleCore(True, False)   ' doExport=False - export desligado del paso 02
End Sub

Private Sub ConvSimpleCore(autoMode As Boolean, doExport As Boolean)
    On Error GoTo EH
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    WIRING_SILENT = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual

    Dim diary As Worksheet
    Set diary = CSimpleDiary()

    Dim logRow As Long: logRow = 5
    Call CSimpleLog(diary, logRow, "INIT", "Modo=" & IIf(autoMode, "AUTO", "STEP") & " Export=" & doExport, "")
    logRow = logRow + 1

    Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_RecalcAllSilent"
    Dim h0 As Double, r0 As Double, hd0 As Double, s0 As Double
    h0 = CSRead("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "B5")
    r0 = CSRead("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "B6")
    hd0 = CSRead("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "B7")
    s0 = CSRead("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "B8")
    Call CSimpleLog(diary, logRow, "BASELINE", _
        "Health=" & h0 & " Risk=" & r0 & " Headroom=" & hd0 & " Stall=" & s0, _
        "targets: H>=95 R<=10 HD>=300 S<0.67")
    logRow = logRow + 1

    ' Lista de knobs: cada uno = Array(sheet, addr, newValue, descripcion)
    ' Priorizados por impacto probable. Los no aplicables se saltan solos.
    Dim knobs As Variant
    knobs = Array( _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C31", 90, "Subir buffer_seconds a 90"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C31", 120, "Subir buffer_seconds a 120"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C32", 15, "Subir live_delay_seconds a 15"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C32", 20, "Subir live_delay_seconds a 20"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C33", 150, "Subir reconnect_attempts a 150"), _
        Array("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H10", 20, "fragLoadPolicy_maxNumRetry=20"), _
        Array("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H11", 15, "nudgeMaxRetry=15"), _
        Array("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H12", 0.75, "abrBandWidthFactor=0.75"), _
        Array("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H12", 0.65, "abrBandWidthFactor=0.65"), _
        Array("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H13", 1.3, "maxLiveSyncPlaybackRate=1.3"), _
        Array("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "H13", 1.5, "maxLiveSyncPlaybackRate=1.5"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C16", "keep-alive", "Connection_Default=keep-alive"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C14", "https://www.netflix.com/", "Referer netflix"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C15", "https://www.netflix.com", "Origin netflix"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C13", "Mozilla/5.0 (Web0S; Linux/SmartTV) AppleWebKit/537.36 Chrome/91 Safari/537.36", "UA Web0S"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C21", True, "Strip_Spoofed_IPs=True"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C22", True, "Sanitize_Single_Value=True"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C23", True, "Force_Web0S_If_APE=True"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C24", True, "Force_Netflix_If_DuckDNS=True"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C25", True, "Always_Include_Port=True"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C18", 8, "EXTHTTP_Cap_KB=8"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C11", "P1", "Default_Profile=P1"), _
        Array("18_CONFIG", "C12", "MAX_COMPAT", "Default_Mode=MAX_COMPAT") _
    )

    Dim applied As Long, reverted As Long, skipped As Long
    applied = 0: reverted = 0: skipped = 0

    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(knobs) To UBound(knobs)
        DoEvents  '' anti-freeze: ceder control a Windows cada iter
        Application.StatusBar = "Converge knob " & (i + 1) & " de " & (UBound(knobs) + 1) & ": " & CStr(knobs(i)(3))
        Dim sheet As String: sheet = CStr(knobs(i)(0))
        Dim addr As String: addr = CStr(knobs(i)(1))
        Dim newV As Variant: newV = knobs(i)(2)
        Dim desc As String: desc = CStr(knobs(i)(3))

        Dim oldV As Variant
        On Error Resume Next
        oldV = ThisWorkbook.sheets(sheet).Range(addr).value
        On Error GoTo EH

        Dim sameVal As Boolean: sameVal = False
        On Error Resume Next
        If CStr(oldV) = CStr(newV) Then sameVal = True
        If Err.Number <> 0 Then Err.Clear
        On Error GoTo EH
        If sameVal Then
            Call CSimpleLog(diary, logRow, "SKIP", desc, "ya esta en " & CStr(newV))
            logRow = logRow + 1
            skipped = skipped + 1
            GoTo NextK
        End If

        ' Modo step-by-step: pregunta antes
        If Not autoMode Then
            Application.EnableEvents = True   ' permitir MsgBox
            Application.ScreenUpdating = True
            Dim resp As VbMsgBoxResult
            resp = MsgBox( _
                "Knob " & (i + 1) & " de " & (UBound(knobs) + 1) & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
                "Estado actual:" & vbCrLf & _
                "  Health=" & h0 & "  Risk=" & r0 & vbCrLf & _
                "  Headroom=" & hd0 & "  Stall=" & s0 & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
                "CAMBIO PROPUESTO: " & desc & vbCrLf & _
                "Celda: " & sheet & "!" & addr & vbCrLf & _
                "De: " & CStr(oldV) & vbCrLf & _
                "A:  " & CStr(newV) & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
                "SI = aplicar y medir impacto real" & vbCrLf & _
                "NO = saltar este knob" & vbCrLf & _
                "CANCELAR = terminar el motor", _
                vbYesNoCancel + vbQuestion, "APE Converge - Paso " & (i + 1))
            Application.EnableEvents = False
            Application.ScreenUpdating = False
            If resp = vbCancel Then
                Call CSimpleLog(diary, logRow, "USER-CANCEL", "Iter " & (i + 1), "")
                Exit For
            ElseIf resp = vbNo Then
                Call CSimpleLog(diary, logRow, "USER-SKIP", desc, "")
                logRow = logRow + 1
                skipped = skipped + 1
                GoTo NextK
            End If
        End If

        ' Aplicar
        ThisWorkbook.sheets(sheet).Range(addr).value = newV
        Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_RecalcAllSilent"
        Dim h1 As Double, r1 As Double, hd1 As Double, s1 As Double
        h1 = CSRead("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "B5")
        r1 = CSRead("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "B6")
        hd1 = CSRead("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "B7")
        s1 = CSRead("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "B8")

        ' Score: +health, -risk, -stall, +headroom si <300
        Dim scoreBefore As Double, scoreAfter As Double
        scoreBefore = h0 - r0 - (s0 * 100#) + CSBonusHeadroom(hd0)
        scoreAfter = h1 - r1 - (s1 * 100#) + CSBonusHeadroom(hd1)

        If scoreAfter >= scoreBefore - 0.01 Then
            ' Mejora o neutro: conservar
            Call CSimpleLog(diary, logRow, "APPLIED", desc, _
                "H:" & h0 & "->" & h1 & " R:" & r0 & "->" & r1 & " score:" & Format(scoreBefore, "0.0") & "->" & Format(scoreAfter, "0.0"))
            applied = applied + 1
            h0 = h1: r0 = r1: hd0 = hd1: s0 = s1
        Else
            ' Empeora: revertir
            ThisWorkbook.sheets(sheet).Range(addr).value = oldV
            Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_RecalcAllSilent"
            Call CSimpleLog(diary, logRow, "REVERT", desc, _
                "H:" & h0 & "->" & h1 & " R:" & r0 & "->" & r1 & " score bajo " & Format(scoreAfter - scoreBefore, "0.0"))
            reverted = reverted + 1
        End If
        logRow = logRow + 1
NextK:
    Next i

    ' Final
    Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_RecalcAllSilent"
    h0 = CSRead("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "B5")
    r0 = CSRead("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "B6")
    hd0 = CSRead("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "B7")
    s0 = CSRead("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD", "B8")

    Dim sufri As String
    sufri = ""
    If h0 < 95 Then sufri = sufri & "Health=" & h0 & "/95 (FALTA " & (95 - h0) & "), "
    If r0 > 10 Then sufri = sufri & "Risk=" & r0 & "/<=10 (SOBRA " & (r0 - 10) & "), "
    If hd0 < 300 Then sufri = sufri & "Headroom=" & hd0 & "/300, "
    If s0 > 0.67 Then sufri = sufri & "Stall=" & s0 & "/<0.67, "
    If sufri = "" Then sufri = "TODOS TARGETS CUMPLIDOS"
    Call CSimpleLog(diary, logRow, "FINAL", _
        "H=" & h0 & " R=" & r0 & " HD=" & hd0 & " S=" & s0, sufri)
    logRow = logRow + 1
    Call CSimpleLog(diary, logRow, "STATS", _
        "aplicados=" & applied & "  revertidos=" & reverted & "  saltados=" & skipped, "")
    logRow = logRow + 1

    ' Export JSON si corresponde
    If doExport Then
        On Error Resume Next
        Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_ExportToFrontendPlus"
        If Err.Number <> 0 Then
            Call CSimpleLog(diary, logRow, "EXPORT-ERR", Err.Number & ": " & Err.description, "")
            Err.Clear
        Else
            Call CSimpleLog(diary, logRow, "EXPORT-OK", "Brain_ExportToFrontend ejecutado", "JSON producido")
        End If
        On Error GoTo EH
        logRow = logRow + 1
    End If

    diary.Columns("A:E").AutoFit
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.StatusBar = False   ' limpiar progreso
    WIRING_SILENT = False
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not diary Is Nothing Then
        Call CSimpleLog(diary, logRow, "ERROR", Err.Number & " - " & Err.description, "")
    End If
End Sub

Private Function CSRead(sheet As String, addr As String) As Double
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim v As Variant
    v = ThisWorkbook.sheets(sheet).Range(addr).value
    If isNumeric(v) Then
        CSRead = CDbl(v)
    Else
        ' "100%" -> 100
        Dim s As String: s = CStr(v)
        s = Replace(s, "%", "")
        If isNumeric(s) Then CSRead = CDbl(s) Else CSRead = 0
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function CSBonusHeadroom(h As Double) As Double
    If h >= 300 Then CSBonusHeadroom = 0 Else CSBonusHeadroom = (300 - h) * (-0.1)
End Function

Private Function CSimpleDiary() As Worksheet
    Const SHN As String = "00_CONVERGENCE_DIARY"
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHN)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets.Add(After:=ThisWorkbook.sheets(ThisWorkbook.sheets.count))
        ws.name = SHN
    Else
        ws.Cells.Clear
    End If
    ws.Range("A1").value = "APE_CONVERGE_SIMPLE - Diario iterativo"
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Bold = True: ws.Range("A1").Font.size = 14
    ws.Range("A2").value = "Inicio: " & Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss")
    ws.Range("A4").value = "Timestamp": ws.Range("B4").value = "Accion"
    ws.Range("C4").value = "Descripcion": ws.Range("D4").value = "Detalle"
    ws.Range("E4").value = "Notas"
    ws.Range("A4:E4").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("A4:E4").Interior.Color = RGB(31, 78, 121)
    ws.Range("A4:E4").Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    Set CSimpleDiary = ws
End Function

Private Sub CSimpleLog(ws As Worksheet, row As Long, action As String, desc As String, notes As String)
    ws.Cells(row, 1).value = Format(Now, "hh:mm:ss")
    ws.Cells(row, 2).value = action
    ws.Cells(row, 3).value = desc
    ws.Cells(row, 5).value = notes
    Select Case action
        Case "APPLIED": ws.Cells(row, 2).Interior.Color = RGB(146, 208, 80)
        Case "REVERT": ws.Cells(row, 2).Interior.Color = RGB(248, 105, 107)
        Case "SKIP", "USER-SKIP": ws.Cells(row, 2).Interior.Color = RGB(189, 215, 238)
        Case "USER-CANCEL": ws.Cells(row, 2).Interior.Color = RGB(255, 192, 0)
        Case "BASELINE", "FINAL": ws.Cells(row, 2).Interior.Color = RGB(255, 235, 156)
        Case "ERROR", "EXPORT-ERR": ws.Cells(row, 2).Interior.Color = RGB(192, 0, 0): ws.Cells(row, 2).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
        Case "EXPORT-OK": ws.Cells(row, 2).Interior.Color = RGB(146, 208, 80)
    End Select
End Sub

VBA MACRO APE_OMEGA_GUARANTEES.bas 
in file: xl/vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/APE_OMEGA_GUARANTEES'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
'============================================================
' APE_OMEGA_GUARANTEES
' Implementa la DOGMA LOGICA INQUEBRANTABLE: 10 Garantias OMEGA
' del ecosistema IPTV Navigator PRO v5.4 (1% Uniqueness Level).
'
' Cada lista M3U8 generada se audita contra 10 reglas obligatorias.
' El resultado se integra en el calculo de Streaming Health via
' multiplicador omegaCompliance (0.5..1.0).
'
' Entrypoints:
'   Brain_AuditTenGuarantees(m3u8Text) As Long  -> score 0..10
'   Brain_OmegaScorecard()                      -> corre audit sobre
'                                                 Output_M3U8_Path y
'                                                 escribe 00_OMEGA_SCORECARD
'   Brain_GetOmegaCompliance() As Double        -> score/10 en [0..1]
'   Brain_InjectOmegaTags()                     -> completa tags faltantes
'============================================================
Option Explicit

Private Const SHEET_SCORECARD As String = "00_OMEGA_SCORECARD"

'============================================================
' ReadFileAll optimizada con ADODB.Stream (v2, para M3U8 grandes)
'============================================================
Public Function ReadFileAll(path As String) As String
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim stream As Object
    Set stream = CreateObject("ADODB.Stream")
    stream.Charset = "utf-8"
    stream.Open
    stream.LoadFromFile path
    ReadFileAll = stream.ReadText
    stream.Close
    Set stream = Nothing
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then
        Err.Clear
        ' Fallback: Line Input (lento pero seguro)
        Dim fn As Integer: fn = FreeFile
        Open path For Input As #fn
        Dim s As String, ln As String
        Do While Not EOF(fn)
            Line Input #fn, ln
            s = s & ln & vbCrLf
        Loop
        Close #fn
        ReadFileAll = s
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

'------------------------------------------------------------
' AUDITORIA: escanea un texto M3U8 y devuelve cuantas garantias
' de las 10 estan presentes (regex style InStr).
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function Brain_AuditTenGuarantees(m3u8text As String) As Long
    Dim n As Long: n = 0
    If G1_AntiFreezeNuclear(m3u8text) Then n = n + 1
    If G2_MultiSource(m3u8text) Then n = n + 1
    If G3_BufferStrategyNuclear(m3u8text) Then n = n + 1
    If G4_LiveCatchUpSync(m3u8text) Then n = n + 1
    If G5_UrlPurity(m3u8text) Then n = n + 1
    If G6_MaxAbrGreedy(m3u8text) Then n = n + 1
    If G7_MaxImagenHdr(m3u8text) Then n = n + 1
    If G8_HttpErrorTree(m3u8text) Then n = n + 1
    If G9_MultiProvider(m3u8text) Then n = n + 1
    If G10_MasterIntegrator(m3u8text) Then n = n + 1
    Brain_AuditTenGuarantees = n
End Function

Private Function G1_AntiFreezeNuclear(s As String) As Boolean
    G1_AntiFreezeNuclear = _
        InStr(s, "#EXT-X-APE-ANTI-FREEZE-NUCLEAR") > 0 And _
        InStr(s, "network-continuous-stream=true") > 0
End Function

Private Function G2_MultiSource(s As String) As Boolean
    G2_MultiSource = _
        InStr(s, "#EXT-X-APE-MULTI-SOURCE") > 0 And _
        InStr(s, "network-reconnect-count=99") > 0
End Function

Private Function G3_BufferStrategyNuclear(s As String) As Boolean
    G3_BufferStrategyNuclear = _
        InStr(s, "NUCLEAR_NO_COMPROMISE") > 0 And _
        InStr(s, "network-caching=60000") > 0 And _
        InStr(s, "sout-mux-caching=60000") > 0
End Function

Private Function G4_LiveCatchUpSync(s As String) As Boolean
    G4_LiveCatchUpSync = _
        InStr(s, "catchup=""flussonic""") > 0 And _
        InStr(s, "clock-synchro=0") > 0 And _
        InStr(s, "LIVE-CATCHUP") > 0
End Function

Private Function G5_UrlPurity(s As String) As Boolean
    ' No doble ?? ni &&, ni trailing / en URLs (heuristico: no "/?" ni "/&")
    G5_UrlPurity = _
        InStr(s, "??") = 0 And _
        InStr(s, "&&") = 0 And _
        InStr(s, "/?") = 0
End Function

Private Function G6_MaxAbrGreedy(s As String) As Boolean
    G6_MaxAbrGreedy = _
        InStr(s, "QMAX-STRATEGY") > 0 And _
        InStr(UCase(s), "GREEDY") > 0
End Function

Private Function G7_MaxImagenHdr(s As String) As Boolean
    G7_MaxImagenHdr = _
        InStr(s, "X-HDR-MAX-CLL=5000") > 0 And _
        InStr(s, "avcodec-hw=any") > 0
End Function

Private Function G8_HttpErrorTree(s As String) As Boolean
    G8_HttpErrorTree = _
        InStr(s, "OTT Navigator") > 0 And _
        InStr(s, "405-REWRITE-TO-HEAD") > 0 And _
        InStr(s, "http-forward-cookies=true") > 0
End Function

Private Function G9_MultiProvider(s As String) As Boolean
    G9_MultiProvider = _
        InStr(s, "#EXT-X-CONTENT-STEERING") > 0
End Function

Private Function G10_MasterIntegrator(s As String) As Boolean
    G10_MasterIntegrator = _
        InStr(s, "OMEGA_BUILD") > 0 And _
        InStr(s, "absolute-hardening-lock") > 0
End Function

'------------------------------------------------------------
' SCORECARD: corre el audit contra Output_M3U8_Path y escribe
' hoja 00_OMEGA_SCORECARD con detalle por cada una de las 10.
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Sub Brain_OmegaScorecard()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim path As String
    path = ConfigVal("Output_M3U8_Path")
    If path = "" Or Len(dir(path)) = 0 Then
        ' Fallback: si no existe, genera scorecard con puntaje 0 + mensaje
        Call WriteScorecard("(archivo no encontrado: " & path & ")", 0, False, False, False, False, False, False, False, False, False, False)
        Call WriteConfigOmegaScore(0)
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim txt As String
    txt = ReadFileAll(path)

    Dim g1 As Boolean, g2 As Boolean, g3 As Boolean, g4 As Boolean, g5 As Boolean
    Dim g6 As Boolean, g7 As Boolean, g8 As Boolean, g9 As Boolean, g10 As Boolean
    g1 = G1_AntiFreezeNuclear(txt): g2 = G2_MultiSource(txt)
    g3 = G3_BufferStrategyNuclear(txt): g4 = G4_LiveCatchUpSync(txt)
    g5 = G5_UrlPurity(txt): g6 = G6_MaxAbrGreedy(txt)
    g7 = G7_MaxImagenHdr(txt): g8 = G8_HttpErrorTree(txt)
    g9 = G9_MultiProvider(txt): g10 = G10_MasterIntegrator(txt)

    Dim score As Long: score = 0
    If g1 Then score = score + 1
    If g2 Then score = score + 1
    If g3 Then score = score + 1
    If g4 Then score = score + 1
    If g5 Then score = score + 1
    If g6 Then score = score + 1
    If g7 Then score = score + 1
    If g8 Then score = score + 1
    If g9 Then score = score + 1
    If g10 Then score = score + 1

    Call WriteScorecard(path, Len(txt), g1, g2, g3, g4, g5, g6, g7, g8, g9, g10)
    Call WriteConfigOmegaScore(score)
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Call WB_LogMsg("Brain_OmegaScorecard ERROR: " & Err.description)
End Sub

Private Sub WriteScorecard(ByVal path As String, ByVal size As Long, _
    g1 As Boolean, g2 As Boolean, g3 As Boolean, g4 As Boolean, g5 As Boolean, _
    g6 As Boolean, g7 As Boolean, g8 As Boolean, g9 As Boolean, g10 As Boolean)

    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(SHEET_SCORECARD)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets.Add(After:=ThisWorkbook.sheets(ThisWorkbook.sheets.count))
        ws.name = SHEET_SCORECARD
    Else
        ws.Cells.Clear
    End If

    ws.Range("A1").value = "GOD-TIER 10 GUARANTEES ENGINE - Scorecard"
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Bold = True: ws.Range("A1").Font.size = 14
    ws.Range("A2").value = "Archivo auditado: " & path
    ws.Range("A3").value = "Tamano: " & size & " chars  |  Auditado: " & Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss")

    Dim hdrs As Variant
    hdrs = Array("#", "Garantia", "Status", "Mecanismo")
    Dim c As Long
    For c = 0 To UBound(hdrs)
        ws.Cells(5, c + 1).value = hdrs(c)
        ws.Cells(5, c + 1).Font.Bold = True
        ws.Cells(5, c + 1).Interior.Color = RGB(31, 78, 121)
        ws.Cells(5, c + 1).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    Next c

    Dim rows As Variant
    rows = Array( _
        Array("G1", "0 FREEZES (Gap-Controller)", g1, "#EXT-X-APE-ANTI-FREEZE-NUCLEAR + network-continuous-stream=true"), _
        Array("G2", "0 CORTES (ABR + Redundant URLs)", g2, "#EXT-X-APE-MULTI-SOURCE + network-reconnect-count=99"), _
        Array("G3", "0 REBUFFER (4-Layer Buffer)", g3, "NUCLEAR_NO_COMPROMISE + network-caching=60000 + sout-mux-caching=60000"), _
        Array("G4", "0 LAG (Live Catch-Up Sync)", g4, "catchup=flussonic + clock-synchro=0 + LIVE-CATCHUP"), _
        Array("G5", "0 BUGS CMCD (URL Purity)", g5, "No doble ?? ni && ni /? trails"), _
        Array("G6", "MAX ABR (Greedy Dinamico)", g6, "QMAX-STRATEGY + GREEDY"), _
        Array("G7", "MAX IMAGEN (HDR Supremacy)", g7, "X-HDR-MAX-CLL=5000 + avcodec-hw=any"), _
        Array("G8", "HTTP Error Tree (Evasion L7)", g8, "OTT Navigator UA + 405-REWRITE-TO-HEAD + http-forward-cookies=true"), _
        Array("G9", "Multi-Provider Integration", g9, "#EXT-X-CONTENT-STEERING"), _
        Array("G10", "Master Integrator (OMEGA_BUILD)", g10, "OMEGA_BUILD + absolute-hardening-lock") _
    )
    Dim i As Long
    For i = 0 To UBound(rows)
        ws.Cells(6 + i, 1).value = rows(i)(0)
        ws.Cells(6 + i, 2).value = rows(i)(1)
        Dim ok As Boolean: ok = CBool(rows(i)(2))
        If ok Then
            ws.Cells(6 + i, 3).value = "OK"
            ws.Cells(6 + i, 3).Interior.Color = RGB(146, 208, 80)
        Else
            ws.Cells(6 + i, 3).value = "FALTA"
            ws.Cells(6 + i, 3).Interior.Color = RGB(248, 105, 107)
            ws.Cells(6 + i, 3).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
        End If
        ws.Cells(6 + i, 4).value = rows(i)(3)
    Next i

    ' Fila de total
    Dim total As Long: total = 0
    For i = 0 To 9
        If CBool(rows(i)(2)) Then total = total + 1
    Next i
    ws.Cells(17, 1).value = "SCORE"
    ws.Cells(17, 1).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Cells(17, 2).value = "OMEGA COMPLIANCE"
    ws.Cells(17, 2).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Cells(17, 3).value = total & " / 10  (" & Format(total / 10#, "0%") & ")"
    ws.Cells(17, 3).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Cells(17, 3).Font.size = 14
    If total = 10 Then
        ws.Cells(17, 3).Interior.Color = RGB(146, 208, 80)
        ws.Cells(17, 4).value = "1% UNIQUENESS ACHIEVED - Despliegue listo"
        ws.Cells(17, 4).Font.Bold = True
    ElseIf total >= 7 Then
        ws.Cells(17, 3).Interior.Color = RGB(255, 235, 156)
        ws.Cells(17, 4).value = "Aceptable, pero sub-optimal. Degradado parcial."
    Else
        ws.Cells(17, 3).Interior.Color = RGB(248, 105, 107)
        ws.Cells(17, 3).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
        ws.Cells(17, 4).value = "DOWNGRADE: arquitectura fallando. Ver garantias en FALTA."
    End If

    ws.Columns("A:D").AutoFit
End Sub

'------------------------------------------------------------
' ESCRITURA DEL SCORE OMEGA EN 18_CONFIG para que Brain_CalcStreamingHealth
' lo pueda leer via ConfigVal.
'------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub WriteConfigOmegaScore(score As Long)
    Dim cfg As Worksheet: Set cfg = ThisWorkbook.sheets("18_CONFIG")
    Dim lastR As Long: lastR = cfg.Cells(cfg.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    ' Buscar existente
    Dim r As Long, found As Long: found = 0
    For r = 3 To lastR
        If LCase(CStr(cfg.Cells(r, 2).value)) = LCase("Omega_Compliance_Score") Then
            found = r: Exit For
        End If
    Next r
    If found = 0 Then
        found = lastR + 1
        cfg.Cells(found, 1).value = "X"
        cfg.Cells(found, 2).value = "Omega_Compliance_Score"
        cfg.Cells(found, 4).value = "Score 0-10 del audit de 10 Garantias OMEGA. Multiplica Health."
    End If
    cfg.Cells(found, 3).value = score
End Sub

'------------------------------------------------------------
' GET COMPLIANCE: 0..1. Lee de 18_CONFIG!Omega_Compliance_Score / 10
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function Brain_GetOmegaCompliance() As Double
    Dim s As String: s = ConfigVal("Omega_Compliance_Score")
    If s = "" Or Not isNumeric(s) Then
        Brain_GetOmegaCompliance = 1#  ' asumir compliance total si no hay audit
    Else
        Brain_GetOmegaCompliance = CDbl(s) / 10#
    End If
End Function

VBA MACRO APE_WIRING.bas 
in file: xl/vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/APE_WIRING'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
'============================================================
' APE_WIRING
' Integridad referencial + orquestador de macros para APE M3U8 LAB.
'
' Responsabilidades:
'   1. Grafo de dependencias (source -> targets) entre hojas.
'      Fuente de verdad en VBA (no en celdas, para que no se corrompa).
'   2. Propagacion automatica sin generar ciclos.
'   3. Ejecutor batch de todas las macros Brain_* con reporte.
'   4. Supresion de MsgBox modales cuando WIRING_SILENT = True.
'============================================================
Option Explicit

Public WIRING_SILENT As Boolean
Public WIRING_BUSY As Boolean

Private Const REPORT_SHEET As String = "00_MACRO_RUN_REPORT"
Private Const LOG_SHEET As String = "00_WIRING_LOG"
Private Const DIARY_SHEET As String = "00_SOLVER_DIARY"


' --------------------------------------------------------------------
' Brain_Step03_AuditM3U8_ModernUI — Audit con UI WPF moderna pixel-perfect
' Usa C:\tmp\scorecard_modern_ui.ps1 (wrapper WPF) que delega a
' scorecard_v2_full.ps1 (intacto). NO modifica el flujo Progressive existente.
'
' Diferencia con Brain_Step03_AuditM3U8_Progressive:
'   - Progressive: UserForm VBA simple, polling 400ms
'   - ModernUI:    Ventana WPF nativa con design pixel-perfect, polling 250ms
'                  Cancel button rojo + click X de la macOS-style title bar
'
' Cancel cleanup: scorecard_modern_ui.ps1 mata su child PS y borra temp files.
' --------------------------------------------------------------------
Public Sub Brain_Step03_AuditM3U8_ModernUI()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim m3u8Path As String
    m3u8Path = CStr(ThisWorkbook.sheets("18_CONFIG").Cells(30, 3).Value2)
    If Len(m3u8Path) = 0 Then
        MsgBox "18_CONFIG!C30 (Output_M3U8_Path) esta vacio. Completalo antes de auditar.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim wrapperScript As String
    wrapperScript = "C:\tmp\scorecard_modern_ui.ps1"
    If dir(wrapperScript) = "" Then
        MsgBox "No existe el wrapper WPF: " & wrapperScript & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
               "Crea o restaura ese archivo antes de usar ModernUI.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim cmd As String
    cmd = "powershell.exe -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -WindowStyle Hidden -File """ & _
          wrapperScript & """ -m3u8 """ & m3u8Path & """"

    Application.StatusBar = "Lanzando audit con UI moderna (WPF)..."
    Dim sh As Object: Set sh = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
    ' Window=0 hidden para ocultar PS host; la ventana WPF abre por sí sola
    ' Wait=True bloquea hasta que la ventana WPF se cierre
    sh.Run cmd, 0, True
    Application.StatusBar = False

    Call MarkStep("03", True)
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then
        MsgBox "Audit ModernUI completado. Revisa hoja 00_OMEGA_SCORECARD.", vbInformation, "AUDIT M3U8"
    End If
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.StatusBar = False
    MsgBox "ERROR Brain_Step03_AuditM3U8_ModernUI: " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub



' --------------------------------------------------------------------
' CleanupAuditAfterCancel — limpieza total tras cancelar audit M3U8
' Mata PS, borra temp files, resetea Application state, libera memoria.
' Llamado desde:
'   - btnCancel_Click via cancel flag check en main loop
'   - QueryClose (X del form) via cancel flag
'   - Error handler EH
'   - Timeout
' --------------------------------------------------------------------
Public Sub CleanupAuditAfterCancel(ByVal psPid As Long, ByVal progFile As String, ByVal pidFile As String)
    On Error Resume Next

    ' 1) Matar PS process (si tenemos PID)
    If psPid > 0 Then
        Call KillPsPid(psPid)
        Call WB_LogMsg("CleanupAuditAfterCancel: KillPsPid(" & psPid & ")")
    End If

    ' 2) Borrar temp files (progress + pid)
    Dim fso As Object: Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If fso.FileExists(progFile) Then
        fso.DeleteFile progFile, True
        Call WB_LogMsg("CleanupAuditAfterCancel: deleted " & progFile)
    End If
    If fso.FileExists(pidFile) Then
        fso.DeleteFile pidFile, True
        Call WB_LogMsg("CleanupAuditAfterCancel: deleted " & pidFile)
    End If

    ' 3) Restaurar Application state (anti-freeze guarantee)
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.StatusBar = False
    Application.Cursor = xlDefault

    ' 4) Resetear flags globales del wiring
    On Error Resume Next
    WIRING_SILENT = False
    On Error GoTo 0

    ' 5) Liberar instancia del UserForm para next run sin residuo
    On Error Resume Next
    If Not frmProgress Is Nothing Then
        Unload frmProgress
        Set frmProgress = Nothing
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0

    ' 6) Force GC (best effort en VBA)
    DoEvents
    DoEvents

    ' 7) Notificar
    Application.StatusBar = "Audit M3U8 cancelado - memoria liberada, listo para nuevo run"
    Call WB_LogMsg("CleanupAuditAfterCancel: COMPLETO - state restaurado")
End Sub


'============================================================
' Brain_Step02alt_OmegaOptimizer  (META v3.1 PEAK-HOUR)
' Idempotente + polimorfico + implacable.
' Integra: Brain_OmegaOptimizer, Brain_RunOmega, Brain_RefineToMax, Brain_RunAll5
' Bounds peak-hour alineados con 10 Garantias OMEGA (G3 NUCLEAR 60s+)
'============================================================
Public Sub Brain_Step02alt_OmegaOptimizer()
    If UCase(ConfigVal("Default_Profile")) = "ALL_PROFILES" Then
        Call Brain_Step02alt_OmegaOptimizerAuto
        Exit Sub
    End If
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer
    Const META_TIMEOUT As Double = 90#
    Const META_MAX_ITER As Long = 4
    Dim stepId As String: stepId = "INIT"

    WIRING_SILENT = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.DisplayStatusBar = True
    Application.StatusBar = "META v3.1 peak-hour: init..."

    stepId = "REFS"
    Dim wP As Worksheet: Set wP = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR")
    Dim wB As Worksheet: Set wB = ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD")
    Dim wC As Worksheet: Set wC = ThisWorkbook.sheets("18_CONFIG")
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("31_SOLVER_SETUP")

    stepId = "PRIORS"
    Dim wQ#, wSt#, wL#, wR#, wA#
    wQ = Meta_SafeCDbl(wP.Range("C25").Value2, 40) / 100#
    wSt = Meta_SafeCDbl(wP.Range("C26").Value2, 20) / 100#
    wL = Meta_SafeCDbl(wP.Range("C27").Value2, 10) / 100#
    wR = Meta_SafeCDbl(wP.Range("C28").Value2, 10) / 100#
    wA = Meta_SafeCDbl(wP.Range("C29").Value2, 20) / 100#
    Dim wsum#: wsum = wQ + wSt + wL + wR + wA
    If wsum < 0.99 Or wsum > 1.01 Then GoTo CLEANUP_FAIL

    stepId = "DIARY"
    Dim wd As Worksheet: Set wd = Meta_GetOrCreateSheet("00_SOLVER_DIARY")
    Call Meta_Log(wd, "META-v31-INIT", "wQ=" & Format(wQ, "0.00") & " wS=" & Format(wSt, "0.00") & _
                  " wL=" & Format(wL, "0.00") & " wR=" & Format(wR, "0.00") & " wA=" & Format(wA, "0.00") & " PEAK-HOUR")

    stepId = "HASH"
    Dim inHash As String: inHash = Meta_InputHash(wP, ws)
    Dim lastInHash As String: lastInHash = CStr(Meta_ReadConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Last_InputHash"))
    Dim forceRun As Boolean: forceRun = (LCase(CStr(Meta_ReadConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Force_Run"))) = "x")
    Call Meta_Log(wd, "DEBUG:HASH", "in=" & inHash & " last=" & lastInHash & " force=" & forceRun)

    If inHash = lastInHash And lastInHash <> "" And Not forceRun Then
        Call Meta_Log(wd, "IDEMPOTENT", "inputHash=" & inHash & " == cache. SKIP.")
        Application.StatusBar = "META: cache hit"
        GoTo FINISH_OK
    End If

    stepId = "BASELINE"
    Dim baseK As String: baseK = Meta_SnapshotKnobs(wC, wB)
    Dim baseF As Double: baseF = Meta_EvalFitness(wB, wQ, wSt, wL, wR, wA)
    Dim baseOK As Long: baseOK = Meta_CountOK(wB)
    Call Meta_Log(wd, "BASELINE", "fit=" & Format(baseF, "0.000000") & " ok=" & baseOK & "/10 knobs=[" & baseK & "]")

    Dim bestK As String: bestK = baseK
    Dim bestF As Double: bestF = baseF
    Dim bestSrc As String: bestSrc = "BASELINE"

    stepId = "SEED"
    Dim seed As Long: seed = Meta_HashToSeed(inHash)
    Rnd -1
    Randomize seed
    Call Meta_Log(wd, "SEED", "seed=" & seed)

    Dim iter As Long, noImprove As Long: noImprove = 0
    For iter = 1 To META_MAX_ITER
        If (Timer - t0) > META_TIMEOUT Then
            Call Meta_Log(wd, "TIMEOUT", "> " & META_TIMEOUT & "s en iter " & iter)
            Exit For
        End If
        Application.StatusBar = "META iter " & iter & "/" & META_MAX_ITER & " | fit=" & Format(bestF, "0.000") & " | ok=" & Meta_CountOK(wB) & "/10"
        DoEvents

        stepId = "ITER" & iter & "-A"
        Call Meta_RestoreKnobs(wC, wB, bestK)
        On Error Resume Next
        Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_OmegaOptimizer"
        Dim eA As Long: eA = Err.Number
        Err.Clear
        On Error GoTo EH
        Dim kA As String: kA = Meta_SnapshotKnobs(wC, wB)
        Dim fA As Double: fA = Meta_EvalFitness(wB, wQ, wSt, wL, wR, wA)
        Call Meta_Log(wd, stepId, "Omega err=" & eA & " fit=" & Format(fA, "0.000000") & " ok=" & Meta_CountOK(wB))

        stepId = "ITER" & iter & "-B"
        Call Meta_RestoreKnobs(wC, wB, bestK)
        On Error Resume Next
        Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_RunOmega"
        Dim eB As Long: eB = Err.Number
        Err.Clear
        On Error GoTo EH
        Call Meta_PerturbKnobs_PeakHour(wC, wB)
        Dim kB As String: kB = Meta_SnapshotKnobs(wC, wB)
        Dim fB As Double: fB = Meta_EvalFitness(wB, wQ, wSt, wL, wR, wA)
        Call Meta_Log(wd, stepId, "RunOmega+peak err=" & eB & " fit=" & Format(fB, "0.000000") & " ok=" & Meta_CountOK(wB))

        stepId = "ITER" & iter & "-C"
        Call Meta_RestoreKnobs(wC, wB, bestK)
        Call Meta_RunTrackC_Safe(wd, iter)
        Dim kC As String: kC = Meta_SnapshotKnobs(wC, wB)
        Dim fC As Double: fC = Meta_EvalFitness(wB, wQ, wSt, wL, wR, wA)
        Call Meta_Log(wd, stepId, "Omega+Refine+All5 fit=" & Format(fC, "0.000000") & " ok=" & Meta_CountOK(wB))

        Dim winK As String: winK = bestK
        Dim winF As Double: winF = bestF
        Dim winSrc As String: winSrc = "current"
        If fA > winF Then winK = kA: winF = fA: winSrc = "A-Optimizer"
        If fB > winF Then winK = kB: winF = fB: winSrc = "B-RunOmega"
        If fC > winF Then winK = kC: winF = fC: winSrc = "C-Refine"

        If winF > bestF + 0.000001 Then
            bestK = winK: bestF = winF: bestSrc = winSrc
            noImprove = 0
            Call Meta_Log(wd, "ITER" & iter & "-WIN", "src=" & winSrc & " fit=" & Format(winF, "0.000000"))
        Else
            noImprove = noImprove + 1
            Call Meta_Log(wd, "ITER" & iter & "-NO-IMPROVE", "(" & noImprove & "/2)")
            If noImprove >= 2 Then Exit For
        End If

        Call Meta_RestoreKnobs(wC, wB, bestK)
        If Meta_AllGuaranteesOK(wB) Then
            Call Meta_Log(wd, "ALL-OK", "10/10 converged")
            Exit For
        End If
    Next iter

    stepId = "APPLY"
    Call Meta_RestoreKnobs(wC, wB, bestK)
    Dim outHash As String: outHash = Meta_HashString(bestK)
    Call Meta_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Last_InputHash", inHash)
    Call Meta_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Last_OutputHash", outHash)
    Call Meta_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Last_Fit", Format(bestF, "0.000000"))
    Call Meta_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Last_Source", bestSrc)
    Call Meta_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Last_Run", Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss"))
    Call Meta_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Guarantees_OK", Meta_CountOK(wB) & " of 10")
    Call Meta_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Iterations", iter)
    Call Meta_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Force_Run", "")
    Call Meta_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Mode", "PEAK-HOUR")
    Call Meta_Log(wd, "META-DONE", "winner=" & bestSrc & " fit=" & Format(bestF, "0.000000") & _
                  " ok=" & Meta_CountOK(wB) & "/10 iters=" & iter & " time=" & Format(Timer - t0, "0.0") & "s")

FINISH_OK:
    On Error Resume Next
    Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!MarkStep", "02a", True
    Err.Clear
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.StatusBar = "META DONE: fit=" & Format(bestF, "0.0000") & " ok=" & Meta_CountOK(wB) & "/10"
    WIRING_SILENT = False
    Exit Sub

CLEANUP_FAIL:
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.StatusBar = False
    WIRING_SILENT = False
    MsgBox "META: pesos 36_BRAIN_SELECTOR!C25:C29 no suman 100.", vbExclamation
    Exit Sub

EH:
    Dim errN As Long: errN = Err.Number
    Dim errD As String: errD = Err.description
    Err.Clear
    On Error Resume Next
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.StatusBar = "META ERROR @ " & stepId
    WIRING_SILENT = False
    Call WB_LogMsg("META ERROR step=" & stepId & " #" & errN & " - " & errD)
    Dim wd2 As Worksheet
    Set wd2 = Nothing
    Set wd2 = ThisWorkbook.sheets("00_SOLVER_DIARY")
    If Not wd2 Is Nothing Then
        Call Meta_Log(wd2, "META-ERROR", "step=" & stepId & " #" & errN & " desc=" & errD)
    End If
    Err.Clear
End Sub

Private Sub Meta_RunTrackC_Safe(wd As Worksheet, iter As Long)
    On Error Resume Next
    Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_RunOmega"
    Err.Clear
    DoEvents
    Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_RefineToMax"
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then
        Call Meta_Log(wd, "DEBUG:C" & iter, "RefineToMax no existe o err=" & Err.Number)
        Err.Clear
    End If
    DoEvents
    Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_RunAll5"
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then Err.Clear
    DoEvents
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Function Meta_SnapshotKnobs(wC As Worksheet, wB As Worksheet) As String
    On Error Resume Next
    Meta_SnapshotKnobs = _
        Format(Meta_SafeCDbl(wC.Range("C31").Value2, 60), "0") & "|" & _
        Format(Meta_SafeCDbl(wC.Range("C32").Value2, 10), "0") & "|" & _
        Format(Meta_SafeCDbl(wC.Range("C33").Value2, 60), "0") & "|" & _
        Format(Meta_SafeCDbl(wB.Range("H10").Value2, 10), "0") & "|" & _
        Format(Meta_SafeCDbl(wB.Range("H11").Value2, 9), "0") & "|" & _
        Format(Meta_SafeCDbl(wB.Range("H12").Value2, 0.85), "0.00") & "|" & _
        Format(Meta_SafeCDbl(wB.Range("H13").Value2, 1.3), "0.00")
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Sub Meta_RestoreKnobs(wC As Worksheet, wB As Worksheet, ByVal snap As String)
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim p() As String: p = Split(snap, "|")
    If UBound(p) < 6 Then Exit Sub
    wC.Range("C31").Value2 = Meta_SafeCDbl(p(0), 60)
    wC.Range("C32").Value2 = Meta_SafeCDbl(p(1), 10)
    wC.Range("C33").Value2 = Meta_SafeCDbl(p(2), 60)
    wB.Range("H10").Value2 = Meta_SafeCDbl(p(3), 10)
    wB.Range("H11").Value2 = Meta_SafeCDbl(p(4), 9)
    wB.Range("H12").Value2 = Meta_SafeCDbl(p(5), 0.85)
    wB.Range("H13").Value2 = Meta_SafeCDbl(p(6), 1.3)
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Function Meta_EvalFitness(wB As Worksheet, wQ#, wSt#, wL#, wR#, wA#) As Double
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim health#: health = Meta_SafeCDbl(wB.Range("B5").Value2, 50)
    Dim risk#: risk = Meta_SafeCDbl(wB.Range("B6").Value2, 50)
    Dim headroom#: headroom = Meta_SafeCDbl(wB.Range("B7").Value2, 100)
    Dim stall#: stall = Meta_SafeCDbl(wB.Range("B8").Value2, 1)
    Dim d1#, d2#, d3#, d4#
    d1 = Meta_Clamp01(health / 100#)
    d2 = Meta_Clamp01(1# - risk / 100#)
    d3 = Meta_Clamp01((headroom - 100#) / 900#)
    d4 = Meta_Clamp01(1# - stall / 10#)
    Dim n As Long: n = Meta_CountOK(wB)
    Dim dG#: dG = n / 10#
    Meta_EvalFitness = wQ * (0.6 * d1 + 0.4 * dG) + _
                       wSt * (0.6 * d4 + 0.4 * dG) + _
                       wL * d2 + wR * d2 + _
                       wA * (0.7 * d3 + 0.3 * dG)
    If n = 10 Then Meta_EvalFitness = Meta_EvalFitness + 0.1
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function Meta_CountOK(wB As Worksheet) As Long
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim r As Long, n As Long
    For r = 13 To 22
        If LCase(CStr(wB.Cells(r, 4).Value2)) = "ok" Then n = n + 1
    Next r
    Meta_CountOK = n
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function Meta_AllGuaranteesOK(wB As Worksheet) As Boolean
    Meta_AllGuaranteesOK = (Meta_CountOK(wB) = 10)
End Function

Private Function Meta_Clamp01(v As Double) As Double
    If v < 0 Then
        Meta_Clamp01 = 0
    ElseIf v > 1 Then
        Meta_Clamp01 = 1
    Else
        Meta_Clamp01 = v
    End If
End Function

Private Function Meta_SafeCDbl(v As Variant, fallback As Double) As Double
    On Error Resume Next
    Meta_SafeCDbl = fallback
    If isNumeric(v) Then Meta_SafeCDbl = CDbl(v)
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Sub Meta_PerturbKnobs_PeakHour(wC As Worksheet, wB As Worksheet)
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim cur#
    cur = Meta_SafeCDbl(wC.Range("C31").Value2, 90)
    wC.Range("C31").Value2 = Application.WorksheetFunction.max(60, Application.WorksheetFunction.Min(180, Int(cur + (Rnd() - 0.5) * 20)))
    cur = Meta_SafeCDbl(wC.Range("C32").Value2, 15)
    wC.Range("C32").Value2 = Application.WorksheetFunction.max(8, Application.WorksheetFunction.Min(30, Int(cur + (Rnd() - 0.5) * 6)))
    cur = Meta_SafeCDbl(wC.Range("C33").Value2, 150)
    wC.Range("C33").Value2 = Application.WorksheetFunction.max(99, Application.WorksheetFunction.Min(200, Int(cur + (Rnd() - 0.5) * 30)))
    cur = Meta_SafeCDbl(wB.Range("H10").Value2, 15)
    wB.Range("H10").Value2 = Application.WorksheetFunction.max(10, Application.WorksheetFunction.Min(20, Int(cur + (Rnd() - 0.5) * 4)))
    cur = Meta_SafeCDbl(wB.Range("H11").Value2, 12)
    wB.Range("H11").Value2 = Application.WorksheetFunction.max(8, Application.WorksheetFunction.Min(15, Int(cur + (Rnd() - 0.5) * 3)))
    cur = Meta_SafeCDbl(wB.Range("H12").Value2, 0.9)
    wB.Range("H12").Value2 = Application.WorksheetFunction.max(0.85, Application.WorksheetFunction.Min(1#, cur + (Rnd() - 0.5) * 0.1))
    cur = Meta_SafeCDbl(wB.Range("H13").Value2, 1.3)
    wB.Range("H13").Value2 = Application.WorksheetFunction.max(1.2, Application.WorksheetFunction.Min(1.5, cur + (Rnd() - 0.5) * 0.15))
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Function Meta_HashString(ByVal s As String) As String
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim i As Long, h As Double, m As Double
    m = 2147483647#: h = 0
    For i = 1 To Len(s)
        h = h * 31#
        h = h - Int(h / m) * m
        h = h + CDbl(Asc(Mid(s, i, 1))) * CDbl(i)
        h = h - Int(h / m) * m
    Next i
    Meta_HashString = Right("00000000" & Hex(CLng(h)), 8)
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function Meta_HashToSeed(ByVal h As String) As Long
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim n As Long: n = CLng("&H" & h) And &H7FFFFFFF
    If n = 0 Then n = 12345
    Meta_HashToSeed = n
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function Meta_InputHash(wP As Worksheet, ws As Worksheet) As String
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim s As String
    s = "P:" & CStr(wP.Range("C25").Value2) & "," & CStr(wP.Range("C26").Value2) & "," & _
        CStr(wP.Range("C27").Value2) & "," & CStr(wP.Range("C28").Value2) & "," & CStr(wP.Range("C29").Value2)
    s = s & ";T:"
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 6 To 12: s = s & CStr(ws.Cells(r, 6).Value2) & ",": Next r
    s = s & ";R:"
    For r = 16 To 20
        s = s & CStr(ws.Cells(r, 3).Value2) & CStr(ws.Cells(r, 4).Value2) & CStr(ws.Cells(r, 5).Value2) & ","
    Next r
    Meta_InputHash = Meta_HashString(s)
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Function Meta_ReadConfig(wC As Worksheet, key As String) As Variant
    On Error Resume Next
    Meta_ReadConfig = ""
    Dim lastR As Long: lastR = wC.Cells(wC.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 3 To lastR
        If CStr(wC.Cells(r, 2).Value2) = key Then
            Meta_ReadConfig = wC.Cells(r, 3).Value2
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next r
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Sub Meta_UpsertConfig(wC As Worksheet, key As String, v As Variant)
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim lastR As Long: lastR = wC.Cells(wC.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    Dim r As Long, foundR As Long: foundR = 0
    For r = 3 To lastR
        If CStr(wC.Cells(r, 2).Value2) = key Then foundR = r: Exit For
    Next r
    If foundR = 0 Then foundR = lastR + 1
    wC.Cells(foundR, 1).Value2 = "X"
    wC.Cells(foundR, 2).Value2 = key
    wC.Cells(foundR, 3).Value2 = v
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Function Meta_GetOrCreateSheet(nm As String) As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set Meta_GetOrCreateSheet = ThisWorkbook.sheets(nm)
    If Meta_GetOrCreateSheet Is Nothing Then
        Set Meta_GetOrCreateSheet = ThisWorkbook.sheets.Add(After:=ThisWorkbook.sheets(ThisWorkbook.sheets.count))
        Meta_GetOrCreateSheet.name = nm
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Private Sub Meta_Log(ws As Worksheet, phase As String, msg As String)
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim r As Long: r = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row + 1
    If r < 5 Then r = 5
    ws.Cells(r, 1).Value2 = CDbl(Now)
    ws.Cells(r, 1).NumberFormat = "yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss"
    ws.Cells(r, 2).Value2 = "META:" & phase
    ws.Cells(r, 3).Value2 = msg
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

    On Error Resume Next
    Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!ImportFromFrontend_UltraFast"
    Call MarkStep("01", True)
End Sub

'============================================================
' PANEL v5  .  Flow state tracking con dependencias
'   IN col N:  gris=LOCKED, ambar=READY, slate=DONE_consumido
'   OUT col S: gris=PENDING, verde=DONE
' Cada wrapper Brain_StepNN_* ejecuta su macro, marca OUT verde
' y re-evalua dependencias via UpdateFlowState.
'============================================================

Public Sub MarkStep(ByVal stepId As String, ByVal Done As Boolean)
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim r As Long: r = GetStepRow(stepId)
    If r = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim outCell As Range
    Set outCell = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR").Cells(r, 19)   ' col S = 19
    If Done Then
        outCell.Interior.Color = RGB(34, 197, 94)
        outCell.Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
        outCell.Font.Bold = True
    Else
        outCell.Interior.Color = RGB(243, 244, 246)
        outCell.Font.Color = RGB(17, 24, 39)
        outCell.Font.Bold = False
    End If
    Call UpdateFlowState
End Sub

Public Sub ResetFlowStatus()
    Dim ids As Variant: ids = Array("01", "02", "02a", "03", "04", "05", "05b", "06", "07", "08", "09", "10", "11")
    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(ids) To UBound(ids)
        Call MarkStep(CStr(ids(i)), False)
    Next i
    Call UpdateFlowState
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Flow reset.  IN de paso 01 iluminado ambar (READY).", vbInformation, "Reset"
End Sub

Private Function GetStepRow(ByVal stepId As String) As Long
    ' FIX 2026-04-26: Steps 06-10 +1 fila (separador MOTORES AVANZADOS en row 61)
    Select Case stepId
        Case "01":  GetStepRow = 54
        Case "02":  GetStepRow = 55
        Case "02a": GetStepRow = 56
        Case "03":  GetStepRow = 57
        Case "04":  GetStepRow = 58
        Case "05":  GetStepRow = 59
        Case "05b": GetStepRow = 60
        ' --- Row 61 = separador 'MOTORES AVANZADOS' (skip) ---
        Case "06":  GetStepRow = 62   'antes 61 (BUG fixed)
        Case "07":  GetStepRow = 63   'antes 62 (BUG fixed)
        Case "08":  GetStepRow = 64   'antes 63 (BUG fixed)
        Case "09":  GetStepRow = 65   'antes 64 (BUG fixed)
        Case "10":  GetStepRow = 66   'antes 65 (BUG fixed)
        Case "11":  GetStepRow = 67   'OK
        Case Else:  GetStepRow = 0
    End Select
End Function

Private Function IsStepDone(ByVal stepId As String) As Boolean
    Dim r As Long: r = GetStepRow(stepId)
    If r = 0 Then IsStepDone = False: Exit Function
    Dim clr As Long
    clr = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR").Cells(r, 19).Interior.Color
    ' Verde = RGB(34,197,94) = Long value (B*65536 + G*256 + R)
    IsStepDone = (clr = (94& * 65536) + (197& * 256&) + 34&)
End Function

'============================================================
' UpdateFlowState
' Recorre todos los pasos y determina el estado de su IN cell:
'   LOCKED (gris) si sus dependencias no estan DONE
'   READY (ambar) si todas sus dependencias estan DONE y el paso aun no
'   CONSUMED (slate) si el paso mismo ya esta DONE
'============================================================
Public Sub UpdateFlowState()
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR")
    Dim colorLocked As Long: colorLocked = RGB(243, 244, 246)
    Dim colorReady As Long:  colorReady = RGB(253, 224, 71)
    Dim colorConsumed As Long: colorConsumed = RGB(226, 232, 240)

    Dim ids As Variant: ids = Array("01", "02", "02a", "03", "04", "05", "05b", "06", "07", "08", "09", "10", "11")
    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(ids) To UBound(ids)
        Dim id As String: id = CStr(ids(i))
        Dim r As Long: r = GetStepRow(id)
        If r = 0 Then GoTo nextId
        Dim inCell As Range: Set inCell = ws.Cells(r, 14)   ' col N = 14

        If IsStepDone(id) Then
            inCell.Interior.Color = colorConsumed
        Else
            Dim ready As Boolean: ready = False
            Select Case id
                Case "01": ready = True   ' siempre ready
                Case "02", "02a": ready = IsStepDone("01")
                Case "03":
                    ' ready si hay m3u8 path valido en 18_CONFIG!C30 (independiente de 02)
                    Dim m3u8 As String: m3u8 = ""
                    m3u8 = CStr(ws.Parent.sheets("18_CONFIG").Range("C30").value)
                    ready = (m3u8 <> "" And Len(dir(m3u8)) > 0)
                Case "04": ready = IsStepDone("03")
                Case "05": ready = IsStepDone("04") And (IsStepDone("02") Or IsStepDone("02a"))
                Case "05b": ready = IsStepDone("05")
                Case "06", "07", "08", "09", "10": ready = True   ' siempre (uso individual)
                Case "11": ready = True   ' siempre disponible como exportador
                Case Else: ready = False
            End Select
            If ready Then
                inCell.Interior.Color = colorReady
            Else
                inCell.Interior.Color = colorLocked
            End If
        End If
nextId:
    Next i
End Sub
