'============================================================
' GapPlan v2.2 - IDEMPOTENTE + POLIMORFICO
'
' Contrato:
'   - Re-ejecutar el export N veces -> mismo resultado (idempotente)
'   - Detecta si cada directiva ya esta en profiles_calibrated, nivel1_headers,
'     nivel3, servers, etc. -> flag already_present_in_lab
'   - Preserva items "custom" que el user haya metido manualmente en el JSON
'     si su id no esta en el scorecard (via IDs extraidos del gap_plan previo)
'   - Jamas sobrescribe otras secciones del JSON (profiles, servers, config)
'     porque el RemoveJsonFieldSimple es field-specific
'============================================================

'------------------------------------------------------------
' LineAlreadyInLab
' Busca la firma principal de la linea canonical en labJson
' (excluyendo el bloque omega_gap_plan mismo -- asume que el caller
'  ya removio ese bloque antes de pasar el texto).
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function LineAlreadyInLab(ByVal labJson As String, ByVal line As String) As Boolean
    If labJson = "" Or line = "" Then LineAlreadyInLab = False: Exit Function
    Dim sig As String
    ' Signatura = primer token tag (#EXT-...) + clave
    If Left(line, 4) = "#EXT" Then
        Dim colonIdx As Long: colonIdx = InStr(line, ":")
        If colonIdx > 4 Then
            sig = Left(line, colonIdx - 1)
        Else
            sig = Left(line, 30)
        End If
    ElseIf InStr(line, "=") > 0 Then
        ' key=value -> usar key
        sig = Left(line, InStr(line, "=") - 1)
    ElseIf InStr(line, ":") > 0 Then
        sig = Left(line, InStr(line, ":") - 1)
    Else
        sig = line
    End If
    If Len(sig) < 3 Then LineAlreadyInLab = False: Exit Function
    ' Buscar en el JSON (ya sin gap_plan)
    LineAlreadyInLab = (InStr(1, labJson, sig, vbTextCompare) > 0) Or _
                      (InStr(1, labJson, EscJson(sig), vbTextCompare) > 0)
End Function

'------------------------------------------------------------
' BuildAlreadyPresentMap
' Para un check_id y el texto del lab, calcula un mini-JSON:
'   {"NIVEL_1_HEADER": true|false, "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL": true|false}
' indicando si cada nivel ya tiene sus lineas canonicas en otra parte del lab.
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function BuildAlreadyPresentMap(ByVal cid As String, ByVal labJson As String) As String
    On Error Resume Next
    If labJson = "" Then BuildAlreadyPresentMap = "{}": Exit Function

    ' Obtener el canonical_by_level como JSON string y parsearlo crudo buscando keys
    Dim tpl As String: tpl = GetCanonicalByLevelJson(cid)
    If tpl = "{}" Then BuildAlreadyPresentMap = "{}": Exit Function

    ' tpl tiene formato: { "NIVEL_X": ["line1","line2"], ... }
    ' Extraer levels y sus lineas via parsing simple
    Dim out As String: out = "{"
    Dim allTrue As Boolean: allTrue = True
    Dim anyFound As Boolean: anyFound = False
    Dim first As Boolean: first = True

    Dim levels As Variant
    levels = Array("NIVEL_1_HEADER", "NIVEL_2_PROFILES", "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL", "PLAYER_CONFIG", "METADATA", "MULTI_LEVEL")
    Dim lvl As Variant
    For Each lvl In levels
        Dim lvlKey As String: lvlKey = Chr(34) & CStr(lvl) & Chr(34)
        Dim kidx As Long: kidx = InStr(tpl, lvlKey)
        If kidx = 0 Then GoTo nextLvl

        ' Encontrar el [ y ] de ese nivel
        Dim obr As Long: obr = InStr(kidx, tpl, "[")
        Dim cbr As Long: cbr = InStr(obr, tpl, "]")
        If obr = 0 Or cbr = 0 Then GoTo nextLvl

        Dim arrText As String: arrText = Mid(tpl, obr + 1, cbr - obr - 1)

        ' Split por "," y verificar si al menos una linea esta ya presente
        Dim parts() As String: parts = Split(arrText, Chr(34) & "," & Chr(34))
        Dim i As Long
        Dim levelPresent As Boolean: levelPresent = False
        For i = LBound(parts) To UBound(parts)
            Dim lineTxt As String: lineTxt = parts(i)
            lineTxt = Replace(lineTxt, Chr(34), "")
            If lineTxt <> "" Then
                If LineAlreadyInLab(labJson, lineTxt) Then levelPresent = True: Exit For
            End If
        Next i

        If Not first Then out = out & ","
        first = False
        out = out & vbCrLf & "          " & lvlKey & ": " & IIf(levelPresent, "true", "false")
        If Not levelPresent Then allTrue = False
        anyFound = True
nextLvl:
    Next lvl

    If Not anyFound Then BuildAlreadyPresentMap = "{}": Exit Function
    out = out & vbCrLf & "        }"
    BuildAlreadyPresentMap = out
End Function

'------------------------------------------------------------
' ExtractPreservedCustomItems
' Lee el bloque omega_gap_plan.items[] del labJson existente (SIN remover aun)
' y devuelve un string JSON con los items cuyo id NO aparece en scorecardIds
' (items custom del user -- los preservamos en la nueva escritura).
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function ExtractPreservedCustomItems(ByVal labJson As String, ByVal scorecardIds As String) As String
    On Error Resume Next
    If labJson = "" Then ExtractPreservedCustomItems = "": Exit Function
    ' Busqueda por regex simple: {"id":"XYZ",...}  dentro del items[]
    Dim startIdx As Long: startIdx = InStr(labJson, Chr(34) & "omega_gap_plan" & Chr(34))
    If startIdx = 0 Then ExtractPreservedCustomItems = "": Exit Function
    Dim itemsKeyIdx As Long: itemsKeyIdx = InStr(startIdx, labJson, Chr(34) & "items" & Chr(34))
    If itemsKeyIdx = 0 Then ExtractPreservedCustomItems = "": Exit Function
    Dim openBracket As Long: openBracket = InStr(itemsKeyIdx, labJson, "[")
    If openBracket = 0 Then ExtractPreservedCustomItems = "": Exit Function

    ' Balancear [ y ] para encontrar cierre del items array
    Dim depth As Long: depth = 0
    Dim i As Long: i = openBracket
    Do While i <= Len(labJson)
        Dim ch As String: ch = Mid(labJson, i, 1)
        If ch = "[" Then depth = depth + 1
        If ch = "]" Then
            depth = depth - 1
            If depth = 0 Then Exit Do
        End If
        i = i + 1
    Loop
    Dim closeBracket As Long: closeBracket = i
    If closeBracket <= openBracket Then ExtractPreservedCustomItems = "": Exit Function

    Dim itemsBlock As String: itemsBlock = Mid(labJson, openBracket + 1, closeBracket - openBracket - 1)

    ' Iterar items del bloque buscando aquellos cuyo id no este en scorecardIds
    Dim preserved As String: preserved = ""
    Dim pos As Long: pos = 1
    Do
        Dim idStart As Long: idStart = InStr(pos, itemsBlock, Chr(34) & "id" & Chr(34))
        If idStart = 0 Then Exit Do
        Dim firstQuote As Long: firstQuote = InStr(idStart + 4, itemsBlock, Chr(34))
        Dim secQuote As Long: secQuote = InStr(firstQuote + 1, itemsBlock, Chr(34))
        Dim thirdQuote As Long: thirdQuote = InStr(secQuote + 1, itemsBlock, Chr(34))
        Dim fourthQuote As Long: fourthQuote = InStr(thirdQuote + 1, itemsBlock, Chr(34))
        If fourthQuote = 0 Then Exit Do
        Dim thisId As String: thisId = Mid(itemsBlock, thirdQuote + 1, fourthQuote - thirdQuote - 1)

        ' Encontrar el { que abre este item (antes de idStart)
        Dim braceOpen As Long: braceOpen = idStart
        Do While braceOpen > 1
            If Mid(itemsBlock, braceOpen, 1) = "{" Then Exit Do
            braceOpen = braceOpen - 1
        Loop

        ' Encontrar el } que cierra (balance)
        Dim bd As Long: bd = 0
        Dim j As Long: j = braceOpen
        Do While j <= Len(itemsBlock)
            Dim c2 As String: c2 = Mid(itemsBlock, j, 1)
            If c2 = "{" Then bd = bd + 1
            If c2 = "}" Then
                bd = bd - 1
                If bd = 0 Then Exit Do
            End If
            j = j + 1
        Loop
        Dim braceClose As Long: braceClose = j

        ' Si thisId NO esta en scorecardIds, preservar
        If InStr(1, "," & scorecardIds & ",", "," & thisId & ",", vbTextCompare) = 0 Then
            Dim itemText As String: itemText = Mid(itemsBlock, braceOpen, braceClose - braceOpen + 1)
            If preserved <> "" Then preserved = preserved & "," & vbCrLf
            preserved = preserved & "      " & itemText
        End If

        pos = braceClose + 1
    Loop

    ExtractPreservedCustomItems = preserved
End Function

'============================================================
' BuildGapPlanJsonBlock v2.2
' Recibe opcionalmente el texto del JSON actual para:
'   - flaggear already_present_in_lab por item
'   - incluir items custom preservados (no sobrescribir edits manuales)
'============================================================
Public Function BuildGapPlanJsonBlock(Optional ByVal labJson As String = "") As String
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("00_OMEGA_SCORECARD")
    If ws Is Nothing Then BuildGapPlanJsonBlock = "": Exit Function
    Dim vV As Worksheet: Set vV = ThisWorkbook.sheets("00_OMEGA_VERBATIM")

    Dim inj As Object: Set inj = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    inj.Add "A1", "global_header|10"
    inj.Add "A2", "global_header|20"
    inj.Add "A3", "global_header|30"
    inj.Add "A4", "global_header_and_per_channel|40"
    inj.Add "A5", "global_header|50"
    inj.Add "A6", "global_header|60"
    inj.Add "A7", "stream_inf_attribute_and_daterange|70"
    inj.Add "A8", "global_exthttp_and_per_channel|80"
    inj.Add "A9", "global_header|90"
    inj.Add "A10", "global_header|100"
    inj.Add "B5", "stream_inf_attribute|200"
    inj.Add "B6", "stream_inf_attribute|210"
    inj.Add "B7", "stream_inf_attribute|220"
    inj.Add "B8", "global_header|230"
    inj.Add "B9", "global_header|240"
    inj.Add "B10", "player_config|250"
    inj.Add "B11", "extvlcopt_or_exthttp|260"
    inj.Add "B12", "extvlcopt_or_exthttp|270"
    inj.Add "B13", "extvlcopt_or_exthttp|280"
    inj.Add "B14", "extvlcopt_global|290"
    inj.Add "B15", "player_config|300"
    inj.Add "B16", "profile_setting|310"
    inj.Add "B17", "playlist_variants|320"
    inj.Add "B18", "servers_pool_config|330"
    inj.Add "B19", "profile_setting|340"
    inj.Add "B20", "profile_setting|350"

    Dim scoreTotal As String: scoreTotal = CStr(ws.Range("B4").value)
    Dim grade As String: grade = Replace(CStr(ws.Range("C4").value), "Grado: ", "")

    Dim lastR As Long: lastR = ws.UsedRange.rows.count
    Dim items As String: items = ""
    Dim scorecardIds As String: scorecardIds = ""
    Dim cntR As Long, cntI As Long, cntQ As Long
    cntR = 0: cntI = 0: cntQ = 0
    Dim first As Boolean: first = True
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 7 To lastR
        Dim cid As String: cid = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value)
        Dim cdesc As String: cdesc = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 2).value)
        Dim cstatus As String: cstatus = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 3).value)
        Dim cdetail As String: cdetail = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 4).value)
        Dim caction As String: caction = CStr(ws.Cells(r, 5).value)

        If cid = "" Then GoTo nextRow
        If Left(cid, 2) = "--" Then GoTo nextRow
        If caction <> "REPLICAR" And caction <> "IMPLEMENTAR" And caction <> "QUITAR" Then GoTo nextRow

        If caction = "REPLICAR" Then cntR = cntR + 1
        If caction = "IMPLEMENTAR" Then cntI = cntI + 1
        If caction = "QUITAR" Then cntQ = cntQ + 1

        scorecardIds = scorecardIds & cid & ","

        Dim verbatimJson As String: verbatimJson = "[]"
        If Not (vV Is Nothing) Then verbatimJson = CollectVerbatimLines(vV, cid)

        Dim canonicalByLvl As String: canonicalByLvl = GetCanonicalByLevelJson(cid)
        Dim alreadyMap As String: alreadyMap = BuildAlreadyPresentMap(cid, labJson)
        Dim primaryLvl As String: primaryLvl = GetPrimaryLevel(cid)
        Dim allProfiles As Boolean: allProfiles = ResolvesAllProfiles(cid)

        Dim injTarget As String: injTarget = "unknown"
        Dim injOrder As Long: injOrder = 999
        If inj.Exists(cid) Then
            Dim parts() As String: parts = Split(CStr(inj(cid)), "|")
            If UBound(parts) >= 0 Then injTarget = parts(0)
            If UBound(parts) >= 1 Then injOrder = CLng(parts(1))
        End If

        Dim layer As String
        If Left(cid, 1) = "A" Then
            layer = "A"
        ElseIf Left(cid, 1) = "B" Then
            layer = "B"
        ElseIf Left(cid, 1) = "Q" Then
            layer = "C"
        Else
            layer = "?"
        End If

        If Not first Then items = items & "," & vbCrLf
        first = False
        items = items & "      {" & vbCrLf
        items = items & "        " & q("id") & ": " & q(cid) & "," & vbCrLf
        items = items & "        " & q("layer") & ": " & q(layer) & "," & vbCrLf
        items = items & "        " & q("m3u8_level") & ": " & q(primaryLvl) & "," & vbCrLf
        items = items & "        " & q("resolves_to_all_profiles") & ": " & IIf(allProfiles, "true", "false") & "," & vbCrLf
        items = items & "        " & q("description") & ": " & q(EscJson(cdesc)) & "," & vbCrLf
        items = items & "        " & q("status_in_audit") & ": " & q(cstatus) & "," & vbCrLf
        items = items & "        " & q("action") & ": " & q(caction) & "," & vbCrLf
        items = items & "        " & q("detail") & ": " & q(EscJson(cdetail)) & "," & vbCrLf
        items = items & "        " & q("verbatim_lines") & ": " & verbatimJson & "," & vbCrLf
        items = items & "        " & q("canonical_template_by_level") & ": " & canonicalByLvl & "," & vbCrLf
        items = items & "        " & q("already_present_in_lab") & ": " & alreadyMap & "," & vbCrLf
        items = items & "        " & q("injection_target") & ": " & q(injTarget) & "," & vbCrLf
        items = items & "        " & q("injection_order") & ": " & injOrder & vbCrLf
        items = items & "      }"
nextRow:
    Next r

    ' Preservar items custom del user (ids que no estan en el scorecard)
    Dim preservedItems As String
    preservedItems = ExtractPreservedCustomItems(labJson, Left(scorecardIds, Len(scorecardIds) - 1))
    Dim hasPreserved As Boolean: hasPreserved = (preservedItems <> "")
    If hasPreserved Then
        If items <> "" Then items = items & "," & vbCrLf
        items = items & preservedItems
    End If

    If (cntR + cntI + cntQ) = 0 And Not hasPreserved Then
        BuildGapPlanJsonBlock = ""
        Exit Function
    End If

    Dim sourceM3U8 As String: sourceM3U8 = ConfigVal("Output_M3U8_Path")

    Dim block As String
    block = "  " & q("omega_gap_plan") & ": {" & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("gap_plan_version") & ": " & q("2.2") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("generated_at") & ": " & q(Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd\THH:nn:ss")) & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("idempotent") & ": true," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("polymorphic") & ": true," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("source_audit_m3u8") & ": " & q(EscJson(sourceM3U8)) & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("scorecard_total") & ": " & q(scoreTotal) & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("scorecard_grade") & ": " & q(grade) & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("idempotency_policy") & ": " & q("Re-ejecutar el export con el mismo scorecard produce el mismo resultado. No duplica lineas que ya existen en profiles_calibrated / servers / config_global (ver already_present_in_lab por item). Items custom cuyo id no esta en el scorecard se preservan tal cual (no se borran).") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("m3u8_level_guide") & ": {" & vbCrLf
    block = block & "      " & q("NIVEL_1_HEADER") & ": " & q("Tags globales antes del primer variant. Mapea a hoja 5_NIVEL_1_HEADER.") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "      " & q("NIVEL_2_PROFILES") & ": " & q("Atributos por profile P0-P5. Mapea a 6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES. Si resolves_to_all_profiles=true, itera los 6.") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "      " & q("NIVEL_3_CHANNEL") & ": " & q("Tags per-channel. Mapea a 7_NIVEL_3_CHANNEL.") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "      " & q("MULTI_LEVEL") & ": " & q("Consultar canonical_template_by_level keys.") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "      " & q("PLAYER_CONFIG") & ": " & q("Config runtime del player.") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "      " & q("METADATA") & ": " & q("Sin impacto en M3U8 directo.") & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    }," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("toolkit_notes") & ": " & q("Para cada item: 1) consultar already_present_in_lab antes de inyectar (skip si true). 2) Leer canonical_template_by_level y aplicar cada key (NIVEL_1/2/3) en su zona. 3) Si resolves_to_all_profiles=true iterar P0-P5 usando profiles_calibrated del mismo JSON. 4) Respetar injection_order para orden relativo. 5) REPLICAR=verbatim_lines | IMPLEMENTAR=canonical_template_by_level | QUITAR=omit patrones.") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("summary") & ": {" & vbCrLf
    block = block & "      " & q("replicar") & ": " & cntR & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "      " & q("implementar") & ": " & cntI & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "      " & q("quitar") & ": " & cntQ & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "      " & q("total_items_from_scorecard") & ": " & (cntR + cntI + cntQ) & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "      " & q("preserved_custom_items") & ": " & IIf(hasPreserved, "true", "false") & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    }," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("items") & ": [" & vbCrLf
    block = block & items & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    ]" & vbCrLf
    block = block & "  }"

    BuildGapPlanJsonBlock = block
End Function

'============================================================
' Brain_ExportToFrontendPlus  v2.2 (idempotente + polimorfico)
'============================================================
Public Sub Brain_ExportToFrontendPlus()
    On Error GoTo EH
    ' 1. Base export
    Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_ExportToFrontend"

    ' 2. Localizar JSON reciente
    Dim latest As String: latest = FindLatestLabCalibrated()
    If latest = "" Then
        If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "No se encontro LAB_CALIBRATED_*.json generado.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    ' 3. Leer
    Dim txt As String: txt = ReadFileAll(latest)
    If Len(txt) = 0 Then Exit Sub

    ' 4. Guardar copia del txt ANTES de remover (para extraer items custom)
    Dim txtOriginal As String: txtOriginal = txt

    ' 5. Remover bloques previos (idempotencia)
    If InStr(txt, Chr(34) & "omega_gap_plan" & Chr(34)) > 0 Then
        txt = RemoveJsonFieldSimple(txt, "omega_gap_plan")
    End If
    If InStr(txt, Chr(34) & "omega_guarantees_scorecard" & Chr(34)) > 0 Then
        txt = RemoveJsonFieldSimple(txt, "omega_guarantees_scorecard")
    End If

    ' 6. Construir bloques nuevos -- pasar txt (ya sin gap_plan) para already_present check
    '    y txtOriginal para poder extraer items custom del gap_plan previo
    Dim gapBlock As String: gapBlock = BuildGapPlanJsonBlock(txt)
    Dim guarBlock As String: guarBlock = BuildOmegaGuaranteesBlock()

    ' 7. Si no hay nada que inyectar, terminar
    If gapBlock = "" And guarBlock = "" Then
        If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "LAB_CALIBRATED base exportado (sin gap_plan: no hay selecciones en 00_OMEGA_SCORECARD col E).", vbInformation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    ' 8. Inyectar antes del } final
    Dim idx As Long: idx = InStrRev(txt, "}")
    If idx = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim inject As String: inject = ""
    If guarBlock <> "" Then inject = inject & "," & vbCrLf & guarBlock
    If gapBlock <> "" Then inject = inject & "," & vbCrLf & gapBlock
    Dim newTxt As String
    newTxt = Left(txt, idx - 1) & inject & vbCrLf & "}" & vbCrLf

    ' 9. Reescribir
    Dim fno As Integer: fno = FreeFile
    Open latest For Output As #fno
    Print #fno, newTxt;
    Close #fno

    Dim msg As String
    msg = "LAB_CALIBRATED (v2.2 idempotente+polimorfico):" & vbCrLf & latest & vbCrLf & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "Embebidos:" & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "  - omega_gap_plan (items por nivel + already_present_in_lab)" & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "  - omega_guarantees_scorecard" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf
    msg = msg & "Re-ejecutar no duplica; items custom se preservan."
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox msg, vbInformation, "EXPORT v2.2"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "ERROR ExportPlus: " & Err.Number & " - " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub

'============================================================
' Brain_ExportGapPlan (wrapper) - re-usa Plus con idempotencia
'============================================================
Public Sub Brain_ExportGapPlan()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("00_OMEGA_SCORECARD")
    On Error GoTo EH
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        MsgBox "Primero corre AUDITAR M3U8 (no existe 00_OMEGA_SCORECARD).", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Dim latest As String: latest = FindLatestLabCalibrated()
    Dim freshEnough As Boolean: freshEnough = False
    If latest <> "" Then
        Dim modTime As Date: modTime = FileDateTime(latest)
        If (Now - modTime) < (1# / 24#) Then freshEnough = True
    End If

    If Not freshEnough Then
        Dim resp As VbMsgBoxResult
        resp = MsgBox( _
            "No hay LAB_CALIBRATED_*.json reciente. Disparo export completo con gap_plan embebido?", _
            vbYesNo + vbQuestion, "GAP Plan")
        If resp = vbYes Then Call Brain_ExportToFrontendPlus
        Exit Sub
    End If

    ' Re-inyectar gap_plan sobre el existente (idempotente)
    Dim txt As String: txt = ReadFileAll(latest)
    If InStr(txt, Chr(34) & "omega_gap_plan" & Chr(34)) > 0 Then
        txt = RemoveJsonFieldSimple(txt, "omega_gap_plan")
    End If
    Dim gapBlock As String: gapBlock = BuildGapPlanJsonBlock(txt)
    If gapBlock = "" Then
        MsgBox "No hay selecciones REPLICAR/IMPLEMENTAR/QUITAR en columna E.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Dim idx As Long: idx = InStrRev(txt, "}")
    If idx = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim newTxt As String
    newTxt = Left(txt, idx - 1) & "," & vbCrLf & gapBlock & vbCrLf & "}" & vbCrLf
    Dim fno As Integer: fno = FreeFile
    Open latest For Output As #fno
    Print #fno, newTxt;
    Close #fno
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "GAP PLAN re-inyectado idempotentemente en:" & vbCrLf & latest, vbInformation
    Exit Sub
EH:
    MsgBox "ERROR: " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub

'============================================================
' GapPlan v2.1  (Ultrathink, con m3u8_level por directiva)
'
' Cada item ahora trae:
'   - m3u8_level               : NIVEL_1_HEADER / NIVEL_2_PROFILES / NIVEL_3_CHANNEL / PLAYER_CONFIG / METADATA
'   - canonical_template_by_level : objeto con lineas separadas por nivel
'   - resolves_to_all_profiles : true si aplica a cada P0-P5 (NIVEL_2)
'
' Mapping check -> nivel basado en:
'   - Hoja 5_NIVEL_1_HEADER  : tags globales (antes de variants/channels)
'   - Hoja 6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES : atributos STREAM-INF por perfil P0-P5
'   - Hoja 7_NIVEL_3_CHANNEL  : tags per-channel (EXTVLCOPT, KODIPROP, EXTHTTP)
'============================================================

Public Function GetPrimaryLevel(ByVal cid As String) As String
    Select Case cid
        Case "A1", "A2", "A3", "A6", "A9", "A10": GetPrimaryLevel = "NIVEL_1_HEADER"
        Case "A4", "A8": GetPrimaryLevel = "MULTI_LEVEL"
        Case "A5": GetPrimaryLevel = "NIVEL_1_HEADER"
        Case "A7": GetPrimaryLevel = "NIVEL_2_PROFILES"
        Case "B1": GetPrimaryLevel = "METADATA"
        Case "B2", "B3", "B4": GetPrimaryLevel = "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL"
        Case "B5", "B6", "B7": GetPrimaryLevel = "NIVEL_2_PROFILES"
        Case "B8", "B9": GetPrimaryLevel = "NIVEL_1_HEADER"
        Case "B10": GetPrimaryLevel = "PLAYER_CONFIG"
        Case "B11", "B12", "B13": GetPrimaryLevel = "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL"
        Case "B14": GetPrimaryLevel = "NIVEL_1_HEADER"
        Case "B15": GetPrimaryLevel = "PLAYER_CONFIG"
        Case "B16", "B19", "B20": GetPrimaryLevel = "NIVEL_2_PROFILES"
        Case "B17": GetPrimaryLevel = "NIVEL_2_PROFILES"
        Case "B18": GetPrimaryLevel = "METADATA"
        Case "Q1", "Q2", "Q3", "Q4", "Q5", "Q6", "Q7": GetPrimaryLevel = "METADATA"
        Case Else: GetPrimaryLevel = "UNKNOWN"
    End Select
End Function

Public Function ResolvesAllProfiles(ByVal cid As String) As Boolean
    ' true si este check aplica a cada P0-P5 (genera 6 sets de lineas)
    Select Case cid
        Case "A7", "B5", "B6", "B7", "B16", "B17", "B19", "B20"
            ResolvesAllProfiles = True
        Case Else
            ResolvesAllProfiles = False
    End Select
End Function

'------------------------------------------------------------
' GetCanonicalByLevelJson
' Retorna objeto JSON con: {"NIVEL_1_HEADER": [...], "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL": [...]}
' Lineas canonicas organizadas segun el nivel donde deben ir.
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function GetCanonicalByLevelJson(ByVal cid As String) As String
    ' Cada entry: "cid|level|line"
    Dim data As Object: Set data = CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A1", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXT-X-APE-ANTI-FREEZE-NUCLEAR:ENABLED|NET_CACHE=120000")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A1", "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL", "#EXTVLCOPT:network-continuous-stream=true")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A2", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXT-X-APE-MULTI-SOURCE:ENABLED|FAILOVER=50ms")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A2", "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL", "#EXTVLCOPT:network-reconnect-count=99")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A3", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXT-X-APE-BUFFER-STRATEGY:NUCLEAR_NO_COMPROMISE|MIN=60s")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A3", "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL", "#EXTVLCOPT:network-caching=60000")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A3", "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL", "#EXTVLCOPT:sout-mux-caching=60000")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A4", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXT-X-CATCHUP:catchup=" & Chr(34) & "flussonic" & Chr(34))
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A4", "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL", "#EXTVLCOPT:clock-synchro=0")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A4", "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL", "#EXT-X-DATERANGE:ID=" & Chr(34) & "omega-live" & Chr(34) & ",X-OMEGA-TYPE=" & Chr(34) & "LIVE-CATCHUP" & Chr(34))
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A5", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXT-X-APE-CMCD-OBJECT:type=l,br=5000,d=6000,ot=v")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A5", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXT-X-APE-CMCD-SESSION:sid=" & Chr(34) & "{sessionId}" & Chr(34) & ",cid=" & Chr(34) & "{streamId}" & Chr(34) & ",sf=h,st=l")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A5", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXT-X-APE-CMCD-REQUEST:bl=30000,dl=6000,nor=" & Chr(34) & "../next.ts" & Chr(34))
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A5", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXT-X-APE-CMCD-STATUS:bs,rtp=1000")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A6", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXT-X-APE-QMAX-STRATEGY:GREEDY-BEST-AVAILABLE")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A7", "NIVEL_2_PROFILES", "#EXT-X-STREAM-INF:...,VIDEO-RANGE=HDR10+DV-P81")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A7", "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL", "#EXT-X-DATERANGE:ID=" & Chr(34) & "hdr-1" & Chr(34) & ",X-HDR-TYPE=" & Chr(34) & "HDR10+DV-P81-P10" & Chr(34) & ",X-HDR-MAX-CLL=5000")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A7", "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL", "#EXTVLCOPT:avcodec-hw=any")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A8", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXTHTTP:User-Agent=" & Chr(34) & "OTT Navigator/1.7.0.0 (Linux;Android 11) ExoPlayerLib/2.18.1" & Chr(34))
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A8", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXT-X-APE-EVASION-GET:405-REWRITE-TO-HEAD")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A8", "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL", "#EXTVLCOPT:http-forward-cookies=true")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A9", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXT-X-CONTENT-STEERING:SERVER-URI=" & Chr(34) & "/steering.json" & Chr(34))
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A10", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXT-X-DEFINE:NAME=" & Chr(34) & "OMEGA_BUILD" & Chr(34))
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "A10", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXT-X-APE-INTEGRITY:LOCK=" & Chr(34) & "absolute-hardening-lock" & Chr(34) & ",BUILD=" & Chr(34) & "OMEGA_v5.4" & Chr(34))
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B5", "NIVEL_2_PROFILES", "#EXT-X-STREAM-INF:BANDWIDTH={maxBitrateKbps*1000}")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B6", "NIVEL_2_PROFILES", "#EXT-X-STREAM-INF:CODECS=" & Chr(34) & "{codec_full},{audio_codec}" & Chr(34))
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B7", "NIVEL_2_PROFILES", "#EXT-X-STREAM-INF:RESOLUTION={maxResolution}")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B8", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXTVLCOPT:network-caching=60000")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B9", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXTVLCOPT:sout-mux-caching=60000")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B10", "PLAYER_CONFIG", "{fragLoadPolicy.maxNumRetry: 10}")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B11", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXTHTTP:User-Agent={UA_Default from 18_CONFIG}")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B11", "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL", "#EXTVLCOPT:http-user-agent={UA_Default from 18_CONFIG}")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B12", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXTHTTP:Referer={Referer_Default from 18_CONFIG}")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B12", "NIVEL_3_CHANNEL", "#EXTVLCOPT:http-referrer={Referer_Default from 18_CONFIG}")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B13", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXTHTTP:Connection=keep-alive")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B14", "NIVEL_1_HEADER", "#EXTVLCOPT:clock-synchro=0")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B15", "PLAYER_CONFIG", "{maxLiveSyncPlaybackRate: 1.3}")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B16", "NIVEL_2_PROFILES", "{bufferTargetSec: 30-60 per profile P0-P5}")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B19", "NIVEL_2_PROFILES", "#EXT-X-STREAM-INF:...,VIDEO-RANGE=PQ")
    Call AddLvlTpl(data, "B20", "NIVEL_2_PROFILES", "#EXT-X-STREAM-INF:CODECS con av01 o dvh1")

    If Not data.Exists(cid) Then GetCanonicalByLevelJson = "{}": Exit Function

    ' data[cid] contiene un hash: level -> array de lineas
    Dim levelMap As Object: Set levelMap = data(cid)
    Dim out As String: out = "{"
    Dim firstLvl As Boolean: firstLvl = True
    Dim k As Variant
    For Each k In levelMap.keys
        If Not firstLvl Then out = out & ","
        firstLvl = False
        out = out & vbCrLf & "          " & q(CStr(k)) & ": ["
        Dim arr As Variant: arr = levelMap(k)
        Dim i As Long, firstLn As Boolean: firstLn = True
        For i = LBound(arr) To UBound(arr)
            If Not firstLn Then out = out & ","
            firstLn = False
            out = out & q(EscJson(CStr(arr(i))))
        Next i
        out = out & "]"
    Next k
    out = out & vbCrLf & "        }"
    GetCanonicalByLevelJson = out
End Function

Private Sub AddLvlTpl(dict As Object, cid As String, level As String, line As String)
    If Not dict.Exists(cid) Then dict.Add cid, CreateObject("Scripting.Dictionary")
    Dim lvlMap As Object: Set lvlMap = dict(cid)
    If Not lvlMap.Exists(level) Then
        Dim emptyArr(0 To 0) As String
        emptyArr(0) = line
        lvlMap.Add level, emptyArr
    Else
        Dim curr As Variant: curr = lvlMap(level)
        Dim n As Long: n = UBound(curr) + 1
        ReDim Preserve curr(0 To n)
        curr(n) = line
        lvlMap(level) = curr
    End If
End Sub

'------------------------------------------------------------
' BuildOmegaGuaranteesBlock - scorecard resumido
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function BuildOmegaGuaranteesBlock() As String
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim score As Long: score = CLng(Brain_GetOmegaCompliance() * 10)
    If score < 0 Then score = 0
    If score > 10 Then score = 10

    Dim m3u8Path As String: m3u8Path = ConfigVal("Output_M3U8_Path")
    Dim m3u8text As String: m3u8text = ""
    If m3u8Path <> "" And Len(dir(m3u8Path)) > 0 Then
        m3u8text = ReadFileAll(m3u8Path)
        score = Brain_AuditTenGuarantees(m3u8text)
    End If

    Dim block As String
    block = "  " & q("omega_guarantees_scorecard") & ": {" & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("score") & ": " & score & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("max") & ": 10," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("compliance_pct") & ": " & Round(score / 10# * 100, 0) & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("G1_anti_freeze_nuclear") & ": " & IIf(InStr(m3u8text, "#EXT-X-APE-ANTI-FREEZE-NUCLEAR") > 0, "true", "false") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("G2_multi_source") & ": " & IIf(InStr(m3u8text, "#EXT-X-APE-MULTI-SOURCE") > 0, "true", "false") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("G3_buffer_nuclear") & ": " & IIf(InStr(m3u8text, "NUCLEAR_NO_COMPROMISE") > 0, "true", "false") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("G4_live_catchup") & ": " & IIf(InStr(m3u8text, "catchup=" & Chr(34) & "flussonic" & Chr(34)) > 0, "true", "false") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("G5_url_purity") & ": " & IIf(InStr(m3u8text, "??") = 0 And InStr(m3u8text, "&&") = 0, "true", "false") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("G6_max_abr_greedy") & ": " & IIf(InStr(m3u8text, "QMAX-STRATEGY") > 0, "true", "false") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("G7_max_imagen_hdr") & ": " & IIf(InStr(m3u8text, "X-HDR-MAX-CLL=5000") > 0, "true", "false") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("G8_http_error_tree") & ": " & IIf(InStr(m3u8text, "OTT Navigator") > 0, "true", "false") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("G9_multi_provider") & ": " & IIf(InStr(m3u8text, "#EXT-X-CONTENT-STEERING") > 0, "true", "false") & "," & vbCrLf
    block = block & "    " & q("G10_master_integrator") & ": " & IIf(InStr(m3u8text, "OMEGA_BUILD") > 0, "true", "false") & vbCrLf
    block = block & "  }"
    BuildOmegaGuaranteesBlock = block
End Function

'------------------------------------------------------------
' FindLatestLabCalibrated - busca el JSON mas reciente
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function FindLatestLabCalibrated() As String
    Dim dirPath As String: dirPath = "C:\Users\HFRC\Downloads\"
    Dim fn As String: fn = dir(dirPath & "LAB_CALIBRATED_*.json")
    Dim latest As String: latest = ""
    Dim latestDate As Date: latestDate = DateSerial(2000, 1, 1)
    Do While fn <> ""
        Dim fd As Date: fd = FileDateTime(dirPath & fn)
        If fd > latestDate Then
            latestDate = fd
            latest = dirPath & fn
        End If
        fn = dir
    Loop
    FindLatestLabCalibrated = latest
End Function

'------------------------------------------------------------
' Helpers
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function q(ByVal s As String) As String
    q = Chr(34) & s & Chr(34)
End Function



Public Function RemoveJsonFieldSimple(ByVal txt As String, ByVal fieldName As String) As String
    Dim needle As String: needle = Chr(34) & fieldName & Chr(34) & ":"
    Dim sIdx As Long: sIdx = InStr(txt, needle)
    If sIdx = 0 Then RemoveJsonFieldSimple = txt: Exit Function

    ' Buscar la coma que separa al field previo
    Dim precComma As Long: precComma = sIdx - 1
    Do While precComma > 0
        Dim ch As String: ch = Mid(txt, precComma, 1)
        If ch = "," Then Exit Do
        If ch = "{" Then precComma = sIdx: Exit Do
        precComma = precComma - 1
    Loop

    ' Buscar primera { despues del :
    Dim colon As Long: colon = InStr(sIdx, txt, ":")
    Dim openB As Long: openB = InStr(colon, txt, "{")
    If openB = 0 Then RemoveJsonFieldSimple = txt: Exit Function

    ' Balance braces
    Dim depth As Long: depth = 0
    Dim i As Long: i = openB
    Do While i <= Len(txt)
        Dim c As String: c = Mid(txt, i, 1)
        If c = "{" Then depth = depth + 1
        If c = "}" Then
            depth = depth - 1
            If depth = 0 Then Exit Do
        End If
        i = i + 1
    Loop
    Dim endPos As Long: endPos = i
    ' Quitar coma trailing si existe
    If endPos < Len(txt) Then
        If Mid(txt, endPos + 1, 1) = "," Then endPos = endPos + 1
    End If

    RemoveJsonFieldSimple = Left(txt, precComma - 1) & Mid(txt, endPos + 1)
End Function

Private Function CollectVerbatimLines(vV As Worksheet, cid As String) As String
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim lR As Long: lR = vV.UsedRange.rows.count
    Dim items As String: items = ""
    Dim first As Boolean: first = True
    Dim r As Long
    For r = 2 To lR
        If CStr(vV.Cells(r, 1).value) = cid Then
            Dim ln As String: ln = CStr(vV.Cells(r, 3).value)
            If ln <> "" Then
                If Not first Then items = items & ","
                first = False
                items = items & Chr(34) & EscJson(ln) & Chr(34)
            End If
        End If
    Next r
    On Error GoTo 0
    CollectVerbatimLines = "[" & items & "]"
End Function

Private Function BuildJsonArr(ByVal rawLines As String) As String
    Dim parts() As String: parts = Split(rawLines, "|||")
    Dim i As Long, out As String: out = ""
    For i = LBound(parts) To UBound(parts)
        If i > 0 Then out = out & ","
        out = out & Chr(34) & EscJson(parts(i)) & Chr(34)
    Next i
    BuildJsonArr = out
End Function

'============================================================
' Brain_RunOmegaScorecardV2
' Lanza el audit PowerShell externo (3 capas, scan ~3min).
'============================================================
Public Sub Brain_RunOmegaScorecardV2()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim sh As Object: Set sh = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
    Dim cmd As String
    Dim m3u8Path As String
    m3u8Path = CStr(ThisWorkbook.sheets("18_CONFIG").Cells(30, 3).Value2)
    If Len(m3u8Path) = 0 Then
        MsgBox "18_CONFIG!C30 (Output_M3U8_Path) esta vacio. Completalo antes de auditar.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If
    cmd = "powershell.exe -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File ""C:\tmp\scorecard_v2_full.ps1"" -m3u8 """ & m3u8Path & """"
    Dim resp As VbMsgBoxResult
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then
        resp = MsgBox( _
            "El audit escanea el M3U8 actual (path en 18_CONFIG!C30)." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            "Tiempo estimado: ~3 minutos (155 MB)" & vbCrLf & _
            "Se ejecuta en ventana de PowerShell visible." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            "Al finalizar veras la hoja 00_OMEGA_SCORECARD actualizada" & vbCrLf & _
            "con columna D de dropdowns (REPLICAR / IMPLEMENTAR / QUITAR)." & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
            "Procedo?", _
            vbYesNo + vbQuestion, "AUDITAR M3U8 - Scorecard v2")
        If resp = vbNo Then Exit Sub
    End If
    Application.StatusBar = "Lanzando audit en PowerShell..."
    sh.Run cmd, 1, True
    Application.StatusBar = False
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then MsgBox "Audit completo. Revisa hoja 00_OMEGA_SCORECARD.", vbInformation, "AUDITAR M3U8"
    Exit Sub
EH:
    Application.StatusBar = False
    MsgBox "ERROR: " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub

Private Function EscJson(ByVal s As String) As String
    s = Replace(s, "\", "\\")
    s = Replace(s, Chr(34), "\" & Chr(34))
    s = Replace(s, vbCrLf, "\n")
    s = Replace(s, vbCr, "\n")
    s = Replace(s, vbLf, "\n")
    s = Replace(s, vbTab, "\t")
    EscJson = s
End Function

'------------------------------------------------------------
' Wrapper de MsgBox. Las macros parcheadas llaman aqui cuando
' WIRING_SILENT=True. Siempre loggea, solo muestra si no silent.
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Sub WB_Msg(ByVal msg As String, _
                   Optional ByVal style As Long = vbInformation, _
                   Optional ByVal titleStr As String = "")
    Call WB_LogMsg("MSGBOX[" & titleStr & "] " & Left(msg, 300))
    If WIRING_SILENT Then Exit Sub
    If titleStr = "" Then
        MsgBox msg, style
    Else
        MsgBox msg, style, titleStr
    End If
End Sub

Public Sub WB_LogMsg(ByVal msg As String)
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Set ws = Nothing
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(LOG_SHEET)
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets.Add(After:=ThisWorkbook.sheets(ThisWorkbook.sheets.count))
        ws.name = LOG_SHEET
        ws.Range("A1").value = "Timestamp"
        ws.Range("B1").value = "Message"
        ws.Range("A1:B1").Font.Bold = True
    End If
    Dim r As Long
    r = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 1).End(xlUp).row + 1
    If r < 2 Then r = 2
    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss")
    ws.Cells(r, 2).value = msg
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

'------------------------------------------------------------
' CONTRATO DE CABLEADO: array 2D (sourceRef, targetRef, notes)
' Cada fila declara una dependencia de datos entre celdas.
' Ampliable: editar aqui cuando se aÃ±adan mas dependencias.
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function WB_Contract() As Variant
    Dim c(0 To 2, 0 To 2) As String
    c(0, 0) = "18_CONFIG!C31": c(0, 1) = "9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD!F13": c(0, 2) = "buffer_seconds -> dashboard nota buffer"
    c(1, 0) = "18_CONFIG!C32": c(1, 1) = "9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD!F16": c(1, 2) = "live_delay_seconds -> dashboard nota live"
    c(2, 0) = "18_CONFIG!C33": c(2, 1) = "9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD!F18": c(2, 2) = "reconnect_attempts -> dashboard nota retry"
    WB_Contract = c
End Function

'------------------------------------------------------------
' Propagacion de cambios: cuando una celda fuente cambia,
' escribe su valor en todos sus targets del contrato.
' Protegido contra recursion y ciclos.
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Sub WB_Propagate(ByVal sourceRef As String)
    If WIRING_BUSY Then Exit Sub
    WIRING_BUSY = True
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    On Error GoTo cleanup

    Dim srcVal As Variant
    srcVal = WB_ReadRef(sourceRef)

    Dim c As Variant, i As Long
    c = WB_Contract()
    For i = LBound(c, 1) To UBound(c, 1)
        If LCase(c(i, 0)) = LCase(sourceRef) Then
            Dim tgt As String
            tgt = c(i, 1)
            If WB_WouldCycle(sourceRef, tgt) Then
                Call WB_LogMsg("CYCLE: " & sourceRef & " -> " & tgt & " (skip)")
            Else
                Call WB_WriteRef(tgt, srcVal)
                Call WB_LogMsg("PROPAGATE: " & sourceRef & "=" & CStr(srcVal) & " -> " & tgt)
            End If
        End If
    Next i

cleanup:
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    WIRING_BUSY = False
End Sub

'------------------------------------------------------------
' Deteccion de ciclos por BFS sobre el grafo de dependencias.
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function WB_WouldCycle(ByVal src As String, ByVal tgt As String) As Boolean
    Dim visited As Collection
    Set visited = New Collection
    Dim queue As Collection
    Set queue = New Collection
    queue.Add tgt

    Dim c As Variant, i As Long
    c = WB_Contract()

    Do While queue.count > 0
        Dim cur As String
        cur = queue.Item(1)
        queue.Remove 1

        If LCase(cur) = LCase(src) Then
            WB_WouldCycle = True
            Exit Function
        End If

        On Error Resume Next
        visited.Add cur, LCase(cur)
        If Err.Number <> 0 Then
            Err.Clear
            GoTo skipnext
        End If
        On Error GoTo 0

        For i = LBound(c, 1) To UBound(c, 1)
            If LCase(c(i, 0)) = LCase(cur) Then queue.Add c(i, 1)
        Next i
skipnext:
    Loop
    WB_WouldCycle = False
End Function

'------------------------------------------------------------
' Helpers de lectura/escritura por referencia string "Hoja!Celda"
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Function WB_ReadRef(ByVal ref As String) As Variant
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim parts() As String
    parts = Split(ref, "!")
    If UBound(parts) < 1 Then
        WB_ReadRef = ""
        Exit Function
    End If
    WB_ReadRef = ThisWorkbook.sheets(parts(0)).Range(parts(1)).value
    On Error GoTo 0
End Function

Public Sub WB_WriteRef(ByVal ref As String, ByVal value As Variant)
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim parts() As String
    parts = Split(ref, "!")
    If UBound(parts) < 1 Then Exit Sub
    ThisWorkbook.sheets(parts(0)).Range(parts(1)).value = value
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

'------------------------------------------------------------
' ORQUESTADOR: ejecuta todas las macros Brain_* con WIRING_SILENT=True
' y escribe reporte en hoja 00_MACRO_RUN_REPORT.
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Sub APE_RunAllSafe()
    WIRING_SILENT = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False

    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Set ws = Nothing
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(REPORT_SHEET)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets.Add(After:=ThisWorkbook.sheets(ThisWorkbook.sheets.count))
        ws.name = REPORT_SHEET
    End If
    ws.Cells.Clear

    ws.Range("A1").value = "APE_RunAllSafe - Reporte de ejecucion"
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("A1").Font.size = 14
    ws.Range("A2").value = "Ejecutado: " & Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss")

    Dim hdrs As Variant
    hdrs = Array("#", "Macro", "Status", "Duracion (s)", "Error", "Notas")
    Dim cc As Long
    For cc = 0 To UBound(hdrs)
        ws.Cells(4, cc + 1).value = hdrs(cc)
        ws.Cells(4, cc + 1).Font.Bold = True
        ws.Cells(4, cc + 1).Interior.Color = RGB(31, 78, 121)
        ws.Cells(4, cc + 1).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    Next cc

    Dim macros As Variant
    macros = Array( _
        "Brain_RecalcAllSilent", _
        "Brain_DashboardOmegaRefresh", _
        "Brain_BuildMatrix_Omega_Silent", _
        "Brain_ColorMatrix_Omega", _
        "Brain_ValidateValues", _
        "Brain_DetectContradictions", _
        "Brain_SuggestOptimal", _
        "Brain_FindOrphans", _
        "Brain_DetectBottleneck", _
        "Brain_EnforceMatrixScope_Safe", _
        "Brain_SyncMatrixToPanel", _
        "Brain_AutoFillPerfect", _
        "Brain_AutoFillNivel1", _
        "Brain_AutoFillNivel3", _
        "Brain_BuildUnifiedValues", _
        "Brain_RunOmega", _
        "Brain_RefineToMax", _
        "Brain_RunAll5", _
        "Brain_OptimizerLoop", _
        "Brain_FindUniversalCompat", _
        "Brain_RunOmega_Ultimate" _
    )

    Dim r As Long: r = 5
    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(macros) To UBound(macros)
        Dim mname As String
        mname = CStr(macros(i))
        ws.Cells(r, 1).value = i + 1
        ws.Cells(r, 2).value = mname
        Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer
        Dim statusStr As String, errDesc As String
        statusStr = "": errDesc = ""
        On Error Resume Next
        Err.Clear
        Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!" & mname
        If Err.Number <> 0 Then
            statusStr = "ERROR"
            errDesc = Err.Number & ": " & Err.description
            ws.Cells(r, 3).Interior.Color = RGB(248, 105, 107)
        Else
            statusStr = "OK"
            ws.Cells(r, 3).Interior.Color = RGB(146, 208, 80)
        End If
        Err.Clear
        On Error GoTo 0
        ws.Cells(r, 3).value = statusStr
        ws.Cells(r, 4).value = Round(Timer - t0, 2)
        ws.Cells(r, 5).value = errDesc
        Call WB_LogMsg("RUN: " & mname & " -> " & statusStr & " (" & Round(Timer - t0, 2) & "s) " & errDesc)
        r = r + 1
        DoEvents
    Next i

    ws.Columns("A:F").AutoFit

    WIRING_SILENT = False
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
End Sub

'------------------------------------------------------------
' Instrumentacion del Solver: corre Brain_OptimizerLoop con log
' por iteracion en hoja 00_SOLVER_DIARY para ver su "sufrimiento".
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Sub APE_RunSolverVerbose()
    WIRING_SILENT = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False

    Dim ws As Worksheet
    Set ws = Nothing
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets(DIARY_SHEET)
    On Error GoTo 0
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets.Add(After:=ThisWorkbook.sheets(ThisWorkbook.sheets.count))
        ws.name = DIARY_SHEET
    End If
    ws.Cells.Clear
    ws.Range("A1").value = "APE_RunSolverVerbose - Diario del Solver"
    ws.Range("A1").Font.Bold = True: ws.Range("A1").Font.size = 14
    ws.Range("A2").value = "Inicio: " & Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss")

    Dim hdrs As Variant
    hdrs = Array("Iter", "Health", "Delta_Target", "Risk", "Headroom", "Stall", "Compat%", "TTFB", "EXTHTTP", "Accion", "Veredicto")
    Dim c As Long
    For c = 0 To UBound(hdrs)
        ws.Cells(4, c + 1).value = hdrs(c)
        ws.Cells(4, c + 1).Font.Bold = True
        ws.Cells(4, c + 1).Interior.Color = RGB(31, 78, 121)
        ws.Cells(4, c + 1).Font.Color = RGB(255, 255, 255)
    Next c

    ' Snapshot INICIAL
    Dim row As Long: row = 5
    Call APE_SnapshotSolver(ws, row, "inicial", "estado previo al Solver")
    row = row + 1

    ' Correr el Solver real
    On Error Resume Next
    Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_OptimizerLoop"
    Dim errMsg As String
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then errMsg = Err.Number & ": " & Err.description
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0

    ' Snapshot POST-Solver
    Call APE_SnapshotSolver(ws, row, "post-Solver", errMsg)
    row = row + 1

    ' Correr el Find-Universal-Compat evolutivo
    On Error Resume Next
    Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_FindUniversalCompat"
    errMsg = ""
    If Err.Number <> 0 Then errMsg = Err.Number & ": " & Err.description
    Err.Clear
    On Error GoTo 0
    Call APE_SnapshotSolver(ws, row, "post-FindUniversal", errMsg)
    row = row + 1

    ' Sufrimiento: targets no cumplidos
    Dim diag As String
    diag = APE_DiagUnmet()
    ws.Range("A" & (row + 1)).value = "TARGETS NO CUMPLIDOS (sufrimiento del Solver):"
    ws.Range("A" & (row + 1)).Font.Bold = True
    ws.Range("A" & (row + 2)).value = diag
    ws.Range("A" & (row + 2)).WrapText = True
    ws.Range("A" & (row + 2)).Interior.Color = RGB(255, 235, 156)

    ws.Columns("A:K").AutoFit
    ws.Range("A" & (row + 2)).ColumnWidth = 120

    WIRING_SILENT = False
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
End Sub

Private Sub APE_SnapshotSolver(ws As Worksheet, row As Long, label As String, note As String)
    On Error Resume Next
    ws.Cells(row, 1).value = label
    ws.Cells(row, 2).value = ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD").Range("B5").value
    ws.Cells(row, 3).value = 95 - CDbl(ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD").Range("B5").value)
    ws.Cells(row, 4).value = ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD").Range("B6").value
    ws.Cells(row, 5).value = ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD").Range("B7").value
    ws.Cells(row, 6).value = ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD").Range("B8").value
    ws.Cells(row, 7).value = ThisWorkbook.sheets("27_COMPAT_MATRIX").Range("J4").value
    ws.Cells(row, 8).value = ThisWorkbook.sheets("20_URL_PROBE").Range("B9").value
    ws.Cells(row, 9).value = ThisWorkbook.sheets("19_URL_BUILDER").Range("B20").value
    ws.Cells(row, 10).value = note
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Public Function APE_DiagUnmet() As String
    Dim s As String
    Dim health As Double, risk As Double, headroom As Double, stall As Double
    Dim compat As Double, ttfb As Double, exthttp As Double
    On Error Resume Next
    health = CDbl(ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD").Range("B5").value)
    risk = CDbl(ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD").Range("B6").value)
    headroom = CDbl(ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD").Range("B7").value)
    stall = CDbl(ThisWorkbook.sheets("9_BRAIN_DASHBOARD").Range("B8").value)
    compat = CDbl(ThisWorkbook.sheets("27_COMPAT_MATRIX").Range("J4").value)
    ttfb = CDbl(ThisWorkbook.sheets("20_URL_PROBE").Range("B9").value)
    exthttp = CDbl(ThisWorkbook.sheets("19_URL_BUILDER").Range("B20").value)
    On Error GoTo 0

    If health < 95 Then s = s & "- Health=" & health & " / target>=95 -> FALTA " & (95 - health) & " pts. Ajuste sugerido: subir buffer_seconds a 75, nudgeMaxRetry a 12." & vbCrLf
    If risk > 10 Then s = s & "- Risk=" & risk & " / target<=10 -> FALTA BAJAR " & (risk - 10) & " pts. Ajuste: reemplazar Connection=close, quitar UA APE-NAVIGATOR, Referer netflix." & vbCrLf
    If headroom < 300 Then s = s & "- Headroom=" & headroom & " / target>=300 -> FALTA " & (300 - headroom) & ". Ajuste: bajar profile (P3->P2) o bajar abrBandWidthFactor." & vbCrLf
    If stall > 0.67 Then s = s & "- Stall=" & stall & " / target<0.67 -> BAJAR " & (stall - 0.67) & ". Ajuste: subir buffer_seconds y fragLoadPolicy_maxNumRetry." & vbCrLf
    If compat < 100 Then s = s & "- Compat=" & compat & " / target=100 -> FALTA " & (100 - compat) & "pp. Correr Brain_FindUniversalCompat." & vbCrLf
    If ttfb > 500 Then s = s & "- TTFB=" & ttfb & "ms / target<500 -> FUERA DEL CONTROL DEL SOLVER (red real). Cambiar servidor o CDN." & vbCrLf
    If exthttp > 8000 Then s = s & "- EXTHTTP=" & exthttp & " / target<8000 -> BAJAR. Eliminar headers redundantes." & vbCrLf
    If s = "" Then s = "OK: todos los targets cumplidos."
    APE_DiagUnmet = s
End Function

'============================================================
' PER-PROFILE AUTO ORCHESTRATOR (Nivel C - added 2026-04-18)
' Itera P5->P0 llamando Brain_OmegaOptimizer_PerProfile para
' cada perfil, persiste knobs en 6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES, luego
' ejecuta Cross-Profile Monotonic Solver.
'============================================================

Public Sub Brain_Step02alt_OmegaOptimizerAuto()
    Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer
    On Error GoTo EH
    WIRING_SILENT = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationManual
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.DisplayStatusBar = True

    Call WB_LogMsg("META-AUTO: init. ALL_PROFILES loop (P5->P0)")

    ' Diary inicial
    Dim diary As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set diary = ThisWorkbook.sheets("00_SOLVER_DIARY")
    On Error GoTo 0

    Dim origDefault As String: origDefault = ConfigVal("Default_Profile")

    ' Loop P5->P0: primero los perfiles "fáciles" actúan de piso
    Dim profiles As Variant: profiles = Array("P5", "P4", "P3", "P2", "P1", "P0")
    Dim i As Long
    Dim fitSum As Double, fitCount As Long
    Dim worstFit As Double: worstFit = 999#
    Dim bestFit As Double: bestFit = 0#

    For i = 0 To UBound(profiles)
        Dim pid As String: pid = CStr(profiles(i))
        Application.StatusBar = "AUTO [" & (i + 1) & "/6] Calibrando " & pid & "..."
        DoEvents

        Dim fitP As Double
        fitP = Brain_OmegaOptimizer_PerProfile(pid)
        If fitP > 0 Then
            fitSum = fitSum + fitP
            fitCount = fitCount + 1
            If fitP < worstFit Then worstFit = fitP
            If fitP > bestFit Then bestFit = fitP
        End If
        Call WB_LogMsg("AUTO-" & pid & ": fit=" & Format(fitP, "0.0000") & " time-total=" & Format(Timer - t0, "0.0") & "s")
        DoEvents

        ' Checkpoint save cada perfil (crash recovery)
        On Error Resume Next
        ThisWorkbook.Save
        On Error GoTo 0
    Next i

    ' Constraint solver: monotonia inter-perfil
    Application.StatusBar = "AUTO: Cross-Profile Monotonic Solver..."
    Call Brain_CrossProfileMonotonicSolver

    ' Restore Default_Profile y mark AUTO
    Call SetConfigVal("Default_Profile", "ALL_PROFILES")

    ' Meta_Optimizer flags en 18_CONFIG
    Dim wC As Worksheet: Set wC = ThisWorkbook.sheets("18_CONFIG")
    Dim avgFit As Double: avgFit = IIf(fitCount > 0, fitSum / fitCount, 0#)
    Call PP_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Last_Run", Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss"))
    Call PP_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Last_Fit", Format(avgFit, "0.000000"))
    Call PP_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Last_Source", "ALL_PROFILES_AUTO")
    Call PP_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Mode", "AUTO-PER-PROFILE")
    Call PP_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Best_Fit", Format(bestFit, "0.000000"))
    Call PP_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Worst_Fit", Format(worstFit, "0.000000"))
    Call PP_UpsertConfig(wC, "Meta_Optimizer_Profiles_Calibrated", fitCount & " of 6")

    On Error Resume Next
    Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!MarkStep", "02a", True

    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.StatusBar = "AUTO DONE: " & fitCount & "/6 calibrated  best=" & Format(bestFit, "0.000") & " worst=" & Format(worstFit, "0.000") & "  time=" & Format(Timer - t0, "0.0") & "s"
    WIRING_SILENT = False
    ThisWorkbook.Save
    Exit Sub

EH:
    Application.Calculation = xlCalculationAutomatic
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.StatusBar = "AUTO ERROR: " & Err.description
    WIRING_SILENT = False
    Call WB_LogMsg("AUTO ERROR: #" & Err.Number & " - " & Err.description)
End Sub

'------------------------------------------------------------
' Cross-Profile Monotonic Solver
' Garantiza: P0.buffer >= P1.buffer >= ... >= P5.buffer
'            P0.reconnect >= P1.reconnect >= ... >= P5.reconnect
'            (mayor exigencia = mayor margen)
'------------------------------------------------------------
Public Sub Brain_CrossProfileMonotonicSolver()
    On Error Resume Next
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES")
    If ws Is Nothing Then Exit Sub

    ' Knobs que requieren monotonia descendente P0>=P1>=...>=P5
    ' Fila en 6_NIVEL_2_PROFILES: 607=buffer, 608=live_delay, 609=reconnect, 610=fragRetry, 611=nudgeRetry
    Dim rowsMono As Variant: rowsMono = Array(607, 608, 609, 610, 611)
    ' Knobs que requieren monotonia ascendente P5<=P4<=...<=P0: abrBWFactor (612), liveSync (613)
    Dim rowsAsc As Variant: rowsAsc = Array(612, 613)

    Dim violations As Long: violations = 0

    ' Monotonia descendente: P0 (col 2) >= P1 (col 3) >= ... >= P5 (col 7)
    Dim k As Variant
    For Each k In rowsMono
        Dim r As Long: r = CLng(k)
        ' Ensure col(c) >= col(c+1) for c=2..6
        Dim c As Long
        For c = 2 To 6
            Dim vL As Double: vL = val(ws.Cells(r, c).Value2)
            Dim vR As Double: vR = val(ws.Cells(r, c + 1).Value2)
            If vL < vR And vR > 0 Then
                ws.Cells(r, c).Value2 = CLng(vR)
                violations = violations + 1
                Call WB_LogMsg("Monotonic fix R" & r & " col " & c & ": " & vL & " -> " & vR & " (from col " & (c + 1) & ")")
            End If
        Next c
    Next k

    ' Monotonia ascendente al reves: col 2 (P0) >= col 3 (P1) >= ... >= col 7 (P5)
    ' Actually abrBWFactor y liveSync: MAS EXIGENTE (P0) = MAS AGRESIVO (factor mas alto)
    ' Entonces P0 >= P1 >= ... >= P5 tambien para estos
    For Each k In rowsAsc
        Dim r2 As Long: r2 = CLng(k)
        Dim c2 As Long
        For c2 = 2 To 6
            Dim w1 As Double: w1 = val(ws.Cells(r2, c2).Value2)
            Dim w2 As Double: w2 = val(ws.Cells(r2, c2 + 1).Value2)
            If w1 < w2 And w2 > 0 Then
                ws.Cells(r2, c2).Value2 = Round(w2, 4)
                violations = violations + 1
            End If
        Next c2
    Next k

    Call WB_LogMsg("Cross-Profile Monotonic Solver: " & violations & " violations fixed")
End Sub

'------------------------------------------------------------
Private Sub PP_UpsertConfig(ws As Worksheet, ByVal keyName As String, ByVal valStr As String)
    Dim lR As Long: lR = ws.Cells(ws.rows.count, 2).End(xlUp).row
    Dim r As Long, foundR As Long: foundR = 0
    For r = 1 To lR
        If CStr(ws.Cells(r, 2).Value2) = keyName Then foundR = r: Exit For
    Next r
    If foundR = 0 Then
        foundR = lR + 1
        ws.Cells(foundR, 1).Value2 = "X"
        ws.Cells(foundR, 2).Value2 = keyName
        ws.Cells(foundR, 4).Value2 = "(auto)"
    End If
    ws.Cells(foundR, 3).Value2 = valStr
End Sub

Public Sub Brain_Step05b_ExportBulletproof()
    ' DESLIGADO 2026-04-20: ya no corre PS ni escribe JSON. Solo flag en 00_EXPORT_STAGE.
    ' El export bulletproof real ocurre dentro de Brain_Step11_ExportFinal.
    On Error Resume Next
    Call StageBulletproofFlag(True)
    Call MarkStep("05b", True)
    If Not WIRING_SILENT Then _
        MsgBox "Bulletproof enriquecimiento FLAGGED para el proximo export." & vbCrLf & _
               "Corre el PASO 11 para generar LAB_CALIBRATED_BULLETPROOF_*.json.", _
               vbInformation, "Paso 05b - Flag"
End Sub



'============================================================
' PROGRESSIVE AUDIT - panel flotante VBA + PS background (2026-04-18)
' Sustituye la pantalla negra PowerShell por UserForm modeless
' con barra de progreso moderna actualizando en tiempo real.
'============================================================


'============================================================
' PROGRESSIVE AUDIT - panel flotante VBA + PS background (2026-04-18)
' Sustituye la pantalla negra PowerShell por UserForm modeless
' con barra de progreso moderna actualizando en tiempo real.
'============================================================




Public Function Brain_Test_frmProgress() As String
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim result As String
    result = "start"
    ' Test 1: ensure form class exists
    Dim f As Object
    Set f = frmProgress
    result = result & "|form_ref_ok"
    ' Test 2: controls access
    Dim c As String: c = f.lblTitle.Caption
    result = result & "|lblTitle=" & c
    ' Test 3: set caption
    f.lblStatus.Caption = "test_ok"
    result = result & "|lblStatus_set_ok"
    ' Test 4: show modeless
    f.Show 0
    result = result & "|show_ok"
    ' Unload immediately
    Unload f
    result = result & "|unload_ok"
    Brain_Test_frmProgress = result
    Exit Function
EH:
    Brain_Test_frmProgress = result & "|ERR#" & Err.Number & ":" & Err.description
End Function

'============================================================
' PROGRESSIVE AUDIT - panel flotante VBA + PS background (2026-04-19)
' Fix correcto: bytesRead real (no estimate * 870), UTF8 sin BOM, PID capture+kill.
'============================================================

Public Sub Brain_Step03_AuditM3U8_Progressive()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Dim t0 As Double: t0 = Timer
    Dim psPid As Long: psPid = 0

    ' 1) Path del M3U8
    Dim m3u8Path As String
    m3u8Path = CStr(ThisWorkbook.sheets("18_CONFIG").Cells(30, 3).Value2)
    If Len(m3u8Path) = 0 Then
        MsgBox "18_CONFIG!C30 esta vacio.", vbExclamation
        Exit Sub
    End If

    ' 2) Limpiar progress + pid files previos
    Const PROGRESS_FILE As String = "C:\tmp\scorecard_progress.txt"
    Const PID_FILE As String = "C:\tmp\scorecard_pid.txt"
    Dim fso As Object: Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
    If fso.FileExists(PROGRESS_FILE) Then fso.DeleteFile PROGRESS_FILE
    If fso.FileExists(PID_FILE) Then fso.DeleteFile PID_FILE

    ' 3) Mostrar UserForm modeless
    frmProgress.Show vbModeless
    frmProgress.lblFilename.Caption = "Archivo: " & m3u8Path
    frmProgress.lblStatus.Caption = "Iniciando PowerShell..."
    frmProgress.Repaint
    DoEvents

    ' 4) Launch PS OCULTO (0 = hidden window, False = dont wait)
    Dim sh As Object: Set sh = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
    Dim cmd As String
    cmd = "powershell.exe -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -WindowStyle Hidden -File ""C:\tmp\scorecard_v2_full.ps1"" -m3u8 """ & m3u8Path & """"
    sh.Run cmd, 0, False
    Call WB_LogMsg("AuditProgressive: PS launched hidden")

    ' 4b) Esperar a que PS escriba scorecard_pid.txt (max 5s) para poder matarlo si algo sale mal
    Dim pidWaitUntil As Double: pidWaitUntil = Timer + 5
    Do While Timer < pidWaitUntil
        DoEvents
        If fso.FileExists(PID_FILE) Then
            On Error Resume Next
            Dim tsp As Object: Set tsp = fso.OpenTextFile(PID_FILE, 1, False)
            Dim pidStr As String: pidStr = Trim(tsp.ReadAll)
            tsp.Close
            On Error GoTo EH
            If Len(pidStr) > 0 And isNumeric(pidStr) Then
                psPid = CLng(pidStr)
                Call WB_LogMsg("AuditProgressive: PS PID capturado = " & psPid)
                Exit Do
            End If
        End If
        Dim w1 As Double: w1 = Timer + 0.2
        Do While Timer < w1: DoEvents: Loop
    Loop
    If psPid = 0 Then Call WB_LogMsg("AuditProgressive: WARN no capture PID en 5s, sigo sin kill-safety")

    ' 5) Polling loop - lee el progress file y actualiza el form
    Dim doneFlag As Boolean: doneFlag = False
    Dim timeoutS As Double: timeoutS = 1800   ' 30 min safety (files gigantes 800MB+ necesitan margen)

    Do While Not doneFlag
        DoEvents

        ' === Cancel check ===
        If frmProgress.bUserCancelled Then
            frmProgress.lblStatus.Caption = "CANCELANDO... matando PS"
            frmProgress.Repaint
            Call CleanupAuditAfterCancel(psPid, PROGRESS_FILE, PID_FILE)
            Exit Do
        End If

        ' Sleep 400ms
        Dim waitUntil As Double: waitUntil = Timer + 0.4
        Do While Timer < waitUntil: DoEvents: Loop

        ' Safety timeout -> matar PS y salir
        If (Timer - t0) > timeoutS Then
            frmProgress.lblStatus.Caption = "TIMEOUT (" & timeoutS & "s) - matando PS..."
            frmProgress.Repaint
            Call CleanupAuditAfterCancel(psPid, PROGRESS_FILE, PID_FILE)
            Exit Do
        End If

        ' Read progress file
        If Not fso.FileExists(PROGRESS_FILE) Then
            frmProgress.lblStatus.Caption = "Esperando PS... (" & Format(Timer - t0, "0.0") & "s)"
            frmProgress.Repaint
            GoTo nextIter
        End If

        Dim content As String
        On Error Resume Next
        Dim ts As Object: Set ts = fso.OpenTextFile(PROGRESS_FILE, 1, False)
        content = ts.ReadAll
        ts.Close
        On Error GoTo EH

        ' Parse "phase|lines|totalBytes|elapsed|message|bytesRead"
        ' bytesRead es el 6to campo (BaseStream.Position real del PS) - sin multiplicaciones, sin Overflow.
        Dim parts() As String: parts = Split(Trim(content), "|")
        If UBound(parts) >= 4 Then
            Dim phase As String: phase = parts(0)
            Dim curLines As Long: curLines = CLng(parts(1))
            Dim totalBytes As Double: totalBytes = CDbl(parts(2))
            Dim elapsed As Double: elapsed = CDbl(parts(3))
            Dim msg As String: msg = parts(4)

            ' bytesProcessed: usar campo 6 real; fallback si PS antiguo no lo emite (CDbl evita Overflow Long)
            Dim bytesProcessed As Double
            If UBound(parts) >= 5 Then
                bytesProcessed = CDbl(parts(5))
            Else
                bytesProcessed = CDbl(curLines) * 185#
            End If
            Dim pct As Double
            If totalBytes > 0 Then
                pct = (bytesProcessed / totalBytes) * 100
                If pct > 100 Then pct = 100
            Else
                pct = 0
            End If

            frmProgress.lblStatus.Caption = "Fase: " & UCase(phase) & " - " & msg
            frmProgress.lblLines.Caption = "Lineas: " & Format(curLines, "#,##0")
            frmProgress.lblElapsed.Caption = "Tiempo: " & Format(elapsed, "0.0") & "s"
            frmProgress.lblPercent.Caption = Format(pct, "0.0") & "%"
            ' Animar progress bar
            Dim maxW As Double: maxW = frmProgress.barBase.width
            frmProgress.barFill.width = maxW * (pct / 100)
            frmProgress.Repaint

            If phase = "done" Then
                doneFlag = True
                frmProgress.lblStatus.Caption = "COMPLETADO - " & msg
                frmProgress.lblPercent.Caption = "100.0%"
                frmProgress.barFill.width = maxW
                frmProgress.Repaint
            End If
        End If
nextIter:
    Loop

    ' 6) Esperar 2s y cerrar form
    Dim closeWait As Double: closeWait = Timer + 2
    Do While Timer < closeWait: DoEvents: Loop
    Unload frmProgress

    ' 7) Mark step
    Call MarkStep("03", True)
    Call WB_LogMsg("AuditProgressive: DONE en " & Format(Timer - t0, "0.0") & "s")
    Exit Sub

EH:
    On Error Resume Next
    ' Matar PS huerfana para no dejar zombies (root cause del problema 2026-04-19)
    Call KillPsPid(psPid)
    If Not frmProgress Is Nothing Then Unload frmProgress
    On Error GoTo 0
    MsgBox "Error AuditProgressive: " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub

' Mata la PS scorecard si aun esta viva. Silencioso.
Private Sub KillPsPid(ByVal psPid As Long)
    On Error Resume Next
    If psPid <= 0 Then Exit Sub
    Dim sh As Object: Set sh = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
    sh.Run "taskkill /F /PID " & psPid, 0, True
    Call WB_LogMsg("AuditProgressive: taskkill PID " & psPid)
End Sub


'============================================================
' STAGING SHEET + STEP 11 EXPORT (added 2026-04-20)
' Arquitectura: todos los pasos anteriores solo CALCULAN (sin I/O).
' El unico exportador es Brain_Step11_ExportFinal que lee el estado
' de 36_BRAIN_SELECTOR (OUT colors) + la hoja 00_EXPORT_STAGE y
' produce LAB_CALIBRATED_<ts>.json con todo lo enriquecido.
'============================================================

Private Function EnsureStageSheet() As Worksheet
    Dim ws As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets("00_EXPORT_STAGE")
    On Error GoTo 0
    If ws Is Nothing Then
        Set ws = ThisWorkbook.sheets.Add(After:=ThisWorkbook.sheets(ThisWorkbook.sheets.count))
        ws.name = "00_EXPORT_STAGE"
        ws.Range("A1").value = "Clave"
        ws.Range("B1").value = "Valor"
        ws.Range("C1").value = "Timestamp"
        ws.Range("A1:C1").Font.Bold = True
        On Error Resume Next
        ws.Visible = xlSheetHidden
        On Error GoTo 0
    End If
    Set EnsureStageSheet = ws
End Function

Private Sub StageSet(ByVal key As String, ByVal val As Variant)
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureStageSheet()
    Dim r As Long: r = 2
    Do While Trim(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value)) <> "" And ws.Cells(r, 1).value <> key
        r = r + 1
    Loop
    ws.Cells(r, 1).value = key
    ws.Cells(r, 2).value = val
    ws.Cells(r, 3).value = Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-dd hh:mm:ss")
End Sub

Private Function StageGet(ByVal key As String) As Variant
    Dim ws As Worksheet: Set ws = EnsureStageSheet()
    Dim r As Long: r = 2
    Do While Trim(CStr(ws.Cells(r, 1).value)) <> ""
        If ws.Cells(r, 1).value = key Then
            StageGet = ws.Cells(r, 2).value
            Exit Function
        End If
        r = r + 1
    Loop
    StageGet = Empty
End Function

Public Sub StageGapPlan()
    ' Validacion de col E: cuenta items con seleccion valida
    Dim sc As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set sc = ThisWorkbook.sheets("00_OMEGA_SCORECARD")
    On Error GoTo 0
    If sc Is Nothing Then
        Call StageSet("gap_plan_ready", False)
        Call StageSet("gap_plan_items", 0)
        Exit Sub
    End If
    Dim lastRow As Long: lastRow = sc.UsedRange.rows.count
    Dim nRep As Long, nImp As Long, nQuit As Long, r As Long
    For r = 7 To lastRow
        Dim v As String: v = Trim(CStr(sc.Cells(r, 5).value))
        Select Case v
            Case "REPLICAR":    nRep = nRep + 1
            Case "IMPLEMENTAR": nImp = nImp + 1
            Case "QUITAR":      nQuit = nQuit + 1
        End Select
    Next r
    Call StageSet("gap_plan_ready", True)
    Call StageSet("gap_plan_replicar", nRep)
    Call StageSet("gap_plan_implementar", nImp)
    Call StageSet("gap_plan_quitar", nQuit)
    Call StageSet("gap_plan_items", nRep + nImp + nQuit)
End Sub

Public Sub StageBulletproofFlag(ByVal enabled As Boolean)
    Call StageSet("bulletproof_requested", enabled)
End Sub

' ---- Helper: escribir linea en el log vivo T67 (DESCRIPCION del step 11) ----
Private Sub Log11(ByRef logBuffer As String, ByVal line As String)
    logBuffer = logBuffer & Format(Now, "hh:mm:ss") & "  " & line & vbCrLf
    Dim panel As Worksheet
    On Error Resume Next
    Set panel = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR")
    If Not panel Is Nothing Then
        panel.Cells(67, 20).value = logBuffer   ' col T = 20
    End If
    On Error GoTo 0
    DoEvents
End Sub

'============================================================
' Brain_Step11_ExportFinal - EXPORTADOR EXCLUSIVO
' Lee que pasos estan DONE y construye LAB_CALIBRATED_<ts>.json
' con todas las enriquezas aplicables.
'============================================================
Public Sub Brain_Step11_ExportFinal()
    On Error GoTo EH
    Application.ScreenUpdating = False
    Application.EnableEvents = False
    Application.DisplayAlerts = False
    WIRING_SILENT = True

    Dim logB As String
    Dim panel As Worksheet: Set panel = ThisWorkbook.sheets("36_BRAIN_SELECTOR")
    ' Limpiar log anterior
    On Error Resume Next
    panel.Cells(67, 20).value = ""
    On Error GoTo EH

    Call Log11(logB, "=== STEP 11 EXPORT FINAL ===")

    ' 1. Inventario de pasos DONE
    Dim ids As Variant: ids = Array("01", "02", "02a", "03", "04", "05", "05b", "06", "07", "08", "09", "10")
    Dim corridos As String: corridos = ""
    Dim pendientes As String: pendientes = ""
    Dim i As Long
    For i = LBound(ids) To UBound(ids)
        Dim id As String: id = CStr(ids(i))
        If IsStepDone(id) Then
            corridos = corridos & id & ", "
        Else
            pendientes = pendientes & id & ", "
        End If
    Next i
    If Len(corridos) > 2 Then corridos = Left(corridos, Len(corridos) - 2)
    If Len(pendientes) > 2 Then pendientes = Left(pendientes, Len(pendientes) - 2)
    Call Log11(logB, "Corridos  : " & corridos)
    Call Log11(logB, "Pendientes: " & pendientes)

    ' 2. Base export (siempre)
    Call Log11(logB, "Enriqueciendo con BASE (Brain_ExportToFrontend)...")
    Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_ExportToFrontend"
    Dim latest As String: latest = FindLatestLabCalibrated()
    If latest = "" Then
        Call Log11(logB, "ERROR: no se encontro LAB_CALIBRATED_*.json generado.")
        GoTo Done
    End If
    Call Log11(logB, "  OK -> " & latest)

    ' 3. GAP PLAN (si step 05 DONE)
    If IsStepDone("05") Then
        Call Log11(logB, "Enriqueciendo con GAP PLAN (col E scorecard)...")
        Dim txt As String: txt = ReadFileAll(latest)
        If InStr(txt, Chr(34) & "omega_gap_plan" & Chr(34)) > 0 Then
            txt = RemoveJsonFieldSimple(txt, "omega_gap_plan")
        End If
        Dim gapBlock As String: gapBlock = BuildGapPlanJsonBlock(txt)
        If gapBlock <> "" Then
            Dim idx As Long: idx = InStrRev(txt, "}")
            If idx > 0 Then
                Dim newTxt As String
                newTxt = Left(txt, idx - 1) & "," & vbCrLf & gapBlock & vbCrLf & "}" & vbCrLf
                Dim fno As Integer: fno = FreeFile
                Open latest For Output As #fno
                Print #fno, newTxt;
                Close #fno
                Call Log11(logB, "  OK gap_plan embebido (" & CStr(StageGet("gap_plan_items")) & " items: " & _
                    CStr(StageGet("gap_plan_replicar")) & " REP + " & _
                    CStr(StageGet("gap_plan_implementar")) & " IMP + " & _
                    CStr(StageGet("gap_plan_quitar")) & " QUIT)")
            End If
        Else
            Call Log11(logB, "  SKIP gap_plan (no hay selecciones en col E)")
        End If
    Else
        Call Log11(logB, "SKIP GAP PLAN (step 05 no DONE)")
    End If

    ' 4. BULLETPROOF — AUTO-aplicado SIEMPRE (2026-04-26 doctrina:
    ' TODO output JSON es bulletproof por defecto, sin condicional).
    ' phase4_bulletproof_json.ps1 enriquece el JSON base + gap_plan ya escritos
    ' con lab_schema_variant + bulletproof + meta_per_profile + per-profile bulletproof
    ' (player_enslavement, actor_injections, optimized_knobs, bounds, role, fitness).
    ' Output canónico: LAB_CALIBRATED_BULLETPROOF_*.json en Downloads.
    Call Log11(logB, "Enriqueciendo con BULLETPROOF (PS phase4) [SIEMPRE on]...")
    Dim shBP As Object: Set shBP = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
    shBP.Run "powershell.exe -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File ""C:\tmp\phase4_bulletproof_json.ps1""", 1, True
    Call Log11(logB, "  OK bulletproof JSON generado en Downloads\LAB_CALIBRATED_BULLETPROOF_*.json")
    Call Log11(logB, "Enriqueciendo con PRISMA LAB-SYNC v2.0 (Stage 1.5)...")
    Application.Run "'" & ThisWorkbook.name & "'!Brain_PrismaEnrichBulletproof"
    Call Log11(logB, "  OK PRISMA section embedded in bulletproof JSON")



    ' 5. Marcar step 11 DONE
    Call MarkStep("11", True)
    Call Log11(logB, "=== EXPORT COMPLETADO ===")
    Call Log11(logB, "Archivo base: " & latest)

Done:
    Application.StatusBar = False
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
    WIRING_SILENT = False
    Exit Sub

EH:
    Application.ScreenUpdating = True
    Application.EnableEvents = True
    Application.DisplayAlerts = True
    WIRING_SILENT = False
    MsgBox "ERROR step11: " & Err.description, vbCritical
End Sub

VBA MACRO frmProgress.frm 
in file: xl/vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/frmProgress'
- - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - - 
Option Explicit

' Flag global del form: True = usuario solicito cancelar audit
Public bUserCancelled As Boolean

' Referencia al boton creado en runtime (necesario para handler)
Private WithEvents btnCancelDyn As MSForms.CommandButton
Attribute btnCancelDyn.VB_VarHelpID = -1

Private Sub UserForm_Initialize()
    bUserCancelled = False

    ' Anadir boton CANCELAR al runtime
    On Error Resume Next
    Set btnCancelDyn = Me.Controls.Add("Forms.CommandButton.1", "btnCancelDyn", True)
    With btnCancelDyn
        .Caption = "X CANCELAR"
        .Top = Me.height - 50
        .Left = Me.width - 130
        .width = 110
        .height = 32
        .BackColor = RGB(220, 38, 38)   ' rojo
        .ForeColor = RGB(255, 255, 255)  ' blanco
        .Font.Bold = True
        .Font.size = 10
        .TakeFocusOnClick = True
    End With
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Sub btnCancelDyn_Click()
    ' Boton CANCELAR pulsado: setear flag, main loop detecta en proximo DoEvents
    bUserCancelled = True
    On Error Resume Next
    btnCancelDyn.enabled = False
    btnCancelDyn.Caption = "Cancelando..."
    btnCancelDyn.BackColor = RGB(120, 120, 120)
    Me.lblStatus.Caption = "CANCELANDO... matando PS y liberando memoria"
    Me.Repaint
    On Error GoTo 0
End Sub

Private Sub UserForm_QueryClose(Cancel As Integer, CloseMode As Integer)
    ' Si user hace click en X (CloseMode=vbFormControlMenu=0)
    ' setear flag y NO cerrar (deja main loop hacer cleanup ordenado)
    If CloseMode = 0 Then
        bUserCancelled = True
        Cancel = True
        On Error Resume Next
        Me.lblStatus.Caption = "CANCELANDO... liberando memoria"
        If Not btnCancelDyn Is Nothing Then
            btnCancelDyn.enabled = False
            btnCancelDyn.Caption = "Cancelando..."
        End If
        Me.Repaint
        On Error GoTo 0
    End If
End Sub

VBA MACRO APE_AUDIT_FIXES.bas 
in file: xl/vbaProject.bin - OLE stream: 'VBA/APE_AUDIT_FIXES'
